# SAMPLE_GEN_V3 changes and cross-WP asks (append-only)

## WP-01 decisions

### WP-01 A1 (environment and settings), 2026-11-03

- **tree-sitter-language-pack is pinned to 0.13.0**, the last release that ships its grammars inside the wheel (so `uv.lock` hash-pins them). 1.x downloads grammars from GitHub at first use: verified, `get_parser("python")` raises `DownloadError` with no network. That breaks offline containers and the "pinned grammars" rule in §7.3. `tree-sitter==0.26.0` was checked against all ten grammars in §7.3/§7.9 (python, javascript, typescript, tsx, go, java, html, css, vue, svelte). `zstandard==0.25.0`. `factory-boy==3.3.3` was already in the dev group.
- **tiktoken is the optional extra `tokenizer`** (`tiktoken==0.14.0`). Neither the Dockerfiles nor `.wt-test.sh --init-venv` install extras, so `budget/estimate.py` (WP-11) must take the `ceil(chars / 3.6)` fallback when the import fails. tiktoken also downloads `o200k_base` at first use unless `TIKTOKEN_CACHE_DIR` is seeded beforehand (WP-11).
- **Both Django Dockerfiles are unchanged.** They install with `uv sync` from `uv.lock` and list no packages themselves. The new wheels are manylinux2014, and the grammars ship in the wheel, so no extra build step is needed.
- **`config/settings/components/generation.py` has no `__all__`.** A dynamic `__all__` hides the star-imported names from mypy and django-stubs (checked: `'Settings' object has no attribute 'EXECUTOR_TICK_SECONDS'`). The environment reader is the private `_env`, so the star import does not rebind `base.env`. Set env-backed values in your block with `_env.str(...)` and its siblings.
- **Blocks:** `WP-01`, `WP-02a`, `WP-03`, `WP-05` … `WP-13`. A block runs from its `# --- WP-xx ---` marker to the next marker, and `# --- end of WP blocks ---` closes the last one. WP-03 (for `GENERATION_SEED_ON_MIGRATE`, §11.1) and WP-10 were added beyond the requested list. The WP-07 block is not empty: it holds `AGENT_{OPENCODE,MINI_SWE_AGENT,AIDER}_VERSION`, because §12.1 gives WP-07 the `AGENT_*_VERSION` block.
- **`GENERATION_BLOBS_ROOT` / `GENERATION_RUNS_ROOT` are `MediaPath` objects** (`os.PathLike`) that resolve `settings.MEDIA_ROOT` on every use, so the per-test MEDIA_ROOT override in `backend/conftest.py` applies. An absolute path in the env var of the same name pins one of them (as a `str`). Callers use `Path(settings.X)`, `os.fspath()` or `str()`. Never concatenate the raw value or JSON-serialize it.
- **Machine limits use the §2.2 names:** `AGENT_SANDBOX_MEM_LIMIT_MAX` (default `3g`) and `AGENT_SANDBOX_NANO_CPUS_MAX` (default `2_000_000_000`). The defaults equal v2's per-run values. On the default 4g apps-dind, the `generous` policy (4g / 4e9) therefore exceeds the machine cap, and policy lint must say so (WP-11). `AGENT_MAX_CONCURRENT_RUNS` is unchanged.
- **The seven settings listed in §2.2 are removed.** "Only the machine limits stay" also covers `AGENT_DEFAULT_HARNESS`, `AGENT_AUTO_EVALUATE` and `AGENT_NETWORK_POLICY`, which v2 code still reads. They sit in the `WP-02a` block as v2 compatibility, together with the aliases `AGENT_SANDBOX_MEM_LIMIT` / `AGENT_SANDBOX_NANO_CPUS` (= the `_MAX` values). WP-02a deletes each one together with its last reader.
- **Executor settings** (`EXECUTOR_*`, `EVAL_MAX_REQUEUES`, `APPS_DIND_MEM_LIMIT`, `APPS_DIND_APP_RESERVE`, `EVAL_SLOT_MEM`, `ANALYSIS_WORKSPACE_MEM`, `SCHEDULE_MIN_INTERVAL_S`) moved verbatim into the WP-01 block. These stay in `base.py`: inference (`LLM_PROXY_*`, `LLM_GATEWAY_EGRESS_*`, `RELAY_*`); `LLM_LOCAL_AGENT_RUNS`, whose default depends on `APPS_DOCKER_HOST`, defined later in `base.py`; and `SAMPLE_BUILD_*` (the other session's BuildKit WIP, §12.4).
- **Worktree venv:** `.wt-test.sh` mounts `llmlab_wt_samplegen_venv` read-write only after `--init-venv` has synced **and verified** it. The marker `/home/ubuntu/.cache/llmlab-v3-work/llmlab_wt_samplegen_venv.ready` holds the sha256 of the `uv.lock` it was synced from, and the script warns when the worktree's lock differs. Without the marker, the script falls back to the read-only devcontainer venv. All sub-worktrees share the volume. `--init-venv` copies the devcontainer venv only when it first creates the volume, then runs `uv sync --frozen --group dev` (incremental) with the worktree mounted read-only. `--init-venv --fresh` recreates the volume. `WT_VENV=devcontainer|worktree` forces either venv. The first sync also upgraded the stale `tornado` 6.5.5 to the locked 6.6.
- **The helper scripts are tracked** (`.wt-test.sh`, `.wt-commit.sh`, `.wt-fecheck.sh`). The two lines that excluded them were removed from the shared `.git/info/exclude`. All three find their worktree from the script's own directory. `.wt-commit.sh` mounts `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` at its host path and uses the venv that `.wt-test.sh --venv-mount` reports. `.wt/` is in `.gitignore` **and** `.dockerignore`: `wt.env` holds DB credentials, and `COPY . /app` would bake it into an image built from the worktree.
- **The tests are in `config/tests/test_generation_settings.py`,** not `generation/tests/wp01_foundation/`, because A1 does not own `backend/generation/**`.

Tests set it: `test_run_presets.py:636`, `test_jobs_and_batches.py:43`, `test_profile_api.py:980`, `test_cloning_and_identity.py:63`.

This also breaks `backend/inference/tests/test_snapshot_local.py` (8 tests, through `agent/config.py`).

- **Docs still describe the removed settings:** `docs/development-guide.md:63-69`, `docs/GENERATION_PROCESS.md:218,318-329,340` (WP-08), `docs/TEMPLATE_PACKAGES.md:66` (WP-03).

## WP-00 decisions

### WP-00 (wipe command and cut-over runbook), 2026-11-03

Branch `chore/wipe-generation-v2`, worktree `/home/ubuntu/code/llmlab-wt-wipe`: `backend/generation/management/commands/wipe_generation_v2.py`, `backend/generation/tests/wp00_wipe/test_wipe.py`, `docs/design/V3_CUTOVER.md`. (The task text said to record these under "## WP-01 decisions". They are WP-00's decisions, so they get their own heading here.)

- **The v2 marker is `generation_generationjob`.** Without it the command is a no-op that says so. `generation_generationartifact` survives into v3 (§3.7) and `analysis_analysisrun.generation_job_id` keeps its column name, so the marker check gates everything: on v3 nothing is touched. When the marker exists **and** `generation_run` or `generation_study` exists too, the command refuses (half-migrated schema).
- **Tables the §3.12 list missed:** `generation_runpreset` (deleted entirely, system rows included, like `contentblock`/`generationprofile`, which also use `is_system`) and `generation_experiment_app_requirements` (the M2M through table). The FK cycles `experiment.baseline_condition`, `created_by_schedule` and `cloned_from` are set NULL first. "Non-system" `apprequirementtemplate` rows are `is_default = false`, the field labelled "system default".
- **Rows of other apps:**
  - analysis runs with `generation_job_id` set, plus their `toolresult` and `finding` rows;
  - reports with `generation_job_id` set, or whose `analysis_run` is wiped, or that a v2 post-run references (`experimentpostrun.report_id`, the auto experiment reports). Reports linked only through JSON (`config.experiment_id`) are kept;
  - runtime containers with `generation_job_id` set, plus their actions, port allocations (deleted, which frees the port) and `execution_runtimebuildlease` rows (only when the BuildKit WIP table exists). `analysis_analyzerworkspace.container_id` is set NULL.
- **Human evaluation is wiped per task, not per app.** An `EvalTask` with `experiment_id` set, or with any app whose `job_id` is set, is deleted with all its rows: apps, items, invites, participants, assignments, responses, observations, attachments, verdicts and HTTP exchanges. Dropping only the job-linked apps would silently change a task's results (apps are the unit of analysis). The attachment files of wiped tasks are deleted after commit through `default_storage`. A kept task's verdict on a wiped finding gets `finding_id = NULL` (model `SET_NULL`; `finding_key` and the snapshot stay). A kept app hosted on a wiped container gets `container_id = NULL`.
- **FK coverage check.** Before doing anything, the command reads every foreign key from `pg_catalog` and refuses with `src(cols) -> tgt` when a key points into a wiped table and no step clears it. `information_schema.constraint_column_usage` would hide tables the role does not own. Postgres FKs created by Django are `DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED` with no DB-level `ON DELETE`, so every referencing row must be handled explicitly.
- **Same counts in the dry run and with `--yes`.** Rows are selected through id sets frozen as temp tables (`pg_temp.wipe_v2_*`) before anything is deleted. The dry run runs `COUNT(*)` over the same predicates in a transaction that is rolled back. `--yes` runs `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`, so the FK order is checked statement by statement, and any error rolls back the whole wipe (tested).
- **`--yes` must be typed in full**: argparse prefix matching is off, so `--y`/`--ye` fail. Other options: `--skip-containers` and `--database`.
- **Docker.** The command stops (it does not remove) the containers of the wiped `runtime_containerinstance` rows by name, with a 10 s timeout, before the transaction. It uses `runtime.services.docker_manager.new_client()`, imported lazily. Every failure is listed and never fatal.
- **After commit (`transaction.on_commit`)**, the command deletes the contents of `GENERATION_RUNS_ROOT` (the directory itself stays; filesystem roots are refused) and the attachment files. File errors go to stderr. The database part is already committed by then.
- **Test "migrate forward".** The "v2 nodes" are every migration node except the generation migrations after `0022_artifact_telemetry` and their dependents (`backwards_plan`). The historical apps come from `loader.project_state(v2_nodes)`, because `project_state()` with no arguments would give the v3 state on the v3 branch. On `chore/wipe-generation-v2` the leaf *is* 0022, so both migrate steps are no-ops there. The test creates the WIP `execution_runtimebuildlease` table with raw SQL when the historical state has no such model.

## WP-00 → WP-01: guard and reversibility for the wipe test

- `0023_v3_wipe_guard` must not count **system** `generation_apprequirementtemplate` rows (`is_default = true`): the wipe keeps them, as §3.12 says. It should count `generation_generationartifact` and the rest of the §3.12 list, including the M2M `generation_experiment_app_requirements`. Otherwise `migrate` fails on a correctly wiped database, and the WP-00 test's final `migrate_to_leaf()` fails on the v3 branch.
- `0023` needs `reverse_code=migrations.RunPython.noop`, and `0024_v3_models` must be reversible on an empty v2 table set. The WP-00 test migrates `generation` back to `0022_artifact_telemetry` on the v3 branch.
- Migration `0016_profile_default_stack` references `backend.generation.models.default_stack_slug`, so that name must stay importable in v3. The WP-00 test passes `scaffolding_slug` explicitly, so the removal of `GENERATION_DEFAULT_STACK` does not matter to it.

## WP-01 decisions (continued)

### WP-01 A2 (identity, blobs, contracts), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/{identity,blobs}.py`, `backend/generation/contracts/**`, `backend/generation/data/tasks/schema.json` (generated), tests in `backend/generation/tests/wp01_foundation/test_{identity,identity_algebra,contracts,blobs,resources,issues}.py`.

**Identity (`identity.py`)**
- `canonical_json` NFC/LF-normalizes dict **keys** as well as values and rejects keys that collide after normalization. Anything that is not a JSON scalar, list, tuple or dict is rejected (`CanonicalJSONError`), including pydantic models (dump them with `model_dump(mode="json")` first), sets, bytes and `Decimal`. `1` and `1.0` hash differently.
- `tree_hash` rejects absolute paths, backslashes, empty/`.`/`..` segments, duplicate paths and non-lowercase-hex shas.
- `condition_hash(levels)` hashes sorted `[factor_key, level]` pairs, not bare levels: enum levels (`stub`, `true`) are not self-describing.
- `plan_hash` sorts the cell rows; `conditions` (condition hashes) and `units` (unit keys) keep index order, because cells refer to them by index.
- Every hash of section 3.1 lives in `identity.py`: `library_content_hash`, `task_structured_hash`, `task_prose_hashes`, `condition_hash`, `design_hash`, `plan_hash`, `prereg_hash`, `target_hash` and `crn_seed` (section 9.4), next to `compute_keys`. `normalized_sha` is `text_sha(<normalizer output>)` and stays with WP-13.
- `IdentityInputs.__post_init__` validates exact key sets (`TREATMENT_KEYS`, `SLOT_POLICY_KEYS`, task/target/model/images keys) and the kind↔protocol map (`app_agentic`→`app@1`, `slot_*`→`slot@1`, `reference`→`ref@1`). `slot_policy` is required for `slot@1` and forbidden for `app@1`. `ref@1` takes exactly one of task/target, and its `task_hash` uses `"protocol": "ref@1"`. `images.gateway` may be `None` only for `slot_single_shot` and `reference`: single-shot calls apply the gateway policy in-process (section 7.6), so there is no gateway image. `starter_base` digests keep the caller's order (Dockerfile FROM order).
- The hash-algebra golden values in `test_identity_algebra.py` were also re-derived with plain `hashlib` + `json.dumps` outside the codebase.

**Contracts: conventions**
- Every model forbids extra keys (a test walks every contract module). Nullable fields default to `None`, lists and dicts to empty, unless the spec gives a default. Cross-field and vocabulary rules (reasoning exclusivity, the call/context cap ratio, endpoint ids referenced by checks, the feature vocabulary, "three prose variants for system tasks") are **lint**, not schema rules, so lint can report them as registered issues.
- Library content refs accept `slug@v` or `slug@v#hash12` (always version-pinned, never a bare slug); the library service should store the hash-pinned form. `CONTENT_MODELS`/`SCHEMA_VERSIONS` give the contract and schema version per kind (task 4, every other kind 1). **Recommendation for the models part:** store and hash `contracts.library.normalize_content(kind, content)` (validated, defaults filled, JSON types), so equal content always hashes equal.
- Aliases keep the spec's wire names: `AuthFlow.register` (field `register_endpoint`), `CheckRequest.json` (field `json_body`), `PolicyClamp.from/to`. Models serialize by alias and validate by name or alias.
- Modules whose pydantic/dataclass annotations must resolve at runtime (`events`, `harness`, `library`, `manifest`, `plan`, `resources`) do not use `from __future__ import annotations`; otherwise ruff TC001 would move the imports under `TYPE_CHECKING` and break pydantic. (WP-01 A1 could instead set `lint.flake8-type-checking.runtime-evaluated-base-classes = ["pydantic.BaseModel"]` in `pyproject.toml`.)
- Shapes the spec leaves open (all additive): `SetupStep {actor?, request, expect_status?, capture}`; `CompiledRequest` = `CheckRequest` + `actor`, `headers`, `expect_status`; `CompiledCheck`/`CompiledProbe` (resolved `requires_checks`); `AuthDriverConfig`; `CheckResult.skipped_due_to`; `EvalResult.flaky_checks`; `EvalStages`/`StageRecord`; `AdherenceRecord.detected` is optional (only for `not_measured`); `PromptTemplateContent.tags` (the aider brief variant is tagged `editor`, section 5.3); `TaskDerived`, `TaskLint` (its `issues` are typed `Issue`), `TaskOracleEntry`; `Validity {termination, truncated, notes}`; `RunResult`, `RunMetrics` (+ `EvalSummary` for `metrics.eval`), `AttemptServed`, `AttemptUsage`, `TimeSplit`; slot `SlotInterface`, `SlotSetSelection`, `SlotIndexStats`, `OutOfSlotEdit`, `DiffVsOriginal`, `SlotAgentTarget`, `ClusterParams`, `PatternScope`, `PatternMatch`; `StimulusRecord`/`SourceSpan` for the `RunManifest.stimuli` column.
- **Manifest.** `ManifestBody` = every section except `study` and `created_at`; `ResolvedCell.manifest` carries it and `build_manifest` adds the two. `model`, `sampling`, `resources` and `identity` are required except for `ref@1`. `gateway`, `harness` and `sandbox` are required only for `app_agentic` and `slot_agent`. A `FactorRecord` has exactly one of `ref` (+ `content_hash`) or `value`. `ManifestSlot.context_policy` is a typed `ContextPolicy`.
- `HarnessContext`, `RenderedHarness`, `GatewayWiring`, `ImageSpec`, `ExitInfo`, `HarnessCapabilities`, `HarnessFacts` and `SlotAgentContext` are frozen dataclasses in `contracts/harness.py` (`HarnessContext` is `kw_only`, so the spec's field order works). `Termination` lives in `contracts/outcome.py` and is re-exported there. WP-07's `harnesses/base.py` should import these, not redefine them. `HarnessContext.policy` is `contracts.resources.EffectivePolicy`.
- `TERMINATION_OUTCOME` rows are `TerminationRule(generation, final_outcome | "evaluation", usable, truncated, retryable, reason, note)`; `outcome_reason` is `llm_error` for LLM_ERROR and `infra_interrupted` for INFRA.
- Validity notes are registered in `contracts/outcome.py` (`NOTE_CODES` plus the parametric `budget_exhausted:<kind>`, `call_truncations:<n>`, `unsupported_knob:<key>`, `lock_source:<model|platform>`, `adherence:<status>`). `Note` rejects unregistered codes. Added beyond section 6.13: `truncated` (7.6), `routing_partial` (appendix B), `batch_unsupported` (4.2).
- Events: `study:<id>` ports the experiment events (`job` becomes `run`, plus `tick`, `state`, `post_run`) and adds `spend`, `budget_alert`, `stopped`, `paused`. `run:<id>` has `status`, `step`, `usage`, `trajectory_step`, `log` and `evaluation`.

**Resources (`contracts/resources.py`)**
- Every `ResourcePolicyContent` field is required, so a stored policy is always complete. `preset_content(tier)` builds the three tiers from `KNOB_META`; WP-11's YAML must equal it (`test_resources.py` already checks the table values).
- `KNOB_META[key]` = `{key, cls, cls_when_tokens, where, ui_group, type, unit, description, defaults{frugal,standard,generous}, key_limit}`. `reasoning` is one group knob ({mode, effort, max_tokens}) with `reasoning.exclude` separate. `pilot.gates` is expanded into one knob per gate. `run.max_cost_usd` has `cls_when_tokens="O"`. Enforcement points add `EVL`, `BLD` and `UI` (used by the table, not in its legend). `KEY_LIMITS` holds the seven policy knobs; "repeats" is a Study column.
- Units: `MB`/`GB` are MiB/GiB (`run.max_workspace_bytes`). `k`/`M` token counts are decimal (72k = 72_000, 64k = 64_000), so frugal satisfies `call.max_input_tokens >= 1.1 x context.max_context_tokens` (72_000 >= 70_400; with binary 64k it would not). `retries.retry_on` is stored as sorted strings (`"429"`, `"5xx"`). `routing.max_price` accepts `"model×k"` or ASCII `"modelxk"` and normalizes to `×`.
- Default stop rules: `consecutive_failures` scope condition, window 5, threshold 5; `cost_overrun` scope condition, window 5, threshold 2.0, action `pause_condition` (the extra alert is STU's job); `spend_fraction` action `alert`. They are stored normalized (every `StopRule` default filled).
- `values_by_class(content, {"C","R"}, budget_basis=...)` returns the flat `{knob_key: value}` view. WP-11's `treatment_view()` should be exactly that call on the clamped content, with `study.budget_basis` resolved, so `generation_policy_hash = H(that dict)`.
- `EffectivePolicy {content, budget_basis, cap_basis, serving_tier, clamps, notes, issues}` and `GatewayConfig` (`config_version 1`: run/attempt/session, front protocol + mode, upstream, model pin + presented names, pricing, sampling, reasoning, budget, call, routing.provider, caching, retries, rate_limit, request_class_source) are data contracts here. WP-11 builds them and WP-06 reads `GatewayConfig`; ask WP-01 for changes.

**Issue registry (`contracts/issues.py`)**
- `Issue {code, severity, message, cells, path, data, fix: [IssueFix{label, action, params}]}` rejects unregistered codes and severities the registry does not allow. **Every new code, including WP-03's library lint codes, is added here through CHANGES before it is emitted.** `make_issue(code, study_kind, ...)` picks the registry severity.
- Codes added beyond the names in sections 6.15 and 4.3: `combination_pruned` (info; section 9.4 "removed combinations are listed in issues with info"), `mixed_config_fingerprint` (warn/error; section 13.4 "a mixed config_fingerprint is flagged"), `exclusion_rate` and `differential_attrition` (warn/warn; 6.13, 7.9), `probe_unvalidated` (warn/warn; 8.3), `editor_harness_agent_brief` (error; 5.3).
- `primary_feature_missing` is `error` in confirmatory studies too: section 7.10 requires primary features for confirmatory slot studies, although the 6.15 table shows "–". `model_alias_in_confirmatory`, `adaptive_stop_in_confirmatory` and `usd_basis_multi_model` do not apply to exploratory studies (`None`). `estimate_exceeds_cap` defaults to `warn` and allows `error`; `single_parent`/`manual_parent_selection` default to `error` in confirmatory and allow `info` (estimand `conditional_on_parent`).
- `test_issues.py` greps the spec (6.15 table, 4.3 lint table, and phrases like "issue `x`", "`x` (warn") and fails on any named code missing from the registry; it also fails on a registered code the spec never names, unless it is in the documented list above.

**Blob store (`blobs.py`)**
- zstd when `zstandard` imports, else gzip. The codec is recorded in `Blob.codec` and in the file extension (`.zst`/`.gz`), readers accept both, and an existing blob is never rewritten in another codec. (The read-only devcontainer venv has no `zstandard`; the worktree venv after `--init-venv` does.)
- The file is written before the `Blob` row (`get_or_create`). A repeated put refreshes the file mtime, so a blob about to be referenced again is not collected. Reads verify the sha. `put_file` streams in one pass through `sha256/.incoming/`. `put_json(obj)` returns `H(obj)` and `put_text(s)` returns `text_sha(s)`. `record=False` skips the row (tests, staging).
- GC (`gc_blobs(dry_run=True, min_age=7d, referenced=None)`) uses file mtimes and collects references from every model's `BLOB_FIELDS` columns plus an optional `iter_blob_refs()` classmethod (for shas inside JSON, e.g. `StarterContent.files`, `RunManifest.stimuli`). The `gc_blobs` management command is not written (it is not in A2's paths); it is a thin wrapper.
- `data/tasks/schema.json` is generated by `contracts.library.write_task_schema()` (draft 2020-12, by-alias form) and checked in. Section 11.1 says it is generated from WP-01's contracts, but it lives in WP-04's `data/` tree: WP-04 must not hand-edit it; regenerate it after any `TaskSpecContent` change.

## WP-01 A2 → WP-01 (models part): what the contracts expect

- `Blob`: `sha256` (PK, Char(64)), `size`, `codec` (Char(8), `zst`|`gz`), `media_type` (Char(80); `blobs.py` truncates), `created_at`. `blobs.py` calls `objects.get_or_create(sha256=..., defaults={size, codec, media_type})` and `objects.filter(sha256__in=...).delete()`.
- Models with sha columns pointing into the blob store list them in `BLOB_FIELDS`; models whose JSON embeds blob shas expose `iter_blob_refs()`.
- JSON fields validate with: `library.CONTENT_MODELS[kind]`, `library.TaskDerived`/`TaskLint`/`TaskOracleEntry`, `plan.Design`/`Estimate`, `Issue` (lists), `outcome.Validity`/`RunResult`/`RunMetrics`/`AttemptServed`/`AttemptUsage`/`TimeSplit`, `manifest.RunManifest`/`StimulusRecord`, `evaluation.EvalStages`/`EvalResult`/`AdherenceRecord`, `slot.SlotAddress`/`ContextPolicy`/`SlotInterface`/`TargetValidation`/`LeakReport`/`SlotSetSelection`/`SlotIndexStats`/`OutOfSlotEdit`/`DiffVsOriginal`/`ClusterParams`/`PatternScope`/`PatternMatch`.

## WP-01 decisions (continued)

### WP-01 A3 (models, migrations, admin, router, skeletons, RunView), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/models/{__init__,choices,base,library,study,run,slot,spend}.py` (replaces `models.py`), `migrations/0023_v3_wipe_guard.py`, `migrations/0024_v3_models.py`, `{apps,admin}.py`, `api/__init__.py`, `runs/view.py`, the package skeletons, `build/context.py`, `runtime/services/build_context.py`, the FK strings and v3 migrations of `analysis` (`0010_v3_code_scope`), `inference` (`0003_v3_endpoint_cost_basis`), `runtime` (`0006_v3_remove_stack`), tests `tests/wp01_foundation/test_{models,migrations}.py` and the temporary `tests/wp01_foundation/settings_u1.py`.

**Validation mechanics (`models/base.py`)**
- `ValidatedModel.CONTRACT_FIELDS = {field: Contract(schema)}` is checked by `full_clean()` (inside `clean_fields`) **and** by `save()`; `save(update_fields=...)` checks only the listed fields. `None` is skipped for nullable JSON columns. `QuerySet.update()`, `bulk_create()` and raw SQL bypass it; the key invariants are also database check constraints.
- `derive_fields()` fills derived columns before validation and before every save: an empty column is filled, a supplied value that differs is a `ValidationError` (never silently overwritten). `DERIVED_FROM` extends `update_fields` so `save(update_fields=["design"])` also writes `design_hash` and the mirrored columns.
- Frozen rows raise `ImmutableVersionError`, an `APIError` with status 409 (`backend.common.exceptions`), so a view that lets it escape answers 409. `VersionedQuerySet.update()` refuses immutable columns too.
- The module is `models/base.py`, not `_base.py`: migration 0024 references `backend.generation.models.base.validate_rel_path`.

**Library**
- `content` is stored **normalized** (`Contract.dump`: validated, defaults filled, aliases applied) and `content_hash = identity.library_content_hash(KIND, schema_version, normalized)`. WP-03/WP-04/WP-10 golden hashes must use the normalized form (`VersionedEntity.compute_content_hash(content)` does it). `schema_version` defaults to `SCHEMA_VERSIONS[KIND]` and must equal it at creation; `content` is validated only at creation (it is immutable).
- Query columns are **derived from content** and verified: `StackProfile.{backend,frontend}_framework/database`, `Starter.level` and `tree_hash` (= `identity.tree_hash` over `content.files`), `StyleDirective.system`, `Guidance.security_hint`, `PromptTemplate.role`, `PromptSet.mode`, `HarnessPreset.harness/harness_version`, `EvalSuite.tester_version` (= `content.tester.version`), `PatternDetector.engine/feature_key`. `ResourcePolicy.tier` is a plain column (default `custom`). `Starter.clean()` requires `content.stack_profile` to name the FK (`ref` or `pinned_ref`).
- Extra constraints beyond 3.3: `version >= 1` and `visibility = 'system' ⇔ is_system` (database). `objects.latest(slug)` overrides `Manager.latest(*fields)` as the spec says; `objects.latest_versions()` returns one row per slug. Reverse accessor of `derived_from` is `derivatives`.
- `TaskSpec.latest_version` defaults to 0 (no version yet). `TaskSpecVersion` derives `content_hash`, `structured_hash` and `prose_hashes` (prose is stored as written and hashed with `text_sha`, so CRLF prose hashes like LF). `derived` and `lint` are nullable until computed; `oracle` defaults to `{}`.
- `Blob` lives in `models/library.py` (`codec` choices `zst`/`gz`). `BLOB_FIELDS`: `GenerationRun.{workspace_blob,resolved_lock_blob}`, `RunFile.blob`, `StudyPlan.payload_blob`, `RunSymbolIndex.blob`, `SlotTarget.original_blob`, `SlotVariantResult.replacement_blob`; `iter_blob_refs()`: `Starter` (`content.files[*].blob`), `RunManifest` (`stimuli[*].blob`).

**Studies**
- `design` is stored normalized and `design_hash = identity.design_hash(dump)` is recomputed on every save. `kind`, `mode`, `repeats`, `base_seed`, `order_seed` and `scheduling` are **mirrors of the design**, rewritten from it on every save (edit the design, not the columns). `clean()` requires `design.resource_policy` to name the `resource_policy` FK.
- A locked study (`locked_at` set in the database) refuses changes to `design`, `analysis_plan` and `resource_policy` (`ImmutableVersionError`); every other column stays mutable.
- Dropped from v2 `Experiment` (not in 3.6): `paused_at`, the `(created_by, slug)` unique constraint, `post_run`, `infra_retries` (now `retries.infra_retries` in the policy), `baseline_condition` (now `StudyCondition.is_baseline`), `created_by_schedule`. `budget_basis` defaults to `usd`, `cap_basis` to `billed`. `pause_reason` is validated (`budget|quota|spend_cap|manual|infra|rule:<code>`). `analysis_plan` stays free-form JSON (its schema is `study/analysis_plan.py`, WP-10).
- New contracts for columns 3.6 types loosely (added to `contracts/plan.py`): `ExecutionOptions` (`Study.execution_options`; the `concurrency` knobs minus `study_max_parallel`, plus `warmup`) and `StopReason` (`Study.stop_reason`).
- Deletion: the study's children (factors, plans, conditions, units, cells, prompt overrides, schedules, post-runs) **cascade** from the study (the spec says CASCADE only for factors, plans and conditions; the rest were bare "FK"). The plan, condition and unit references inside the tree are **RESTRICT** (spec: PROTECT for `StudyCondition.plan`): a plan cannot be deleted while rows refer to it, but deleting a whole study works (PROTECT would make every planned study undeletable). Runs keep `SET_NULL`; `PatternRun.study` and `SpendLedgerEntry.study/run` are PROTECT.
- `StudyCondition.condition_hash` is derived (`identity.condition_hash(levels)`). Database constraints: `StudyUnit` references exactly the object its key names (`task:` ⇒ task version, `slot:` ⇒ slot target); `StudyCell.exclusion_reason` is set only when `state=excluded`. `StudyPlan` rows are immutable except `status`.
- `StudyPostRun.kind`: `analysis` (the ported v2 analysis-then-report pipeline, default) or `patterns`; a patterns post-run keeps `{"detectors": "default" | [pinned refs], ...}` in `config`. Added a nullable `pattern_run` FK next to `report`. New index names `studypostrun_active` and `studysched_due` (Postgres index names are schema-global).

**Runs**
- `protocol` is derived from `kind` (`identity.KIND_PROTOCOL`) and verified; `lane` is set from `kind` at creation (`sandbox` for `app_agentic`/`slot_agent`, `llm` for `slot_single_shot`). Extra check constraint `genrun_kind_protocol`. `clean()`: `usable` is set only with an outcome and equals `outcome ∈ USABLE_OUTCOMES`; `excluded` needs an `ExclusionReason` in `outcome_reason`; `reference_of` is set exactly for reference runs.
- The five identity sha columns may be blank (reference runs without keys). `config_fingerprint` is indexed by `genrun_cfp` only (no duplicate `db_index`). `created_by` is `SET_NULL` (v2: CASCADE).
- **Reverse accessors renamed:** `LLMModel.generation_runs`, `LLMEndpoint.generation_runs`, `User.generation_runs` and `User.studies` (v2: `generation_jobs`, `jobs`, `generation_jobs`, `experiments`). Library FKs on runs use `runs`. Forward FK names on other apps are unchanged (D5).
- `RunManifest` is write-once (any update raises), stores the normalized manifest, derives `manifest_hash = H(manifest)` and `protocol`; `clean()` checks `kind`, `protocol` and the identity keys against the run's columns.
- `RunEvaluation` rows may change while `pending`/`running` and are frozen once `measured`, `not_measured` or `error` (append-only per Appendix A #7). Reverse accessor of `reused_from` is `reuses`.
- `RunAttempt.attempt >= 1` (database). `GenerationArtifact.attempt` defaults to 1. `RunFile.blob` may be empty only for `origin=deleted` (database); `path` is validated as a relative POSIX path.

**Slot and patterns**
- `SlotTarget.target_hash` is derived with `identity.target_hash(parent_tree=slot_set.frozen_tree_hash, address=SlotAddress.identity(), original_sha, allowlist, interface=SlotInterface dump)`, `kind` from `address.kind`; those inputs are frozen after creation. `SlotSet.clean()` checks the parent is a usable `app_agentic` run, `frozen_tree_hash == parent.tree_hash`, the task version and that `index` belongs to the parent.
- `PatternHit`: database constraint `status = 'error' ⇔ error_reason <> ''` (a detector error is never silent). `PatternRun` is frozen once `completed`. `ClusterLabel.clean()` requires a `manual` detector. `DetectorAudit.eval_task` is `SET_NULL`.
- New contracts in `contracts/slot.py`: `LinkedChecksDiff` (`SlotTarget.linked_checks_diff`) and `AuditSample` (`DetectorAudit.sample`).

**Spend**
- `SpendLedgerEntry.save()` refuses updates (append-only; write an `adjust` row). `SpendCap`: database constraints `limit >= 0` and "the scope's target is set"; `user`/`endpoint` CASCADE (cap configuration dies with its owner).
- `LLMEndpoint.cost_basis` defaults to `billed`; `inference 0003` sets existing ollama endpoints to `free`. Subscription endpoints (OpenCode Go) must be switched to `list_price` by their owner. `SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_DEFAULTS` gained `cost_basis`.
- `AnalysisRun.code_scope` is `Char(8)` (`app`/`model`/`slot`, blank for inline-source runs) and `code_scope_paths_sha` is `H(sorted analysed paths)` (computed by WP-02b).

**Migrations**
- `0024_v3_models` = hand-written `RenameModel`/`AlterModelTable` (GenerationJob → GenerationRun `generation_run`, Experiment → Study `generation_study`) and `RenameField` (`GenerationArtifact.job` → `run`), then the `makemigrations` output, hand-checked. It **depends on the v2 leaves of analysis, execution, human_eval, inference, reports and runtime**, so every historical FK to `generationjob`/`experiment` exists before the renames repoint them. With that ordering the FK-string changes in the five other apps need **no `AlterField` migrations** (`makemigrations --check` is clean); without it the graph may apply `human_eval 0001` after the rename, and `makemigrations` did generate `AlterField`s.
- `0023_v3_wipe_guard` counts every §3.12 table plus `generation_runpreset` and the M2M `generation_experiment_app_requirements`, but **not seed rows**: `apprequirementtemplate.is_default` (WP-00's ask) and also `is_system` content blocks, profiles and run presets, which the v2 post-migrate seeding recreates whenever the v2 web container restarts between the wipe and the v3 deploy. 0024 drops those tables anyway. `reverse_code=noop`; 0024 is reversible on an empty database (tested: back to 0022 and forward).
- `runtime 0006_v3_remove_stack` depends on `generation 0024_v3_models` and `runtime 0005_sample_build_artifact`. Note: generation migrations 0016/0019 do not actually FK `runtime.Stack` (0016 references `models.default_stack_slug`); the dependency is kept as the spec says. `models.default_stack_slug()` stays importable and returns the v2 literal `"flask-react"`.

**Router, apps, skeletons**
- `api/__init__.py` attaches the area routers **without a prefix** in the section 9 order (`AREA_ROUTERS`); each area declares full paths (`/harnesses/` and `/config/` share one router, so per-area prefixes would not fit). The retained telemetry/trajectory/variability/job_insights routers are not imported (v2 views).
- `apps.py`: the v2 post-migrate seeding is gone; post-migrate calls `library.seeding.seed_library(using=...)` only when `GENERATION_SEED_ON_MIGRATE` is truthy (absent → off), and never breaks `migrate`.
- Skeleton stubs raise `NotImplementedError("<WP>: not implemented yet")`. Package-level functions named by the spec live in `__init__.py`: `prompts.render_set` (re-exported from `engine`), `evaluation.credentials`, `patterns.run`; `study/seeds.py` re-exports `identity.crn_seed`. `evaluation.acceptance.compile` keeps the spec's name (`noqa: A001`).
- `build/context.py`: `BuildContextReport` is implemented (`mode="none"`, `applicable=False`, `notes`, `changed=False`, `to_dict()` with the v2 `HealingReport` keys plus `notes`) and `check_healing()` rejects any mode but `None`/`"none"`; `prepare_build_dir` checks healing and then raises `NotImplementedError` (WP-02a). The shim also re-exports `build_identity`.
- `runs/view.py`: dataclasses and signatures exactly as 3.11, plus a `CodeScope` literal alias; every accessor raises `NotImplementedError` (WP-02a).
- **Temporary test settings** `tests/wp01_foundation/settings_u1.py` (= `config.settings.test` with `backend.integrations` swapped for an app config whose `ready()` connects nothing). Under the real settings `django.setup()` fails until WP-02b re-points the webhook labels. Delete it at the U1 gate.

## WP-01 → WP-02a / WP-02b / lead: blockers found while running the v3 models

- **`django.setup()` fails** under `config.settings.test`: `integrations/signals.py:connect` calls `apps.get_model("generation.GenerationJob")` (labels in `integrations/services/webhooks.py:74-98`; WP-02b). Until then every pytest run needs `--ds=backend.generation.tests.wp01_foundation.settings_u1`, and management commands need `--skip-checks` (`migrate`, `makemigrations`).
- **URLconf**: `config/api.py:41` mounts `backend.generation.api.views.router` (v2 views). It must become `api.add_router("/generation/", "backend.generation.api.router")` when WP-02a deletes `api/views/` (the file is in no WP's list; lead or WP-02a). Other routers fail at import too, e.g. `backend/users/activity.py:9` (`Experiment`), `analysis/api/views/{context,runs}.py`, `execution/api_views.py`, `export/api/views.py`, `runtime/api/views.py`.
- Modules importing removed generation models at module level (WP-02a/WP-02b): `analysis/api/views/{context,runs}.py`, `execution/{api_views,executor,reconciler,transitions,workers}.py`, `execution/management/commands/executor.py`, `export/{api/views,services/review_sample}.py`, `human_eval/services/{pool,tasks}.py`, `llm_models/{management/commands/import_external_covariates,services/insights}.py`, `rankings/services/aggregator.py`, `reports/services/{generators,study_report}.py`, `runtime/{api/views,management/commands/benchmark_sample_builds}.py`, `statistics/management/commands/healing_ablation.py`, `statistics/services/{accounting,aggregations,study,trend}.py`, `system/services/{maintenance,metrics}.py`, `users/activity.py`.
- `runtime/apps.py` still runs the v2 Stack seeding after `migrate`; it now logs `ImportError: cannot import name 'Stack'` (caught). WP-02a deletes `runtime/seeding.py` and the hook.
- `analysis/models.py:AnalysisRun.get_code_for_analysis` still reads `generation_job.result_data` (now always `{}`). WP-02b re-points the method body to `run_view(run).code_files("app")` and records `code_scope="app"` and `code_scope_paths_sha`; WP-01 grants WP-02b that edit inside `analysis/models.py`.
- The reverse accessors renamed above (`generation_jobs`, `jobs`, `experiments`) must be re-pointed by their readers.
- WP-03: add `GENERATION_SEED_ON_MIGRATE` to the WP-03 settings block; `library.seeding.seed_library(*, using)` is the hook's call signature.
- (A3, guard) `generation_generationartifact` keeps its table name in v3 (`GenerationArtifact` is not renamed), so the guard's count of it only means "v2 data" when 0023 runs on a v2 schema, which is the only time it runs. Re-applying 0023 after migrating a v3 database back to 0022 refuses while v3 artifact rows exist; that is intended.
- (A3, tests) Run the WP-01 suite with `.wt-test.sh wp01m -- pytest -q --ds=backend.generation.tests.wp01_foundation.settings_u1 backend/generation/tests/wp01_foundation`. `test_migrations.py::test_round_trip_through_v2_...` takes about two minutes (Django renders the whole project state twice); the other migration tests use throwaway tables and the migration graph only.

## WP-01 decisions (continued)

### WP-01 A4 (testing infrastructure), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/testing/{__init__,content,factories,fake_gateway,fake_harness,fake_sandbox,fake_eval}.py`, `backend/generation/testing/golden_library/**`, `backend/generation/tests/factories.py` (shim), `backend/generation/tests/wp01_foundation/test_testing_{factories,fakes,golden}.py`, and the `collect_ignore = ["generation/_legacy"]` line in `backend/conftest.py`.

- **Factories** cover every concrete model in `generation.models` (a test fails when a model has none), and every default instance passes `full_clean()`. Derived columns are left to the models. Study rows derive from the top: `StudyCondition` and `StudyUnit` take their study from `plan`, and `StudyCell` takes plan and study from `condition`. Pass `plan=` or `condition=`; passing `study=` alone gives a mismatched tree. Slot variants (traits `slot_single_shot` and `slot_agent`) take their parent run and task from the target. Other traits: `GenerationRunFactory(tested=True | reference=True)`, `RunEvaluationFactory(measured=True)`, `PatternHitFactory(error=True | present=True)`, `StudyUnitFactory(slot=True)`, `PatternDetectorFactory(manual=True)`, and `system=True` on every library factory. `TaskSpecVersionFactory` picks the next free version and moves `TaskSpec.latest_version` with it; `RunAttemptFactory` picks the next attempt. `BlobFactory` writes only the index row.
- **Typing:** factories are `DjangoModelFactory[Model]`, but factory_boy's stubs type `XFactory()` as the factory. Write `XFactory.create()` (or `.create_batch()`) in typed code; the new tests do.
- **`run_manifest_content(run)`** builds a manifest that agrees with the run (protocol, kind, task block, slot block from the target, the run's non-empty identity columns). The other payload builders are in `testing/content.py`: library content per kind, `design_content`, `manifest_content`, `eval_result_content` and `adherence_content`.
- **Shim:** `generation/tests/factories.py` re-exports exactly `testing.factories.__all__`, with no v2 names. The v2 names that consumers still import:
  - `GenerationJobFactory` (58 import lines) → `GenerationRunFactory`
  - `AppRequirementTemplateFactory` (38) → `TaskSpecVersionFactory`
  - `ExperimentFactory` (27) → `StudyFactory`
  - `ExperimentConditionFactory` (23) → `StudyConditionFactory`
  - `GenerationProfileFactory` (13) → no equivalent
  - `ContentBlockFactory` (6) → `PromptTemplateFactory` or `StyleDirectiveFactory`

  They are imported in 70 files across 19 apps. Re-pointing them is WP-02b's job.
- **FakeGateway semantics.** Each choice below is the simplest one consistent with §4.5, §5.4 and §6.6:
  - **Sticky exhaustion:** once a run is exhausted, every later call is refused, even a smaller call that would still fit.
  - **Exhaustion kinds:** `requests`, `aux_requests`, `cost`, `tokens`, `uncached_input` and `output_tokens`, checked in that order.
  - **Request caps** count successful calls plus in-flight calls of the class. Failed calls count only when `count_failed_calls` is set.
  - **Token caps** reserve `est_prompt + max_tokens` per in-flight call.
  - **`cost_basis`:** `free` never trips the cost cap; `list_price` accrues list cost.
  - **Over `call.max_input_tokens`:** the call gets 400 `context_budget` and the run is not exhausted.
  - **Refused calls** are not written to `calls.jsonl`; they appear only in `gateway_events.jsonl`.
  - **Request-class headers** match case-insensitively, and unknown values count as `auxiliary`.
  - **`state.json`** has exactly the 13 keys listed in §4.5.
  - **`calls.jsonl` records:** `canned_call()` builds a record with the same keys, and a test pins them. They keep the native system prompt inline (`native_system_prompt`, first `main` call only) instead of as a blob.
  - **Not modelled:** the rpm token bucket, `cache_control`, passthrough, the Responses and Gemini fronts, Anthropic streaming and upstream retries.
- **FakeHarness / FakeSandbox:**
  - **Fake command:** it runs a stdlib Python script, so the agent image needs `python3` if the fake is ever used in a real container.
  - **Path mapping:** in-container paths are prefixed with `$LLMLAB_ROOT`, which `FakeSandbox` sets to its temp root and which is empty in a container.
  - **Termination rules:** `classify_termination` implements the §5.1 default rules, because the WP-01 `HarnessAdapter` stub raises `NotImplementedError`. WP-07 should move the rules into the base class and drop the override; tests pin the table.
  - **Entry script:** the sandbox's entry script is a local bash re-implementation of §6.5 steps 1-5, not the future `harnesses/base.py:entry_script()`, which uses absolute `/workspace` paths.
  - **Not simulated:** idle detection and OOM.
  - **Oversized workspace:** when the tar exceeds `max_workspace_bytes`, it is replaced by `/logs/workspace.manifest`.
- **FakeEvaluator** writes real, final `RunEvaluation` rows. They are append-only, and a not-measured result has `status=not_measured` with `agent_pass_rate=None`, never 0. `install(monkeypatch)` patches `evaluation.suite.evaluate`.
- **Golden library.** It has 2 tasks (`crud_todo_list`, `notes_markdown_render`), 2 stacks (`flask-react`, `fastapi-vue`), 2 starters (`flask-react-minimal`, `fastapi-vue-bare`), 2 styles, 2 guidance entries, 9 prompt templates, 3 prompt sets, 2 harness presets, 2 sampling presets, `frugal` (frozen from `preset_content("frugal")`), `eval-v2` and the regex detector `py-sql-fstring`, all at v1 with `slug@1` refs.
  - **Parent app:** `parent_app/` is a Flask + React todo app grown from the minimal starter, so its files cover the starter, modified, authored and test origins. It has two deliberate defects: f-string SQL in `list_todos` (cwe-89, matched by the detector) and no owner check in `update_todo` (cwe-639). `parent_app.yaml` maps endpoint ids to handlers and component names to files.
  - **Frozen hashes:** `hashes.json` freezes every content hash plus the parent tree hash. A change to the fixture *or to a content contract's defaults* (for example WP-11 changing `ResourcePolicyContent`) fails `test_hashes_are_frozen`; regenerate with `write_golden_hashes()` on purpose.
  - **Installers:** `install_golden_library()` is idempotent and refuses a slug@1 that has other content. `install_parent_run()` creates the usable parent run. Its `loc` is a count of non-blank lines until WP-02b's `source_loc` lands.
- **Tooling around the fixture trees.**
  - **Lint:** `golden_library/ruff.toml` extends the root config with per-file ignores (INP001, S104, S608 and others) for `parent_app/**` and `starters/**`.
  - **pytest:** `golden_library/conftest.py` keeps pytest from collecting `parent_app/backend/tests/test_todos.py`.
  - **djlint:** both `index.html` files are already in djlint's format (`<!DOCTYPE html>`, indent 2), so the pre-commit reformat does not move the frozen hashes.

## WP-01 A4 → lead (pyproject, WP-01): mypy exclude for the golden fixture trees

- `mypy backend` maps `golden_library/parent_app/backend/` onto the real `backend` package and reports `Module "backend" has no attribute "db"`. Please add `exclude = ["backend/generation/testing/golden_library/(parent_app|starters)/"]` to `[tool.mypy]`. Until then, run mypy on the `testing/*.py` modules only. `mypy` itself needs a settings override until U1, because the django-stubs plugin calls `django.setup()`. A temporary config with `django_settings_module = backend.generation.tests.wp01_foundation.settings_u1` works: the A4 modules and tests pass with no issues.
- `docs/README.md` (WP-10): optionally mention `backend/generation/testing/` (factories, fakes, golden library) in the testing section.

## WP-01 decisions

### WP-01 fixer, review round 1 (6 MAJORs), 2026-11-03

- **Library immutability (MAJOR 1).**
  - **Which columns are frozen:** every concrete library kind freezes `VERSION_IMMUTABLE_FIELDS` (slug, version, schema_version, content, content_hash, `derived_from`, `is_system`), plus its `CONTENT_COLUMNS`, plus its extras: `Starter.stack_profile`/`tree_hash` and `ResourcePolicy.tier`. `models.library.immutable_fields()` builds the list. Only name, description, is_archived and visibility stay mutable, plus the audit columns `created_by`/`created_at`. A test asserts this for each kind: immutable = concrete columns − the mutable four − audit.
  - **Task versions:** `TaskSpecVersion` also freezes `derived_from`. `lint`, `oracle` and `derived` stay mutable.
  - **Slot sets:** `SlotSet.IMMUTABLE_FIELDS` = parent_run, task_version, parent_model, parent_harness, frozen_tree_hash, parent_manifest_hash, parent_eval, index, selection. `save()` refuses changes to these. `name`, `notes` and `parent_build_key` stay mutable; `parent_build_key` is a build-cache key.
  - **Bulk path:** `models.base.FrozenFieldsQuerySet` refuses `update()` of `IMMUTABLE_FIELDS`, under either the field name or the attname (`stack_profile_id`). It is used by `VersionedQuerySet` (every library kind), `TaskSpecVersion`, `SlotSet` and `StudyPlan`. MINOR 4 is fixed too: `WriteOnceQuerySet` refuses every `update()` on `RunManifest` and `SpendLedgerEntry`, and `RunEvaluation.objects.update()` is refused when the queryset contains a final row. Raw SQL and Django's `on_delete=SET_NULL` collector still bypass these guards.
- **Pinned refs (MAJOR 2).** Both of the reviewer's options are implemented together.
  - **Contract:** `contracts.library.VersionRef` is now `PinnedRefStr` (`slug@v#hash12`). `LIBRARY_REF_FIELDS` lists every ref field: stack `brief_fragment`, starter `stack_profile`, style/guidance `fragment` and prompt_set `roles.*`. A test checks that the list covers every pinned-ref-typed content field.
  - **`pin_content_refs(kind, content, resolve)`** is a pure function that rewrites bare `slug@v` refs to the pinned form:
    - A bare ref that cannot be resolved is an error.
    - A pinned ref whose hash disagrees with an existing row is an error.
    - A pinned ref to a version the resolver does not know is accepted. Packages are imported in dependency order and tests use fake hashes. `lint_unknown_library_ref` covers such refs.
  - **On create:** `VersionedEntity.derive_fields` calls it with a DB resolver before normalizing and hashing. Seeders (WP-03/WP-04) and the library service may therefore pass `slug@v`, and the stored content is always pinned. Two copies of one entity hash alike.
  - **Starter check:** `Starter.clean` now requires exactly `stack_profile.pinned_ref`.
  - **Golden fixture:** its YAML still writes `slug@1`; `load_golden_library()` pins it in memory in `INSTALL_ORDER`. `GoldenEntry.content` is the stored, pinned form.
  - **Test builders:** `testing.content` builders pin with `fake_ref("slug@v")`, a deterministic fake hash12. `StarterFactory` uses the profile's `pinned_ref`.
  - **Consequence:** `hashes.json` was regenerated. Every stack, starter, style, guidance and prompt-set hash changed.
  - **Not changed:** `Design` refs (`contracts/plan.py`) were not touched.
- **Nested blob references (MAJOR 3).** New `iter_blob_refs()` hooks, which `blobs.referenced_shas()` already calls:
  - `GenerationRun`: the `ResolvedLock.files` values inside each `resolved_lock_blob` payload.
  - `RunEvaluation`: `adherence.sbom_blob` and every `stages.<stage>.detail.credentials_ref`.
  - `StudyPlan`: `cells[*].stimuli[*].blob` inside each payload. Every plan is scanned, not only unlaunched ones, because the payload blob itself is kept.
  - `RunAttempt`: `observed_inputs.native_system_prompt_blob`.
  - **Reading payloads:** `blobs.nested_blob_refs(sha, extract)` reads a payload blob. A missing, corrupt or non-JSON payload yields nothing (logged), and any other error propagates, so GC aborts instead of deleting.
  - **Test:** `test_review_round1.py::test_gc_keeps_every_sha_the_factories_and_builders_write` creates one row per factory. It then asserts that every `BLOB_FIELDS` value and every JSON value under a `blob`, `*_blob` or `credentials_ref` key is referenced, and that a real `gc_blobs` run deletes only an unreferenced control blob.
  - **Rule for new JSON fields:** any JSON field holding a blob sha must use one of those key names, or the test cannot see it.
- **Trajectory and observed inputs (MAJOR 4; unblocks WP-08).** I chose `RunAttempt` columns, not `RunResult` keys, because both are per-attempt facts:
  - `RunAttempt.observed_inputs`: JSON, nullable, validated by `Contract(ObservedInputs)`. It is queryable, e.g. `observed_inputs__tool_names__contains`, and `observed_inputs__system_prompt_sha`.
  - `RunAttempt.trajectory_blob`: `sha_field(blank)` in `BLOB_FIELDS`, holding the `parse_trajectory` result as JSON in the blob store.
  - **Runner (§6.9):** writes both on the attempt row. The current run's values are those of its last attempt.
  - **Migration:** the columns were folded into the single `0024_v3_models` migration (still one hand-checked migration). `makemigrations --check` is clean.
- **Golden starters (MAJOR 5).** Added `fastapi-vue-minimal`, a FastAPI app serving `/api/health` and the Vite build with a one-component Vue shell. Both stacks now have a `minimal` starter, so the §13.3 golden plan (2 tasks × 2 stacks, `starter_level: minimal`) can resolve. `fastapi-vue-bare` stays for WP-09's "bare starter scores < 50%" check. The fixture now has 3 starters, against "2 starters" in §12.2; this is a deliberate deviation. `hashes.json` was regenerated.
- **Library lint codes (MAJOR 6).**
  - **Registration:** 31 `lint_*` codes are pre-registered in `contracts/issues.py:_LIBRARY_LINT_CODES`, all owned by `library/lint.py`. Each has the same severity for both study kinds; `lint_prose_endpoint_unmentioned` also allows `error`. They cover:
    - any kind: content invalid, unknown or wrong-kind library ref;
    - tasks: unknown feature, surface, endpoint or entity ref; duplicate id; owner entity missing or not owned; auth flow missing; prose variant missing; unmentioned endpoint; forbidden phrase; too long; unstated check;
    - prompts: template syntax; undeclared, unused or unknown variable; prompt-set role missing; role or mode mismatch;
    - stacks, styles and guidance: fragment role, guidance fragment missing, unknown style frontend;
    - starters: forbidden content, security library, unpinned image, missing dockerfile or lockfile, stack mismatch.
  - **WP-03 ownership:** WP-03 owns `_LIBRARY_LINT_CODES`. It may add `lint_*` codes there, recording them in this file, without a WP-01 round trip. Codes outside the `lint_` namespace stay WP-01's. `editor_harness_agent_brief` keeps its spec name.
  - **Test exemption:** `test_every_registered_code_is_named_in_the_spec_or_documented` exempts the `lint_` namespace.
- **Not addressed in this round:** the 10 MINORs, except MINOR 4 (bulk updates). They remain open for the next round or the lead: `usable` derivation, seed/identity freeze, `RunFile.mode` bound, harness char limits, digest fields, `ManifestEndpoint.id` nullability, the duplicate analysis plan, the two sign-offs, and `RunView`'s private field.

### WP-01 fixer, review round 2 (MAJOR: pinned prompt fragments), 2026-11-03

- **Contract change: `PromptSetContent.fragments`** (section 3.4; resolves the spec's "the fragment `auth-contract-<mode>@v` that the PromptSet's context selects", sections 3.5 and 9.2).
  - **Field:** `fragments: dict[FragmentKey, VersionRef] = {}`. Each value is a pinned PromptTemplate ref (role `fragment`).
  - **Closed key set:** `auth_contract.{contract_bearer,cookie_session,spec_defined}`, `network.{none,packages}` and `budget_neutral`. Unknown keys fail validation.
  - **Helpers:** `contracts.library.auth_fragment_key(mode)` and `network_fragment_key(policy)` map a factor level to its key. `AUTH_MODES`, `NETWORK_POLICIES` and `APP_REQUIRED_FRAGMENT_KEYS` are exported. A test pins `AUTH_MODES` to `choices.AuthMode` and `NETWORK_POLICIES` to `ResourcePolicyContent.network_policy`.
  - **Pinning:** `LIBRARY_REF_FIELDS["prompt_set"]` gains `("fragments.*", "prompt")`, so `pin_content_refs` and `VersionedEntity.derive_fields` pin fragment refs the same way as `roles.*`. Ref locations read `fragments.<key>`; for example, `fragments.network.none`.
  - **Renderer rule (WP-03):** `auth_contract`, `network.sentence` and `budget_neutral` render the fragment that the run's prompt set names for the cell's `auth_mode` / `network_policy`. An absent `budget_neutral` renders `""`. There is never a latest-by-slug lookup (A.1).
  - **Consequence:** `prompt_set.content_hash`, and through it `treatment_hash` / `config_fingerprint`, now commit to the fragment text. Editing a fragment means a new template version, a new pinned ref and a new prompt-set version. A test asserts that both keys change.
- **Lint (`library/lint.py`).**
  - **New function:** `prompt_set_issues(content)` covers content validity, the roles the mode requires (app: `brief`; slot: both `slot_single_shot_*` or `slot_agent_brief`) and the fragment slots.
  - **New code:** `lint_prompt_fragment_missing` (error), one issue per missing key, with `data.key` and `path=fragments.<key>`. App-mode sets must define every `auth_contract.*` and `network.*` key. `budget_neutral` is optional, and slot sets need no fragments.
  - **Dispatch:** `lint()` dispatches `prompt_set` to this function. Every other kind still raises `NotImplementedError` for WP-03.
  - **Left for WP-03:** the ref-resolving prompt-set rules (`lint_prompt_role_mismatch`, `lint_prompt_mode_mismatch`, `lint_fragment_role` on `fragments.*`, `lint_unknown_library_ref`), added into the same function.
- **Overrides.**
  - **`OVERRIDE_SWAPS`:** `contracts.library.OVERRIDE_SWAPS` maps each `owner_kind` to (kinds forked, innermost first; the factor swapped):
    - `auth_fragment` forks `prompt` and then `prompt_set`, and swaps the `prompt_set` level, because `auth_mode` is an enum and has no level to swap.
    - `prompt` follows the same path.
    - `stack`, `style` and `guidance` fork their fragment template and the owning entity, and swap `stack_profile` / `style` / `guidance`.
  - **`swap_prompt_set_fragment(content, key, new_ref)`:** builds the forked prompt-set content. It refuses unknown keys and keys the set does not define.
  - **`StudyPromptOverride`:** now a `ValidatedModel`. This is an abstract base, so the migration is unchanged.
    - Both owner refs must be pinned and must differ.
    - For `auth_fragment`, `role` holds the auth mode. The 28-character fragment key does not fit `role` Char(24); it is exposed as `fragment_key`. `swapped_factor` reads `OVERRIDE_SWAPS`.
  - **Left for WP-03:** `library/service.fork_for_override` implements the forks from `OVERRIDE_SWAPS`.
- **Golden fixture.**
  - **New prompts:** `auth-contract-{contract_bearer,cookie_session,spec_defined}`, `network-none`, `network-packages` and `budget-neutral` (texts from sections 8.4 and 4.1). `app-default` pins all six.
  - **Brief:** `brief-app-default` now declares and uses `auth_contract`, `network` and `budget_neutral`, in the section 11.2 order.
  - **Hashes:** `hashes.json` was regenerated. Every prompt-set hash changed (the `fragments` default joins the normalized content), as did `prompt:brief-app-default`; the six new prompt hashes were added. No other hash changed.
  - **Builder:** `testing.content.prompt_set_content()` fills the five required app fragments with fake pinned refs (`app_fragments()`) so the samples lint clean. Pass `fragments=` to override this.
- **Spec text to amend (lead):** add the `fragments` line to the section 3.4 `PromptSetContent` block, and add `lint_prompt_fragment_missing` to the lint list.

### WP-01 fixer, review round 3 (2 MAJORs + minors), 2026-11-03

Files: `models/{study,run}.py`, `contracts/plan.py`, `runs/view.py`, `migrations/0024_v3_models.py` (edited in place), tests `tests/wp01_foundation/test_review_round3.py` (new), `test_models.py`, `test_contracts.py`.

- **Pilot and main cells no longer collide (deviation from §3.6).** `StudyCell` is unique on `(study, condition, unit, stage, repeat_index)`, not `(study, condition, unit, repeat_index)`: pilot repeats are numbered from 0 like main repeats. `contracts.plan.cell_key(c, u, r, stage)` now takes the stage and returns `c<c>-u<u>-r<r>-<stage>` (`stage` ∈ pilot/main); `ResolvedCell` validates that its `cell_key` matches its indices and stage. `plan_hash` already sorted cell rows by stage, so it is unchanged. **Spec text to amend (lead):** §3.6 constraint line and the §9.4 `cell_key` path parameter.
  - **WP-10, `reuse_as_main`:** `StudyCell.stage` stays mutable, so a reused pilot cell is flipped `pilot → main` in place (keeping its runs, seed and `repeat_index=0`). The expand step must then **not** create the main cells whose `repeat_index` the reused pilot cells take (create main `r ≥ n_pilot_repeats` only); flipping onto an existing main row raises `IntegrityError` (tested). The flip also changes the cell's `cell_key`; any `prompt_hashes`/UI references to the pilot key must be re-keyed or resolved by cell id.
  - **WP-10, seeds:** `crn_seed` does not take the stage, so pilot r=0 and main r=0 of the same (condition, unit) share the CRN seed and therefore the same `run_fingerprint`. That is what makes `reuse_as_main` sound, but when the pilot is **not** reused (confirmatory, or a changed plan) the main r=0 run is a replicate of the pilot run with an identical fingerprint. Results must never pool pilot and main rows (they already filter `stage="main"`), and run-level dedup by `run_fingerprint` must include the stage/plan.
- **The confirmatory lock cannot be removed.** `Study.LOCKED_FIELDS` = design, design_hash, the design mirrors (`kind`, `mode`, `repeats`, `base_seed`, `order_seed`, `scheduling`), analysis_plan, analysis_plan_hash, prereg_hash, resource_policy, **locked_at** and **active_plan**. Once the stored `locked_at` is set, `save()` refuses any change to them, so `locked_at=None` + save is refused before the design can be edited. Locking itself (setting `locked_at`, the hashes and `active_plan` in one save while the stored lock is NULL) is allowed. New `StudyQuerySet` (`Study.objects`) refuses `update()` of any `LOCKED_FIELDS` column when the queryset matches a locked row. Raw SQL is still unguarded. Consequence for WP-10: a confirmatory study's active plan is fixed at launch (top-ups/retries must stay on it).
- **Run columns pinned by the manifest (Appendix A #13).** `MANIFEST_FROZEN_FIELDS` = kind, protocol, seed and the five identity columns. Once a `RunManifest` exists, `GenerationRun.save()` lets each only stay as stored or go to the manifest's value (`manifest.identity.*`, `manifest.sampling.seed`, `manifest.kind/protocol`), i.e. write-once from empty; `GenerationRunQuerySet.update()` refuses them outright on runs with a manifest (use `save()`). `RunManifest.save()` (create) now runs the run/manifest agreement check that was only in `clean()`: kind and protocol must be equal, identity columns and seed must be equal where the run already holds a value. One extra query per run `save()` that touches those columns (none for `update_fields` without them). The treatment FK columns (model, task_version, ...) "copied from the manifest" are **not** pinned yet → WP-08 (open).
- **`GenerationRun.usable` is derived** in `derive_fields`: `outcome in USABLE_OUTCOMES` when `outcome` is set, else `None` (a supplied value is overwritten, not compared, because the outcome legitimately changes during the run's life). `DERIVED_FROM = {"outcome": ("usable",)}`, so `save(update_fields=["outcome"])` writes it too. `QuerySet.update(outcome=...)` bypasses it, as for every derived column: WP-08/WP-09 set the outcome through `save()`.
- **`RunView._run`:** `RunView` gains `_run: GenerationRun = field(repr=False, compare=False)` as its last field, so WP-02a's accessors can reach the row. `run_view()` must pass it.
- **`EvalBaseline.clean`:** `reference_run` must be a `kind=reference, reference_of=starter_baseline` run of the baseline's task version (minor 8, fixed).
- **WP-00 asks verified:** `0023_v3_wipe_guard` skips `is_default=true` requirement templates (and `is_system` seed rows) and has `reverse_code=noop`; `0024` round-trips to 0022 (`test_migrations.py`). Nothing further needed.

Freeze them (keeping `stage`, `state`, `current_run`, money and `paused` mutable) when the resolver lands. | WP-10 |

## U1 decisions (runview: WP-02a RunView + build seam), 2026-11-03

Files: `generation/runs/view.py` (implemented), `generation/build/context.py` (`prepare_build_dir` for app runs and file-level slot overlay, `build_identity`), `runtime/services/sample_builds.py` (import line, `_context_key`, `healing="none"`, `GenerationJob` type hints -> `GenerationRun`), tests `generation/tests/u1_runview/test_{run_view,build_context}.py`.

- **API additions beyond 3.11 (additive):** `RunView.files(scope) -> list[FileEntry]` (path, origin, blob, size, mode, language, loc, is_test, excluded, run_id), sorted by path. This is what export `files.csv` and analysis origin tagging should read: overlaid parent rows carry `origin="parent"` (`view.PARENT_ORIGIN`) and the parent's `run_id`. `RunView.parent_run_id`. `file_rows(scope)` returns a real `RunFile` queryset annotated with `view_origin` (`parent` for overlaid rows) and ordered by path under collation "C", the same order as `files()`. Also exported: `source_loc(path, text)` and `current_runs` (the temporary alias for `_current_runs_tmp`).
- **`loc(scope)` returns `int | None`:** `None` when the run has no collected tree (no `tree_hash` and no `RunFile` rows), never a silent 0. It sums the stored `RunFile.loc`, but only for rows with `excluded=''`, `is_test=False`, `is_source_file(path)` and `not is_test_path(path)` (the reports rules). Tests, lockfiles and manifests therefore count 0 even when their stored loc is wrong.
- **Scopes:** `model` = own rows with origin authored/modified/slot and `excluded=''` (test files are included; only `loc()` drops them). `slot` = own `slot` rows. `app` = parent's non-artifact, non-deleted rows marked `parent`, with own rows overlaid (own artifact/deleted rows remove the path); excluded rows (lockfile, vendored, binary, ...) stay in the tree. `code_files()` leaves out `excluded=binary` rows (they have no text; other blobs decode as UTF-8 with `errors="replace"`). A missing blob raises `BlobNotFoundError`. A variant's overlay is one level deep, because a parent is always an app run.
- **Bulk reader:** `run_views_code(runs, scope)` makes one `RunFile` query (parents included for `app`) and reads each distinct blob once. Every run is a key, and a run without files maps to `{}`, which the caller must report as not_measured.
- **`run_views(qs)`:** one query (select_related for model, task, stack, starter, style, guidance, harness/prompt/sampling/resource presets, cell->unit, manifest, parent stack/starter). `manifest()`, `labels()`, `task`, `stack` then trigger no further queries. A slot variant takes its `stack` and `auth_mode` from the parent when its own columns are empty.
- **`latest_evaluation(suite_ref=None)`:** latest row of any status, retests excluded. Without `suite_ref` it uses the manifest's `planned_evaluation.eval_suite.ref`, or the latest of any suite when there is no manifest. `suite_ref` accepts `slug@v` or a pinned ref.
- **`labels()`:** manifest `factors` (ref with `#hash` stripped, or value), completed from the run columns (model -> `model_id`, library FKs -> `slug@v`, stack, auth_mode, prose_variant).
- **`task_dict()` keys:** ref, pinned_ref, slug, version, variant, title, summary, category, features, surfaces, structured_hash, task_hash, prose (the chosen variant's text), structured (content without prose). `None` without a task version.
- **`_current_runs_tmp(study, stage="main")`:** per cell (`cell__stage=stage`, `beyond_plan=False`, ordered by cell_attempt then created_at), the first usable run, else the latest attempt (so attrition stays visible; consumers filter `usable`).
- **TEMPORARY reports import:** `reports/services/__init__` still imports the v2 generators. While it does, `view._reports_loc()` loads `reports/services/loc.py` straight from its file (same code, rules not copied). Once WP-02b fixes the package import, the normal import path is taken, and `source_loc` delegates to `reports.services.loc.source_loc` once it exists.
- **`prepare_build_dir`:** writes `files("app")` verbatim, with modes normalised to 0644/0755. Variants get the parent tree plus their slot files; span splicing stays WP-05. It also writes `ResolvedLock.files` from `resolved_lock_blob` (the parent's for a variant) and records notes in the report. It refuses unsafe paths, and raises `ValueError` for a run without a collected tree. Allowed-base FROM checks are still WP-05.
- **`build_identity`** = `{"protocol", "stack": pinned_ref|None, "starter": tree_hash|None, "lock": sha|None}`, inherited from the parent for variants. `_context_key` hashes it under the key `"build"`; `"healing"` is the constant `"none"`, and the `healing` kwarg is kept but ignored.

The v2 per-run trajectory endpoints are gone: `/runs/{id}/trajectory/` and `/runs/{id}/calls/` are WP-08's.

Nothing calls `schedule_for_run` yet: **WP-08** should call it after a run's generation stage (on commit; it is off under tests).
- **`sampling.py`** keeps only `layered_llm_params` (the v2 template-mode defaults and `RunPreset` lookup are gone). **`code_profile(run)`** reads `run_view(run)` (`code_files`/`file_rows("app")`, `view_origin`), counts LOC on the model scope (`loc`) and app scope (`loc_app`), compares manifests with the run's `Starter` files (blobs) and reads resolved versions from `resolved_lock_blob`; a run without files reports `None` everywhere.

Within ported files: the orchestrator/served-summary/AgentRunner tests, the `backfill_call_telemetry` command tests, the profile-snapshot tests in `test_llm_params.py`, and the variability payload tests (they test `statistics.services.variability`, WP-02b's).

- **Lead (pyproject.toml)**: add `backend/generation/_legacy/` to the mypy `exclude` (its modules import deleted code) and drop the stale `lint.per-file-ignores."backend/runtime/scaffolding/**"` entry.
