## WP-04 decisions (seed content layout, published interface), 2026-11-03

`backend/generation/data/` follows the golden library layout (`testing/golden_library/`, its loader is the reference reader), so one reader serves both:

- **Prompts:** `prompts/<slug>.jinja2` + `prompts/<slug>.meta.yaml` `{name, description?, role, modes, variables, tags?}`. The template is the `.jinja2` text with one trailing `\n` removed. Engine is always `jinja2-sandbox@1`.
- **YAML kinds** (`styles/`, `guidance/`, `prompt_sets/`, `harness_presets/`, `sampling_presets/`, `eval_suites/`): `{name, description, content, is_archived?: bool}`. `is_archived` is a **row** flag (not content): the 5 harness presets of adapters that are not enabled yet ship `is_archived: true` (11.6). Refs inside content are written `slug@1` and pinned at seed time (`pin_content_refs`).
- **Tasks:** `tasks/<slug>/task.yaml` = `TaskSpecContent` minus `prose`; `TaskSpecVersion.name = title`. `tasks/<slug>/prose/{minimal,standard,exhaustive}.md` (one trailing `\n` removed). `tasks/<slug>/reference/<stack_slug>/` is an oracle tree plus `PROVENANCE.md` (never seeded as content).
- **Not library entities** (the seeder must skip them): `tasks/vocab.yaml`, `tasks/schema.json`, `tasks/*/reference/**`, `styles/tones/*.yaml` (a tone catalogue `{name, description, tone_text}` for the style editor; seeded styles copy `tone` + `tone_text` into `StyleContent`), `data/README.md`.
- **`tasks/vocab.yaml`** (read by WP-03 lint): `{version: 1, features: {<key>: {description}}, surfaces: {cwe-<n>: {name}}, probe_requires: {<probe_kind>: {features_any: [...], surfaces_any: [...]}}}`. `features` is the closed 11.3 list; `surfaces` holds the 60 allowed CWE ids.
- **Endpoint mention rule** the content is written to: every endpoint appears in the standard and exhaustive prose as the literal `` `METHOD /path` `` with the same `{param}` placeholders as `task.yaml`. The minimal prose lists every endpoint as `METHOD /path` too (≤ 120 words, counted as whitespace-separated tokens).
- **Context variables the content uses** (spec 9.2 names, not the golden fixture's `stack_fragment`/`style_fragment`): `task.*`, `stack.fragment_text`, `stack.run_contract`, `stack.backend`, `stack.frontend`, `starter.level`, `style.fragment_text`, `style.tone_text`, `style.library`, `auth_contract`, `guidance.fragment_text`, `guidance.cwes`, `guidance.prescriptions`, `network.sentence`, `allowed_bases`, `budget_neutral`, `harness.agentic`, `slot.*` (7.5).
- **Slot sampling default:** `PromptSetContent` has no sampling field, so "slot PromptSets default to `t07`" is a builder/plan default (WP-10 resolver, WP-15 builder), not seed content.
- `data/agent/harness_prompts/opencode-1.18.35-default.md` is a v2 leftover inside `data/`; WP-04 leaves it for WP-07 to move to `harnesses/captured/` (5.2 rule 6) and then deletes it on request.

## WP-04 → WP-03: render fragments with the app context; extend `task` in the context

1. **Fragments are templates rendered with the same context as the brief** (their `variables` are declared and lint-checked like any template), and the rendered text is what lands in `stack.fragment_text`, `style.fragment_text`, `guidance.fragment_text`, `auth_contract`, `network.sentence` and `budget_neutral`. The seed fragments need it: `auth-contract-*` mention the admin account only when `"admin" in task.features` (the uniform-template problem, 11.2) and render nothing when the task has no `auth` feature; `auth-contract-spec_defined` renders the task's `auth_flow`; `style-*` render `style.tone_text`; `guidance-security-cwe` lists `guidance.cwes`; `guidance-libraries-*` list `guidance.prescriptions`. A fragment never uses another fragment's text.
2. **Add to the `task` context dict:** `auth_flow` (the by-alias dict, `register` key, or `None`), `has_frontend`, `surfaces` and `category`. `auth-contract-spec_defined` reads `task.auth_flow` and `task.endpoints` (to print method + path of the flow's endpoint ids).
3. **Seeder:** read the layout above (WP-04 decisions); `is_archived` from the wrapper; skip the non-entity files listed there.
4. **Lint:** prose rules as written in the WP-04 decisions (literal `` `METHOD /path` ``; minimal ≤ 120 whitespace tokens; forbidden phrases `evaluat`, `test suite`, `graded`, `admin/admin`, `default password`, case-insensitive). Until WP-03 lint lands, `tests/wp04_content/` runs an equivalent fallback and switches to `library.lint.lint()` when it stops raising `NotImplementedError`.

## WP-04 → WP-07: harness preset values

`data/harness_presets/<harness>-{native,controlled}.yaml` are seeded from 5.3/5.5 (pins from the 5.3 table; controlled = 300 s command timeout, web tools denied, subagents off, `pin_system_prompt` where the harness supports a pinned prompt; `max_turns` left unset so the policy's `run.max_turns` maps). goose, qwen-code, claude-code, codex and gemini-cli ship `is_archived: true`. WP-04 agrees that WP-07 edits these 16 files directly (tool names, `options` keys from `option_fields`, version bumps); please note each change here.

## WP-04 → WP-09: eval-v2 image digests

`data/eval_suites/eval-v2.yaml` carries the 11.6 values (tester 1, compiler 1, adherence 1, probes `task`, baseline `starter`) with an all-zero placeholder `image_digest` for both images. The real digests exist only after WP-09 builds `eval-tester` and `adherence-checker`; a zero digest can never match a built image, so `ensure_images` refuses it (`image_digest_mismatch`) instead of evaluating on an unknown image. WP-09 may edit the file directly when the images are pinned (a new content hash, so a new suite version on re-seed).

## WP-09 decisions (partial, wave 2), 2026-11-03

- **`evaluation.credentials(run) -> {admin_username, admin_password, secret_key}`** is implemented (spec 8.6). It reads `stages.start.detail.credentials_ref` of the run's latest non-retest `RunEvaluation`. That value is the sha of a blob holding the Fernet ciphertext (`backend.common.crypto`) of the record. A missing or unreadable record raises **`evaluation.CredentialsUnavailableError`** (a `LookupError`), never a default password. Consumers (human_eval portal, DAST, `container_service._do_build`) should catch it, as well as `NotImplementedError`. The helpers `new_credentials()` and `store_credentials(record) -> ref` are public for the start stage.
- **`levels.stages_for(spec)`** returns these keys: `lock, build, functional, probes, adherence_static, adherence, baseline, analysis_static, analysis_dynamic`. It applies the 4.8 table, then the overrides, then the dependencies (no build means no functional/probes/baseline/DAST/runtime adherence). `levels.not_measured_stages(spec)` lists the skipped stages so results can label them "not measured".
- **`reuse.find_reusable`** matches on: a `measured` row whose result says `measured: true`, the same `tree_hash`, the same `resolved_lock_blob`, the same suite row, no retest, and an equal check selection. The selection is read from `stages.tester.detail.only_checks`, which the suite must write.
- **`retest.{select_for_retest(study, fraction), retest_agreement(study), cohen_kappa(pairs)}`**: the rates are `None` (never 0) when nothing was compared. Flaky checks and checks that are neither pass nor fail are left out.
- **Not done in this pass (open):** `suite.evaluate`/`run_eval_stage`, `lock.resolve_lock`, `acceptance.compile`, the eval-tester image, probes and probe mutants, `baseline.ensure_baseline`, the adherence checker, `metrics.run_metrics`, the 9.9 API, and the `oracle_validate`/`conformance_kit` commands. Those modules still raise `NotImplementedError`.

## WP-05 decisions (stacks, starters, build context, depcache; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `generation/build/{context,dockerfiles,starters,starter_rules,locks,seeding,depcache}.py`, `generation/data/stacks/*.yaml` (16 phase-A + `free-choice`), `generation/data/prompts/{stack-<slug>,run-contract-free}.{jinja2,meta.yaml}`, `generation/data/starters/**` (fragments, `dockerfiles/`, `compose.yaml`, `base_images.yaml`, `allowed_bases.yaml`, `security_deny.yaml`, `lock/`), `runtime/services/build_context.py`, `management/commands/build_starters.py`, the `depcache` block of `docker-compose.local.yml` + `compose/local/depcache/**`, the WP-05 settings block, `tests/wp05_build/**`, `docs/STACKS.md` (full description there).

- **Data layout** follows the WP-04 / golden reader: `stacks/<slug>.yaml` = `{name, description, content}` (refs written `slug@1`), prompts `<slug>.jinja2` + `.meta.yaml` (role `fragment`, modes `[app, slot]`, tag `stack`). Stack slugs: `{flask,fastapi,django,express}-{react,vue,svelte,none}` and `free-choice`. Starter slugs: `<stack>-<level>`; phase A = 33 starters (16 × bare/minimal + `free-choice-bare`). Phase-B entries (go, spring-boot, postgres variants) are listed in `compose.yaml` with `phase: B` and reported `skipped`.
- **Free choice:** `free-choice.brief_fragment = run-contract-free@1` (declares the variable `allowed_bases`; WP-04's "fragments render with the brief context" rule applies). The free-choice starter is `README.md` only: no Dockerfile, `base_images: []`, `composition.dockerfile_template: "none"`.
- **`build.starters` (published):** `compose(stack_profile, level) -> StarterContent` (stores the file blobs, no DB row); `compose_tree(stack_slug, stack_content, level, *, stack_ref, stack_name=None) -> ComposedStarter` (pure); `seed_starters(*, using="default", stacks=None, created_by=None, dry_run=False) -> [SeedRecord(slug, status, version, reason)]` (status `created|unchanged|skipped|error`; equal hash no-op, else version+1 system row with `derived_from`; user slugs never touched; failing the rules is `error`); `find_starter(stack_profile, level) -> Starter | None` (latest non-archived starter of exactly that profile version); `compose_plan()`, `load_stack_yaml(slug)`.
- **`build.seeding.seed_stack_library(*, using, created_by, dry_run, stacks, with_starters=True)`** seeds WP-05's prompts and profiles with the same rules, then the starters (used by `build_starters --with-stacks`).
- **`build.dockerfiles` (published):** `allowed_bases_record() -> {"sha", "refs"}` (= `RunManifest.allowed_bases`; `sha` = `H([{ref, digest}, ...])` in file order, the `allowed_bases_sha` of `treatment_hash` for free-choice cells, `None` otherwise); `allowed_bases()`, `base_images()`, `parse_from_lines`, `check_bases(text) -> BaseCheck`, `pinned_bases`, `normalize_image`.
- **`build.context` (seam, additive):** `BaseNotAllowedError(ValueError)` with `.reason == "base_not_allowed"` and `.check` (raised by `prepare_build_dir` for a free-choice stack, own or parent's, whose Dockerfile FROM is not allowlisted, ARG-built, absent, or pins another digest; the tree is still written first); `VariantNotAssembledError(ValueError)`; `BuildContextReport.bases` (`BaseCheck` dict, free-choice only) → `to_dict()["bases"]` for `stages.build.detail`; fixed stacks keep `to_dict() == {"notes": [...]}`. All re-exported by the shim `runtime/services/build_context.py` (plus `BASE_NOT_ALLOWED`).
- **`build.starter_rules` (the rule list, day-1 deliverable for WP-03):** `starter_issues(content, files, *, stack=None) -> [Issue]` and `starter_issues_from_blobs(content, *, stack=None, read=blobs.get_bytes)`, emitting exactly the five registered `lint_starter_*` codes (rules in `docs/STACKS.md`); `RULES_VERSION = 1`; deny list `data/starters/security_deny.yaml` (per ecosystem; spec list plus `argon2-cffi`, `bcryptjs`, `spring-boot-starter-security`, golang-jwt `/v4`, `/v5`).
- **Locks:** Python = `<dir>/requirements.lock` (`uv pip compile --generate-hashes`, installed as `pip install -r requirements.txt -c requirements.lock`), npm = `<dir>/package-lock.json` (`npm ci`). Checked in under `data/starters/lock/<fragment>/...` with `lock/index.yaml` (manifest sha, lock sha, image, tool); a stale lock refuses to compose (`build.locks.StaleLockError`). Generated with `manage.py build_starters --lock` (runs in the digest-pinned base image via the Docker API; `--docker-host` / `STARTERS_DOCKER_HOST`, default the apps daemon). Base digests (2026-11-03): `python:3.13-slim@sha256:ae14d4ce…`, `node:24-slim@sha256:c86ecead…`.
- **`build.depcache` (published):** `sandbox_config(*, dependency_cache, network_policy, available=None) -> SandboxDepConfig{env, files, ephemeral_dirs, proxy_hosts, notes, unavailable, volumes=()}` (probes `DEPCACHE_*_URL` with `DEPCACHE_PROBE_TIMEOUT_S` unless `available` is given; note `depcache_unavailable` only when the policy wants caches and some are missing); `endpoints()`, `probe_endpoints()`, `maven_settings_xml()`, constants `CACHE_ENV`, `EPHEMERAL_ROOT="/tmp/llmlab-deps"`, `MAVEN_SETTINGS_PATH="/etc/llmlab/maven-settings.xml"`.
- **Compose:** services `depcache-{npm,pypi,go,maven}` (profile `depcache`, opt-in), internal network `depcache` (`DEPCACHE_SUBNET`, default `172.30.98.0/28`, caches at .2-.5), volumes `depcache_{npm,pypi,go,maven}` mounted by the caches only. Images digest-pinned (verdaccio 7.0.2, athens v0.20.0, reposilite 3.6.6; devpi-server 6.21.0 built on the pinned python base, tag `llmlab/depcache-pypi:local`).
- **Settings (WP-05 block):** `SAMPLE_BUILD_ALLOWED_BASES` (path; empty = the data file), `DEPCACHE_{NPM,PYPI,GOPROXY,MAVEN}_URL`, `DEPCACHE_PROBE_TIMEOUT_S`, `STARTERS_DOCKER_HOST`.
- **Not done (wave 3, seams only):** phase-B profiles/fragments/templates (go, spring-boot, postgres, `full`); the `go`/`maven` lock specs exist (`build.locks.ECOSYSTEMS`, `phase="B"`) and are skipped.

## WP-05 → WP-03: starter lint, seeding order, free-choice context

1. `library/lint.py` kind `starter`: call `build.starter_rules.starter_issues_from_blobs(content, stack=<the profile's content>)` (it returns registered `lint_starter_*` Issues; WP-05 owns the rules and the deny list).
2. `seed_library`: seed prompts before stacks (stack `brief_fragment` refs), then call `build.starters.seed_starters(using=using, created_by=...)` **after** the stacks; do not seed `data/starters/**` as files yourself (composition output is not checked in). `data/stacks/*.yaml` and `data/prompts/{stack-*,run-contract-*}` use your reader's layout.
3. Render context: for a profile with `content.choice_mode == "free"`, set `allowed_bases = build.dockerfiles.allowed_bases_record()["refs"]` (else `[]`), since `run-contract-free@1` renders it; `stack.fragment_text` is that rendered fragment.

## WP-05 → WP-10: free-choice identity and starter lookup

- Treatment: `allowed_bases_sha = build.dockerfiles.allowed_bases_record()["sha"]` for cells whose stack has `choice_mode == "free"`, else `None`; the manifest's `allowed_bases` is the whole record (same condition).
- Starter resolution: `build.starters.find_starter(stack_profile, level)`; `None` → issue `starter_missing` (error). `free-choice` has only `bare`.
- Sandbox resources: max over the study's profiles of `content.resources.{min_memory, min_cpus}` (free-choice is 3g / 2).

## WP-05 → WP-08: sandbox dependency settings

Apply `build.depcache.sandbox_config(dependency_cache=policy.caches.dependency_cache, network_policy=policy.network_policy)` to every agent container: merge `env`, write `files`, mount **nothing** over `ephemeral_dirs` (they live in the container's writable layer; do not add a cache volume), add `notes` to the run's validity notes (`depcache_unavailable`), and pass `proxy_hosts` to the gateway wiring (below).

## WP-05 → WP-06: gateway egress to the caches

The agent reaches the caches only through the gateway sidecar. Please allow forward-proxying (plain `http://` absolute-URI requests and CONNECT) to exactly the `host:port` pairs in `SandboxDepConfig.proxy_hosts` (from `GatewayWiring`/config), in addition to the registry CONNECT allowlist, and nothing else on private ranges.

## WP-05 → WP-09: build seam outcomes and lock file paths

- Catch `build.context.BaseNotAllowedError` around `sample_builds.build_sample` (it propagates from `prepare_build_dir`): outcome `build_fail`, reason `base_not_allowed` (model-attributable, not retried); store `report.to_dict()` (with `bases`) in `stages.build.detail`. `VariantNotAssembledError` is an infra/config problem (`infra_error`).
- Platform lock files (`ResolvedLock.files`) should use the starter conventions so the starter Dockerfiles consume them: `<dir>/requirements.lock` (pip constraints, hashes allowed: verified with pip 26) next to `<dir>/requirements.txt`, and `<dir>/package-lock.json`.
- Starter Dockerfiles install `-c requirements.lock` / `npm ci` when the lock exists and fall back to an unlocked install when it does not, so a stale model lock is the platform lock step's job to detect (a lock whose manifest changed is not "complete").

## WP-05 → lead (apps-dind owner, `compose/local/apps-dind/entrypoint.sh` and the `apps-dind` service): reach the caches

The caches sit on the internal compose network `depcache` (172.30.98.0/28). Nested gateways in apps-dind cannot reach them today (the DOCKER-USER chain rejects 172.16/12 on the uplink). Please (1) attach `apps-dind` to the `depcache` network, and (2) add, before the private-range REJECTs, `iptables -A DOCKER-USER -i llmlab-gwegr -s "$LLM_GATEWAY_EGRESS_SUBNET" -d "${DEPCACHE_SUBNET:-172.30.98.0/28}" -p tcp -m multiport --dports 4873,3141,3000,8080 -j RETURN` (gateway egress network only, like the LLM proxy exception), then reject the rest of that subnet. Until then set no `DEPCACHE_*_URL`; runs record `depcache_unavailable` and use the public registries.

## WP-05 → WP-10 (docs/README.md): index entry

Please link `docs/STACKS.md` (stacks, starters, build context, dependency caches) from `docs/README.md`.

## WP-05 → WP-12: a pure splice hook for building variants

`prepare_build_dir` builds a slot variant from the parent tree plus the variant's `slot` RunFile rows. For a variant that has no rows yet (validation or a build before `assemble` stored them) it calls **`slot.assemble.spliced_files(run) -> dict[str, bytes]`**: pure (no database writes), the full bytes of every file the replacement changes, spliced exactly as `assemble` would store them (7.7: byte offsets, original line endings, trailing newline rule). Please implement it next to `assemble` (and have `assemble` use it, so both always agree). Until it exists, such a variant raises `build.context.VariantNotAssembledError`; tests fake it with `monkeypatch.setattr(assemble, "spliced_files", ...)`.

## WP-05 → WP-03: note on the seeder integration (supersedes item 2 of the earlier WP-05 → WP-03 ask)

`library/seeding.py` already reads `starters/compose.yaml` and calls `build.starters.compose(stack, level)`; that is compatible and enough (equal inputs → equal `content_hash`, phase-B rows deferred). `seed_starters` stays for `build_starters`; both seeders store identical content. Starter names differ (`<stack>-<level>` from your reader vs `"<profile name>, <level>"` from `seed_starters`); names are mutable and unhashed, so either is fine.

## WP-05 → lead / WP-01 (`.gitignore`): `backend/generation/build/` is ignored and was never committed

The Python-template line `build/` in the root `.gitignore` matches `backend/generation/build/`, so the whole package (WP-01's skeleton, WP-02a's U1 `context.py` and now all of WP-05's modules: `__init__, context, depcache, dockerfiles, locks, seeding, starter_rules, starters`) exists only on disk: `git ls-files backend/generation/build` is empty and `git log --all` has no history for it, although commit 58a56e9's code imports it (`runtime/services/build_context.py`, `sample_builds.py`). Please add `!backend/generation/build/` right after `build/` in `.gitignore` (or `git add -f backend/generation/build` at commit time); without it a fresh checkout cannot import the build seam. No other WP-05 path is ignored (checked with `git status --ignored`).

## WP-06 decisions (gateway: protocols, passthrough, budget enforcement), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/images/llm-gateway/{gateway.py,Dockerfile,.dockerignore,protocols/{__init__,budget_rules,common,openai_chat,anthropic_messages,openai_responses,gemini_generate}.py}`, `backend/generation/images/llm-gateway-local/{local.py,Dockerfile,.dockerignore}`, the budget part of `backend/inference/proxy_server.py` (`budget_rules()`, `budget_config_from_manifest`, `ProxyBudgets`, `ProxyHandler._serve`), `config/settings/components/generation.py` (WP-06 block: `LLM_GATEWAY_PING_INTERVAL_S`), `docs/GATEWAY.md`, tests in `backend/generation/tests/wp06_gateway/` (incl. fixtures). Images built once locally: `llmlab-v3-wt/llm-gateway:dev`, `llmlab-v3-wt/llm-gateway-local:dev`. Full details in `docs/GATEWAY.md`.

**Published interfaces (others depend on these):**
- **Startup contract.** Env `GATEWAY_CONFIG` = `GatewayConfig` JSON (`config_version 1`), read with stdlib (no pydantic in the image). Runtime extras the sandbox may add next to the contract keys (never policy): `listen: {port}`, `connect_allowlist: [hosts]`, `proxy_hosts: ["host:port"]`. Env alternatives win when set: `GATEWAY_PORT` (default 8080), `GATEWAY_CONNECT_ALLOWLIST`, `GATEWAY_PROXY_HOSTS` (comma lists), plus `GATEWAY_LOG_DIR` (default `/logs`), `GATEWAY_PING_INTERVAL_S` (default 10), `GATEWAY_RATE_DIR` (shared rpm buckets; unset = per gateway), `GATEWAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES`, `GATEWAY_REFERER`/`GATEWAY_TITLE`, `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`, `LLMLAB_UPSTREAM_TOKEN`. **A config this image cannot run exits with status 78** after a `config_invalid` event in `gateway_events.jsonl` (translate mode, gemini front, passthrough to a local upstream, `serving_tier=batch`, `local_proxy` without the overlay). `GET /healthz` → `{"ok": true, "front_protocol", "version": 3}`; `GET /stats` → state + counters.
- **Fronts served (one per run, from `front_protocol`):** `openai_chat` native (`POST */chat/completions`, `GET */models`); `anthropic_messages` passthrough (`POST */messages[?beta=true]`, local `POST */messages/count_tokens` → `{"input_tokens", "estimate": true}`, `HEAD` anything → 404, Anthropic-shaped `GET */models`); `openai_responses` passthrough (`POST */responses`; `previous_response_id` → native 400). Any other route → native 404 + `rejected_path`. Upstream paths: `{upstream.base_url}/chat/completions|/messages|/responses`. Images/files in a request → 400 `unsupported_content`.
- **`calls.jsonl`** keys are a superset of the WP-01 `FakeGateway`/`canned_call()` keys (pinned by a test). Extras: `seq`/`ts` (v2 aliases for `services/call_telemetry`), `stream`, `attribution` (Claude Code `x-claude-code-*` headers), `requested_reasoning`, `response_id` (generation id for the `/generation` backfill), `client_gone`, `native_finish_reason`, `truncated`, `usage_estimated`, `cost` (what accrued toward the cap), `cost_source` (`usage.cost|price_table|list_price|free|reservation`), `reserved_prompt_tokens`, `upstream_max_tokens`, `upstream_retries`, `rate_wait_s`, `policy_diff`, `native_system_prompt_sha`, `response` (summary with the raw native usage). `usage` is normalized (`prompt_tokens` includes cache reads and writes; `null` = not reported, never 0); `usage.cost` stays `null` when the upstream reported none.
- **`state.json`**: exactly the 13 spec 4.5 keys (== `fake_gateway.STATE_KEYS`), atomically rewritten (tmp + fsync + rename) after every call start and end; `budget_exhausted` is `"budget_exhausted:<kind>"`. Kinds: `requests`, `aux_requests`, **`attempts`** (new: loop guard, calls started ≥ `2 × (max_llm_requests + max_aux_requests) + 20`), `cost`, `tokens`, `uncached_input`, `output_tokens`.
- **`gateway_events.jsonl`** events: `started`, `refused` (every refusal), `budget_exhausted` (once), `context_budget`, `upstream_retry`, `rate_limit_timeout`, `rejected_path`, `rejected_connect`, `connect`, `proxy`, `rejected_proxy`, `config_invalid`, `internal_error`. **`blobs/<identity.text_sha>.txt`** holds the native system prompt of the first `main` call (also inline in that record, as the fake does).
- **Local overlay → proxy headers:** `X-LLMLab-Request-Class` and `X-LLMLab-Call-Index` (stable across gateway retries of one call).
- **Session fixture format** for passthrough smoke runs (see `docs/GATEWAY.md#session-fixture-format`), read from `tests/wp06_gateway/fixtures/passthrough/*.json` and **`tests/wp07_harnesses/fixtures/gateway/*.json`**.

**Decisions:**
- Pre-call reservation = calibrated prompt estimate (`ceil(chars/3.6)` × the largest actual/estimate ratio seen in the run, ≤ 3×) + full upstream `max_tokens`, at list prices; refused, never clamped; sticky exhaustion (same as the fake). A successful call without usage is charged its reservation (`usage_estimated`), never 0. Concurrent in-flight calls cannot overshoot (13.4 test at ledger and HTTP level).
- Budget refusals: native shape, 400, "Run budget exhausted. Stop working and summarise." (tested against context-overflow phrasings). `call.max_input_tokens` refusals are deliberately worded as the provider's context-length error (Anthropic "prompt is too long: N tokens > M maximum", OpenAI `context_length_exceeded`) so harnesses compact; the run continues.
- Passthrough reasoning is expressed in native fields: Anthropic `thinking {enabled, budget_tokens}` / `output_config.effort` (minimal→low, xhigh→high, none→thinking disabled); Responses `reasoning {effort|max_tokens}` keeping the harness's `summary`, `store:false`, `include` += `reasoning.encrypted_content`. `reasoning.exclude` is ignored on both (signatures must replay). Seeds are never added to native bodies (`policy_diff.seed_not_applicable`). Native `anthropic-version`/`anthropic-beta` headers are forwarded.
- `cache_control`: `off` and `auto` strip every marker; `on` keeps harness markers, rewrites their TTL to `caching.ttl`, and injects (system + last tool result, ≤ 4 markers) only when the model vendor or a pinned/ordered provider is in `caching.cache_control_providers`.
- The chat policy keeps the v2 gateway's key order: the opencode / mini-swe-agent / aider recorded sessions replay **byte-equal** (v2 `_rewrite` output at 58a56e9) apart from `session_id` (`test_v2_replay.py`). New on chat: `max_completion_tokens` and harness routing fields are always stripped; harness `seed` is replaced by the policy's.
- `rpm_per_model`: token bucket (burst = 5 s worth), taken after the reservation (the call counts as in flight, so the idle timer pauses), waiting at most `llm_call_timeout_s`; shared across a study's gateways only when `GATEWAY_RATE_DIR` is a shared mount (flock'ed file per `bucket_key`).
- Local overlay: never retries 500/408, always retries 504 (proxy "still waiting"); strips `usage`, `provider`, `session_id`, `:floor` and `cache_control`.
- Proxy parity: `proxy_server.ProxyBudgets` loads `images/llm-gateway/protocols/budget_rules.py` by path, one ledger per attempt, configured from `budget.gateway_config.build(run)` (manifest fallback), keyed per call by `X-LLMLab-Call-Index` (retries/504 re-attach reuse the reservation). Same scripted calls → same exhaustion point and same state (`test_proxy_parity.py`). The ledgers are in the leader executor's memory: a backstop, the gateway stays authoritative.
- WP-05's ask (gateway egress to the caches) is done: plain-HTTP absolute-URI `GET`/`HEAD`/`POST` and `CONNECT` to exactly the `proxy_hosts` `host:port` pairs; nothing else beyond the registry allowlist on 443.
- Wave 3 seams: `to_chat`/`from_chat`/`stream_from_chat` in every front module (raise `TranslatorUnavailableError`); `common.SUPPORTED_MODES` is the switch that enables translate mode; `translation_dropped` is `{}` on every record today.

## WP-06 → WP-08: starting the gateway

- `_start_gateway` already puts `listen` and `connect_allowlist` in `GATEWAY_CONFIG`; both are accepted. Please also pass the dependency-cache pairs as `proxy_hosts` (or env `GATEWAY_PROXY_HOSTS`), `GATEWAY_PING_INTERVAL_S=settings.LLM_GATEWAY_PING_INTERVAL_S`, and (optional) mount one per-study directory as `GATEWAY_RATE_DIR` into the **gateway** container (never the agent) to share the `rpm_per_model` bucket across the study's gateways.
- A gateway that exits with status **78** (`config_invalid` event in `gateway_events.jsonl`) is a config/infra problem: outcome `infra_error` (reason `gateway_config_invalid`), not a model outcome.
- `calls.jsonl` may lag the harness's last answer by a few ms (the record is appended right after the response is sent); read it after the gateway is stopped, as the sandbox does.
- The gateway image context now includes `protocols/`; `agentic/images.py` hashes the whole directory already.

## WP-06 → WP-11: gateway config details

- Set `sampling.forward_seed=false` (and `seed=None`) for `front_protocol in {anthropic_messages, openai_responses}`: neither native API has a seed and the gateway never adds one, so the manifest's `seed_forwarded` should say so.
- `caching.cache_control_providers` is matched against the model vendor prefix (`anthropic/…`) and the pinned/ordered provider names, lower-cased; your `[facts.family]` works.
- Live spend: the `state.json` keys are exactly `fake_gateway.STATE_KEYS`; `budget_exhausted` can also be `budget_exhausted:attempts` (loop guard). Settlement can read `calls.jsonl` `usage.cost` (null when not reported → `/generation` backfill by `response_id`), `list_cost`, `cost_source` and `usage_estimated`.
- `inference/proxy_server.ProxyBudgets` calls `gateway_config.build(run)` once per attempt (first proxied call) and falls back to the manifest when it raises; keep `build` free of side effects.

- `HarnessFacts.request_class_source`: Claude Code `x-claude-code-request-class`; everyone else `default:main`. Unknown header values count as `auxiliary`.
- Smoke-run fixtures for the passthrough check: please record Claude Code and Codex sessions (native request + upstream SSE) as `backend/generation/tests/wp07_harnesses/fixtures/gateway/<harness>.json` in the format of `docs/GATEWAY.md#session-fixture-format`; `wp06_gateway/test_passthrough_sessions.py` replays every file there automatically.

## WP-06 → owner of `backend/inference/tests/test_e2e_relay.py` (WP-02a / lead): v3 gateway config

`test_agent_proxy_path_through_gateway_overlay` starts the overlay with the v2 config keys (`upstream`, `model`, `forced_params`, `port`, `log_path`, …) and now fails at startup (exit 78: `GATEWAY_CONFIG is malformed`). Replace its `config` with a v3 `GatewayConfig` and the env-based runtime keys, e.g.:

```python
from backend.generation.testing.fake_gateway import gateway_config

config = gateway_config(
    run_id=str(job.pk), session_id=str(job.pk),
    upstream={"kind": "local_proxy", "base_url": f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}/v1"},
    model={"upstream_model": model.model_id},
    sampling={"seed": 77}, call={"max_output_tokens": 128}, budget={"max_llm_requests": 5, "cost_basis": "free"},
).model_dump(mode="json")
env = {**os.environ, "GATEWAY_CONFIG": json.dumps(config), "GATEWAY_PORT": str(gateway_port),
       "GATEWAY_LOG_DIR": str(tmp_path), "LLMLAB_UPSTREAM_TOKEN": proxy_token(job), "OPENROUTER_API_KEY": "",
       "PYTHONPATH": str(GATEWAY_DIR), "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}
```

(`calls.jsonl` is then at `tmp_path / "calls.jsonl"`.) The same path is covered in v3 form by `wp06_gateway/test_proxy_parity.py::test_local_overlay_through_the_real_proxy`. Also `inference/timeouts.py:16` cites the v2 `CONFIG.get("upstream_retries", 4)`; it is now `GatewayConfig.retries.upstream_retries` (comment only).

If you want the fake to stay a strict model, add `attempts` to its kinds. No contract change is needed. The U1 ask to freeze `num_ctx`/`keep_alive` for local endpoints in `ManifestEndpoint` stays with WP-01 (the gateway never sends them; the proxy builds the endpoint body).

## WP-04 decisions (seed content, round 2), 2026-11-03

- **Correction to the round-1 note:** the wrapper flag is **`archived: true`** (WP-03's seeder reads `archived`), not `is_archived`. The 10 harness presets of goose, qwen-code, claude-code, codex and gemini-cli carry it.
- **Delivered:** 43 tasks (the 33 v2 tasks rewritten + the 10 of 11.2), each with `minimal`/`standard`/`exhaustive` prose; `tasks/vocab.yaml` (28 features, 60 CWE surfaces, `probe_requires` for all 17 probe kinds); 34 prompt templates (5 app briefs incl. editor and paraphrases A/B/C, 3 slot templates, 3 auth contracts, 11 style, 4 guidance, `budget-neutral`, 2 network); 8 prompt sets (`app-default`, `app-budget-neutral`, `app-editor`, `app-paraphrase-{a,b,c}`, `slot-single-shot-default`, `slot-agent-default`); 19 styles (11 of 11.5 + `model-choice-<tone>` for the 8 tones) and the tone catalogue; 5 guidance entries; 16 harness presets; 4 sampling presets; `eval-v2`. `seed_library` loads all of it with no error and is idempotent; `lint_seed_data` reports no error or warning on WP-04's entries (tests in `generation/tests/wp04_content/`, 441 tests).
- **`app-default` has no `budget_neutral` fragment** (the default brief says nothing about budgets); `app-budget-neutral` adds the 4.1 sentence for studies that want it.
- **Uniform-template fix:** 30 of 43 tasks have no admin surface. No task lists `/api/health` (it is the run contract's), and v2's `is_active` + admin toggle pattern is gone. `search_catalog_sql` has no accounts at all (its brief has no "Accounts" section).
- **Prose is auth-mode neutral:** it says "signs in"; credentials, cookies, CSRF and the admin account come only from the `auth-contract-*` fragments, so the auth mode is a clean factor. `banking_transfers_cookie` is written for `spec_defined` (its `auth_flow` is cookie + `header_from_endpoint` CSRF with `auth.csrf`), but it renders under every mode.
- **Redirect route:** `api_url_shortener`'s `GET /r/{code}` (302) is stated in the rules and covered by hand-written acceptance checks, not listed as an endpoint, because `Endpoint.success_status` is limited to 200-299 (ask to WP-01 below).

## WP-04 → WP-01: allow 3xx `success_status`

`Endpoint.success_status` is `Field(ge=200, le=299)`, so a redirect endpoint (feature `redirect`, cwe-601) cannot be declared. Please widen it to 200-399. When it lands, WP-04 adds `links.follow` (`GET /r/{code}`, 302, `content_type: none`) to `api_url_shortener` as version 2.

## WP-04 → WP-09: conventions the seed acceptance checks and probes use

- **`json_contains`:** recursive subset match; a list in `json_contains` matches when the observed list holds, in the same relative order, one element matching each listed element (an ordered subsequence). The seed sort-order checks depend on the order (`crud_todo_list`, `geolocation_store_list`, `team`/`tasks` reorders).
- **Setup captures:** `capture: {name: "$.json.path"}` saves a value; later steps and the check's own `request.path` substitute it as `{name}` (for example `/api/todos/{done_id}`). `header_contains: {"Retry-After": ""}` means "the header is present".
- **Multipart setup:** `request.files: {"<field>": {"filename", "content", "content_type"}}` (`file_share_multipart`).
- **Actors:** setup steps without `actor` run as the check's actor; `actor: anon` checks run without credentials.
- **Probe `params`:** `param` (the query parameter to attack), `field` (the body field to inject or mass-assign), `view_endpoint` (the endpoint id that reads stored content back, for `xss_stored`).
- **Environment for `webhook_receiver_signed`:** the app reads `WEBHOOK_SECRET` at start; the suite must set it (like `ADMIN_PASSWORD`, 8.6) and give it to the tester, which signs `<X-Timestamp>.<raw body>` with HMAC-SHA256 as `X-Signature: sha256=<hex>`.
- **Outbox for `account_password_reset`:** reset links are read from `GET /api/outbox?to=<email>` (the in-sandbox mail stand-in).
- **References (wave 3):** none written yet. The seam is `tasks/<slug>/reference/<stack_slug>/` with a `Dockerfile` and `PROVENANCE.md` (a test checks the layout); the kit tasks are `crud_todo_list`, `team_roles_multitenant`, `file_share_multipart`.

## WP-07 decisions (harness adapters, images, harnesses API), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/harnesses/{base,registry,api,opencode,mini_swe_agent,aider,claude_code,codex,goose,qwen_code,gemini_cli}.py`, `harnesses/trajectory/{__init__,common,opencode,mini_swe_agent,aider,claude_stream,codex,goose,gemini_cli}.py`, `harnesses/captured/{opencode-1.18.35,codex-0.166.0,qwen-code-0.26.6,goose-1.57.0,claude-code-2.1.316}.md`, `images/agent-{base,opencode,mini-swe-agent,aider,claude-code,codex,goose,qwen-code,gemini-cli}/`, WP-07 settings block, `tests/wp07_harnesses/**` (incl. `smoke/` and `fixtures/`), `docs/HARNESSES.md`. Images built once each, locally: `llmlab-v3-wt/agent-{base,opencode,mini-swe-agent,aider,claude-code,codex,goose,qwen-code,gemini-cli}:dev`. Details and the smoke findings: `docs/HARNESSES.md`.

**Published interfaces:**
- **Ninja operation ids:** `harnesses_list` (`GET /harnesses/`), `harness_facts` (`GET /harnesses/{name}/facts/`, 404 code `harness_unknown`), `generation_config` (`GET /config/`). `/harnesses/` rows: `{name, label, description, protocol, capabilities, enabled, blocked_reason, versions, pinned_version, option_schema, default_options, facts_version, vendor_affinity, min_call_output_tokens, open_questions}`. `/config/` adds `harnesses: [{name, label, enabled}]` to the 9.3 keys; `factor_vocabulary = {keys, app, slot}`; `knob_classes = {C|R|O|S: text}`; `limits.sandbox_max = {memory, nano_cpus}`.
- **`harnesses.registry`:** `get(name)` (KeyError), `adapters()`, `entries()` (`RegistryEntry{adapter, enabled, blocked_reason}`), `entry`, `is_enabled`, `enabled_adapters`, `require_enabled` (ValueError), `DEFAULT_HARNESS = "opencode"`, and **`plan_issues(harness, *, model_slug, endpoint_kind="openrouter", max_output_tokens=None, model_supports_tools=None, study_kind, cells)`** → `harness_not_enabled`, `harness_protocol_unavailable_for_endpoint`, `output_cap_below_harness_min`, `model_lacks_tools`, `vendor_affinity`. Enabled = `wants_enabled` and no `facts.open_questions` and not in `settings.AGENT_HARNESSES_DISABLED`; registering an adapter with `wants_enabled=True` and open questions raises `HarnessRegistryError`.
- **`harnesses.base`:** `HarnessAdapter` (spec ABC + `version()`, `option_schema()`, `default_options()`, `effective_options()`, `protocol_mode(endpoint_kind=, translators=)`, `unsupported_knobs(ctx)`, `render_record(rendered)` → `{config_files_sha, command_sha}` for `ManifestHarness` (brief and `brief_copies` excluded), `parser()` → incremental parser, `to_dict()`, class attrs `versions`, `image_dir`, `version_build_arg`, `lock_files`, `wants_enabled`, `blocked_reason`, `brief_copies`); `default_termination(exit, traj, facts)` (the 5.1 rules; every adapter inherits it); `knobs(ctx)`; `OptionField` / `normalize_options` (ported); `captured_prompt(name, version)` (`CapturedPromptMissingError`); **`sandbox_payload(rendered, *, wall_clock_s, max_workspace_bytes=None) -> SandboxPayload{files, env, entrypoint=("sh", "/run/entry.sh")}`** and `entry_script(...)` (6.5 steps 1-5).
- **Sandbox layout:** `/workspace`, `/run/brief.md`, `/run/harness/` (rendered configs, `command.sh`), `/run/home` (fresh `HOME`, XDG inside; also baked into `agent-base` as `ENV HOME`), `/logs` (`harness.out`, `harness.err`, `exit.json {exit_code, started, ended, workspace_bytes, workspace_too_large}`, `changes.patch`, `name_status.txt`, `workspace.tar` or `workspace.manifest`, `.idle_mark`, plus harness artifacts such as `traj.json`, `aider.chat.md`). `parse_trajectory(outputs, calls)` takes `outputs` keyed by **basename** under `/logs`.
- **Fixtures:** smoke-run fixtures `tests/wp07_harnesses/fixtures/<name>-<version>/{harness.out|traj.json, calls.jsonl, exit.json, name_status.txt, command.sh, env.json}` for opencode, mini-swe-agent, aider, goose, claude-code, codex, qwen-code (+ `claude-code-2.1.316-max-turns`, `qwen-code-0.26.6-max-turns`); **WP-06 session fixtures** `tests/wp07_harnesses/fixtures/gateway/{claude-code,codex}.json` (real native requests from the pinned binaries; `wp06_gateway/test_passthrough_sessions.py` passes with them).

**Decisions:**
- Turn cap = the stricter of `preset.max_turns` and `run.max_turns`; command timeout = `preset.command_timeout_s` else `time.command_timeout_s`; mini `step_limit = min(max_turns, max_llm_requests)`.
- `observed_inputs` is pure: `native_system_prompt_blob = identity.text_sha(prompt)`, i.e. what `blobs.put_text(common.native_system_prompt_text(calls))` returns. The gateway's `native_system_prompt` (inline) or `native_system_prompt_blob` is used when present.
- Every adapter's `classify_termination` is the base rule; documented exit codes map to Termination values (registry-checked): qwen 53 → `max_turns` (verified), 55 → `budget_exhausted` (`--max-wall-time` is never passed).
- **Enablement (wave 2):** opencode, mini-swe-agent, aider enabled. goose (open question: built-in `web-search` skill + `load_skill` cannot be switched off) and qwen-code (open question: usage statistics only switchable in settings.json, which `--bare` ignores) are verified offline but blocked; claude-code and codex are blocked until WP-06 passthrough is verified against OpenRouter and a real-model smoke run is logged; gemini-cli is blocked for wave 3. No real-model smoke run was possible here (no paid calls); all seven runnable adapters passed a scripted-upstream smoke run in their real images on an `--internal` network.
- **Spec deviations found by the smoke runs** (please amend 5.3, lead): Claude Code `--bare` → minimal SDK prompt + only `Bash`/`Edit`/`Read`, auth only via `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (the adapter sets that, not `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN`); no `x-claude-code-request-class` header on main calls; Qwen `--bare` ignores `settings.json` (so output/context/compaction/caching knobs are unsupported for qwen and the settings file is not rendered); Goose v1.57.0 has no `GOOSE_CONTEXT_STRATEGY` (uses `GOOSE_AUTO_COMPACT_THRESHOLD` + `GOOSE_CONTEXT_LIMIT`); Codex with unknown slugs gets no `apply_patch` tool (no config key forces it) and keeps listing bundled skills; Codex features `multi_agent`, `goals`, `image_generation`, `apps`, `plugins`, browser/computer use, hooks and `unbounded_connection_retries` are switched off in `[features]`.
- The goose pin is written `1.57.0` (settings, preset YAMLs, captured/fixture names); the Dockerfile downloads tag `v${GOOSE_VERSION}`.
- Preset YAML edits (WP-04 agreed): `goose-{native,controlled}.yaml` keep `harness_version: 1.57.0` (unchanged); **`opencode-native.yaml` gains `subagents: true`** (OpenCode's native `task` tool, v2 parity; controlled keeps it off).

## WP-07 → WP-08: using the adapters in the agentic runner

- Build the context with `contracts.harness.HarnessContext` (`gateway = adapter.gateway_wiring(f"{alias}:{port}")`, `policy = EffectivePolicy`), then `rendered = adapter.render(ctx)` (or `adapter.restrict_paths(ctx)` for slot agents; `None` = allowlist-diff only), `payload = base.sandbox_payload(rendered, wall_clock_s=policy.time.wall_clock_s, max_workspace_bytes=policy.run.max_workspace_bytes)`; put `payload.files` into the container, run `payload.entrypoint` with `payload.env` merged over the sandbox env (WP-05 depcache env etc.). Put `rendered.env` keys last: they own `HOME`/XDG.
- After the run: `outputs = {basename: bytes}` of `/logs/*` (and `rendered.artifacts`), `calls` = `calls.jsonl`; `traj = adapter.parse_trajectory(outputs, calls)`, `obs = adapter.observed_inputs(calls)`, then `blobs.put_text(common.native_system_prompt_text(calls))` (its sha equals `obs.native_system_prompt_blob`), `adapter.classify_termination(ExitInfo(...), traj)` with `exit_code`/elapsed from `/logs/exit.json` (`ended - started`). `exit.json.workspace_too_large=true` → `output_invalid:workspace_too_large`.
- Manifest harness section: `adapter.render_record(rendered)` (`config_files_sha`, `command_sha`), `facts_version`, `unsupported_knobs = adapter.unsupported_knobs(ctx)` (also `unsupported_knob:<key>` notes), `vendor_affinity = facts.vendor_affinity`, `image = adapter.image_spec(version).image`.
- Live steps: `parser = adapter.parser()`, `parser.parse_line(line)` per stdout line → new `Step`s for SSE `trajectory_step`. (mini-swe-agent reports through `traj.json`, not stdout.)

## WP-14 decisions (early announcement: shared library components, api/v3 modules, fixtures), 2026-11-03

Import everything from the barrel **`$lib/components/library`** (`frontend/src/lib/components/library/index.ts`); the files below exist from the start of WP-14's work and keep these props (Svelte 5 runes, `onchange` fires on user edits only, values are replaced, never mutated):

- `PinnedRefBadge { ref: string; kind: LibraryKind; latestVersion?: number; href?: string }` — mono `slug@v`, the hash12 in the tooltip, "latest is vN" when older (`latestVersion`, else looked up in `libraryStore`), links to `/library/{kind}/{slug}?v=n` (or `href`).
- `LibraryRefPicker { kind: LibraryKind; multiple?: boolean; value: string[]; filter?: (i: LibraryItemOut) => boolean; onchange: (refs: string[]) => void; placeholder?: string; disabled?: boolean; id?: string }` — emits **pinned** refs (`slug@v#hash12`); a value pinned to an older version stays selected with "Use latest".
- `ResourcePolicyEditor { value: ResourcePolicyContent; base?: ResourcePolicyContent; knobMeta: KnobMeta[]; warnOnFingerprintChange?: boolean; onchange: (v: ResourcePolicyContent) => void; readonly?: boolean; budgetBasis?: 'usd'|'tokens'|'auto'; keyLimitsOnly?: boolean }` — Key limits always visible, accordion groups (Tokens & context, Routing & caching, Time, Retries, Sandbox, Concurrency, Caches, Study caps & estimate, Stop rules & pilot gates), class badges ("affects treatment" for C/R with tooltip, "study design" for S), inherited/override marks with per-knob reset to `base`, fingerprint warning "new fingerprint — not comparable with earlier runs" when `warnOnFingerprintChange` and a C/R knob differs from `base`. `knobMeta` is `GET /budget/knobs/`.`knobs` (`libraryStore.ensureKnobs()` loads it once). "Save as preset" is the caller's (the editor only edits content).
- `PromptTemplateEditor { value: PromptTemplateContent; base?: PromptTemplateContent | null; onchange; readonly?: boolean; renderContext?: AppContextFrom | null; variables?: PromptVariable[] }` — textarea + variable autocomplete (`GET /prompts/variables/`), live render against a sample context, diff vs base.
- `DetectorEditor { value: DetectorContent; onchange; readonly?: boolean; prefill?: Partial<DetectorContent> | null }` — `prefill` (WP-16's "Create detector from selection") is merged over `value` once on mount.
- `StimulusText { stimulus: StimulusOut; onspan?: (s: SourceSpanOut) => void; activeOwner?: string | null }` — rendered text with source spans highlighted by owner (for WP-15's ResolvedCellDrawer); pure helpers `segmentStimulus`, `stimulusOwners`, `ownerHref`, `OWNER_LABEL` in `library/spans.ts`.
- `VersionDiff { unified?: string; a?: string; b?: string; structured?: DiffEntry[]; labels?: [string, string] }`.
- Stores/helpers: `libraryStore` (`list(kind)`, `pin(kind, ref)`, `latestOf`, `ensureMe`, `ensureKnobs`, `onInvalidate`) and **`invalidateLibrary(kind?)`**, which replaces v2's `invalidatePresets` (call it after creating/versioning any library row, e.g. WP-15's "Save as preset"); `KIND_INFO`/`kindInfo`/`libraryHref`; policy helpers `summaryLine`, `getKnob`, `setKnob`, `overrides`, `fingerprintChanges`, `classBadge`, `groupOf`, `tierLabel` (`library/policy.ts`); `slugify`.

**`frontend/src/lib/api/v3/`** (WP-14): `types.ts` holds the shared core (`Page`, `Issue`, `IssueFix`, `ErrorOut`, `UserRef`, `parseRef`/`shortRef`), every library content type, `LibraryItemOut` & co., prompts (`RenderIn`, `RenderOut`, `StimulusOut`, `SourceSpanOut`, `PromptVariable`), harnesses/config (`HarnessOut`, `GenerationConfigOut`) and the knob types (`ResourcePolicyContent`, `KnobMeta`, `KnobsOut`, `PolicyExplainOut`). WP-15/16/17 append in the marked blocks already at the end of the file (`// --- WP-15 ---` … `// --- end WP-15 ---`, same for 16 and 17). `library.ts` = 9.1 + 9.2 (`listLibrary`, `listAllLibrary`, `getLibraryDetail`, `getLibraryVersion`, `createLibraryItem`, `createLibraryVersion`, `archiveLibraryItem`, `setLibraryVisibility`, `validateLibraryContent`, `getLibraryUsage`, `getLibraryDiff`, `forkLibraryItem`, `getTaskOracle`, `queueTaskOracle`, `getStarterTree`, `getLibraryBlob`, `exportLibraryPackage`, `importLibraryPackage`, `getPromptVariables`, `renderPrompts`, `diffPrompts`) plus the two read-only 9.8 calls the Budget preset editor needs, `getBudgetKnobs()` and `explainPolicy(ref, {model?, harness?, study?, executor?})` (WP-15's `budget.ts` may re-export them rather than duplicate). `harnesses.ts` = 9.3 (`listHarnesses`, `getHarnessFacts`, `getGenerationConfig(fresh?)`, cached).

**Fixtures** (`frontend/src/lib/api/v3/__fixtures__/`, generated from the backend by `backend/generation/tests/wp14_frontend/test_fe_fixtures.py`, which fails on drift; regenerate with `./.wt-test.sh <suf> -- env WP14_WRITE_FIXTURES=1 pytest -q backend/generation/tests/wp14_frontend`): `knobs.json` (`/budget/knobs/`), `variables-app.json`, `variables-slot.json`, `render-app.json` (`/prompts/render/` of the golden library), `task-crud_todo_list.json`, `task-notes_markdown_render.json` (`LibraryItemOut` of the golden tasks), `library-resource-policies.json`, `harnesses.json`, `config.json`. Volatile fields (ids, dates, users) are normalized.

**Routes:** `/library`, `/library/[kind]` (kind = the API name, e.g. `prompt-sets`), `/library/[kind]/new`, `/library/[kind]/[slug]` (`?v=n` read-only old version, `?edit=1` editor, `?diff=a..b`), `/library/prompts/playground`. `/templates` redirects to `/library` (old `?tab=specs|stacks|presets&kind=` links map to the matching kind).

## WP-03 decisions (library services, prompt engine, packages, seeding; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `generation/library/{service,lint,seeding,api}.py`, `generation/library/packages/__init__.py`, `generation/prompts/{engine,context,api}.py`, `management/commands/{seed_library,lint_library}.py`, the WP-03 settings block, `tests/wp03_library/**` (194 tests), `docs/{LIBRARY,TEMPLATE_PACKAGES}.md`, and four `lint_*` codes appended to `contracts/issues.py:_LIBRARY_LINT_CODES`.

- **Operation ids (published):** `library_list`, `library_create`, `library_validate`, `library_detail`, `library_version_get`, `library_version_create`, `library_archive`, `library_visibility`, `library_usage`, `library_diff`, `library_fork`, `library_task_oracle_get`, `library_task_oracle_queue`, `library_starter_tree`, `library_blob`, `library_package_export`, `library_package_import`, `prompts_variables`, `prompts_render`, `prompts_diff`.
- **Additive API shapes (beyond 9.1/9.2):** `LibraryItemOut` adds `tags` (content-derived, what `?tag=` matches), `columns` (the kind's query columns, e.g. `structured_hash`/`prose_hashes` for tasks, `tree_hash`/`stack_profile` for starters, `tier` for budget presets) and `unchanged` (set on `POST .../versions/`). `LibraryCreateIn`/`LibraryVersionIn` take an optional `visibility` (`private`|`shared`). New `POST /library/{kind}/{slug}/visibility/ {visibility}` (every version of a user slug). `POST .../archive/` takes an optional `version` (default: the whole slug). `POST .../fork/` takes optional `version`, `description`. `POST /library/{kind}/validate/` returns `content` (normalized, refs pinned) too. A task's `description` is its `content.summary`. `validate` is a reserved slug (static route).
- **Error codes:** `slug_exists` (409, create/fork), `hash_mismatch` (409, a pinned ref whose hash differs), `stale_base` (409), `permission_denied` (403), `not_found` (404), `invalid` (400/422, with `issues`), `in_use` (409, package import with `on_conflict=error`; the body also has `{mode, created, reused, conflicts}`), `not_implemented` (501, `POST .../oracle/` until WP-09 provides `queue_oracle_runs`).
- **Ownership rules:** a slug belongs to the creator of its latest version; only the owner (or staff) versions a user slug; system slugs are versioned only by the seeder (staff get 403 "fork it"). Lint `error` issues refuse API creates/versions (422 + issues).
- **Lint codes added (all `library/lint.py`):** `lint_starter_blob_missing` (error), `lint_template_render_error` (error; render API failures), `lint_detector_rule_invalid` (error; regex compile / Semgrep YAML), `lint_vocabulary_fallback` (info; `tasks/vocab.yaml` missing). Starter lint = blobs present + `forbidden_content_check.passed` + **WP-05's `build.starter_rules.starter_issues`** (as asked). Statedness: rule table `STATEDNESS_VERSION = 1` (endpoint mentioned as `METHOD /path`, then category keywords / failing status codes); `lint.tag_checks(task_content, checks)` tags compiled checks; an author's explicit `stated_in` on a hand-written check wins. `TaskSpecVersion.derived` (`DERIVED_VERSION = 1`: `difficulty_score = endpoints + 1.5·entities + 0.25·fields + relations + 0.75·features + 0.5·probes`; size S ≤ 4 < M ≤ 8 < L ≤ 14 < XL endpoints) and `lint` are written on every task version.
- **Engine (`prompts.engine`, published):** `render(template_content, context, *, template_ref="draft", owner=None) -> RenderedText(text, sources)`; `render_set(prompt_set, context, *, loader=None, templates=None) -> [Stimulus]` (roles in `ROLE_ORDER`; `templates` = per-role drafts, `template_ref="draft:<role>"`); `Stimulus.{sha, chars, est_tokens, as_dict()}`; `preview_prompt_hash(stimuli)` (Stimulus or `(role, text)` pairs; computed **through `identity.compute_keys`** with placeholder inputs, so there is still one implementation); `find_variables`, `parse_template`, `RenderError(kind=syntax|undefined|security|render)`. Only declared `variables` are passed to a template. No attribute access at all (mapping item lookup only; `loop.*` allowed; `d.items()` is not, iterate keys). Source spans partition the text; sourced values keep their owner through the whitelisted filters when all inputs share one owner. Owner kinds used: `prompt` (template literal text, `network.*`/`budget_neutral` fragments), `task`, `stack`, `style`, `guidance` (their fragment text), `auth_fragment` (owner_ref = the fragment template, role = auth mode), `starter`.
- **Context (`prompts.context`, published):** `build_app_context(...)`, `build_slot_context(...)`, `build_render_context(mode=, **levels)`. Accepted level names include WP-10's/WP-12's aliases `task_version`, `prose_variant`, `stack_profile`, `harness_preset`; slot mode accepts either `slot=` (WP-12's dict) or `slot_target` + `slot_policy` + `resolved_context` + `executor` (then calls `slot.prompt.build_slot_context(target, hole_mode, keep_docstring, context, context_policy, executor)` and defaults task/variant/stack/auth mode from the parent run). WP-04's asks are in: fragments render with the base context (`FRAGMENT_VARIABLES`: task, stack, starter, style, guidance, harness, network, allowed_bases, run_contract, slot); `task` carries `auth_flow`, `has_frontend`, `surfaces`, `category`. Free-choice stacks get `allowed_bases = build.dockerfiles.allowed_bases_record()["refs"]` when the caller passes none (WP-05 ask 3). Aliases for the golden fixture: `stack_fragment`, `style_fragment`, `guidance_fragment`, `run_contract`. Policies contribute `network_policy` only (`resource_policy=` content may be passed; it is checked to agree with `network_policy`).
- **Service (`library.service`, published):** `create(kind, *, slug, name, content, description, user, visibility, system, derived_from, extra, allow_lint_errors)`, `new_version(entity, *, base_version, content, ...)` (returns the base when the hash is unchanged), `archive(entity, *, archived, user, all_versions)`, `set_visibility`, `fork(entity, *, new_slug, name, user)`, `fork_for_override(owner_kind, owner_ref, new_template, *, user, prompt_set_ref=None, role=None) -> OverrideResult{owner_kind, owner_ref_base, owner_ref_new, role, swapped_factor, level_base, level_new (aliases level_ref_base/level_ref_new), diff, created, fields()}`, `usage(entity, *, user)`, `latest(kind, slug, *, user=None)`, `resolve_ref(ref, *, kind=None, user=None)` (`[kind:]slug@v[#hash12]`), `validate_content`, `content_diff`, `content_hash_of`, `pin_refs`, `visible`/`can_view`/`can_edit`, `LibraryError{status_code, code, issues, payload()}`. Override forks: the user's own slug gets a new version (an equal-hash version is reused); anything else is forked to `<slug>--<hash8>` (reused when repeated). Without `prompt_set_ref` the one visible prompt set using the template is taken (else 422); without `role` an auth fragment's mode is inferred from the set.
- **Seeding:** `seed_library(*, using, data_dir=None, kinds=None, dry_run=False) -> {created, unchanged, updated, skipped, deferred, errors, lint_errors, warnings, changes, dry_run}`. Entries with lint errors **are seeded** and reported in `lint_errors` (one bad file must not cascade into its dependents); `--strict` fails on errors, lint errors and skips. Invalid content and missing refs are `errors` (not seeded). System content may not reference user entities. `slug@latest` refs pin the newest system version at seed time (seed files only). Wrapper flag: `archived` or `is_archived`; `name`/`description`/archived are synced on the latest system version. `compose.yaml` entries are composed with `build.starters.compose`; phase-B ones whose stack is not seeded go to `deferred`. `lint_seed_data(data_dir, kinds=None)` lints the files in memory (composed starters included). On the real `data/` tree (2026-11-03): `lint_library` 217 entries, 0 errors; `seed_library` 217 created, 22 deferred, 0 errors, 0 lint errors; second run 0 changes.
- **Packages v4:** see `docs/TEMPLATE_PACKAGES.md` (`kind: llm-lab-library-package`, `package_schema_version: 4`, entities in dependency order with verified `content_hash`, base64 blobs, `omitted`). Import: reuse same slug + hash (or any system row with the hash), new slug → v1, own slug → next version, foreign slug → `<slug>-imported[-n]` (`on_conflict=rename`) or 409 (`on_conflict=error`); refs rewritten to the rows used; `mode=dry_run` (default) rolls back. v1-v3 template packages are refused.
- **Settings (WP-03 block):** `GENERATION_LIBRARY_DATA_DIR` (default `backend/generation/data`), `GENERATION_SEED_ON_MIGRATE` (env, **default False everywhere**; deviation from 11.1's "True in dev and test": a seeded test DB would collide with every suite's factory slugs, so tests seed explicitly; the dev stack should set `GENERATION_SEED_ON_MIGRATE=true` in its env).

The golden `flask-react-minimal` and `fastapi-vue-minimal` starters ship manifests without lockfiles, so WP-05's rule list reports `lint_starter_file_missing` twice for each (they seed, but with lint errors; `tests/wp03_library/test_seeding.py::GOLDEN_STARTER_LINT` pins exactly that). Adding `backend/requirements.lock` and `frontend/package-lock.json` to both (and regenerating `hashes.json`) would make the fixture lint clean; tell me and I drop the exception.
