## WP-03 → WP-10: prompt overrides and draft refs

- `study/overrides.py` calls `fork_for_override(owner_kind, owner_ref, template, user=user)`. Please pass `prompt_set_ref=<the study's prompt_set level>` for `prompt`/`auth_fragment` owners (the fallback finds the set only when exactly one visible set uses the template) and `role=<auth mode>` for `auth_fragment`. `result.level_ref_base` / `level_ref_new` exist as you read them. Store `owner_ref_base=result.owner_ref_base` (always pinned) rather than the raw `owner_ref` (a bare `slug@v` fails `StudyPromptOverride` validation).
- Open follow-up #10 (draft refs in `Design`): pin them with `library.service.resolve_ref(ref, kind=..., user=...)` (404 / 409 `hash_mismatch` are `LibraryError`s) or `service.pin_refs`; `library.lint.lint(kind, content)` is the per-entity lint.
- docs index (`docs/README.md`, `backend/docs/services.py`): add `LIBRARY.md` ("The generation library") and keep `TEMPLATE_PACKAGES.md` ("Library packages (v4)").

## WP-03 → WP-12: one prompt-hash implementation

`prompts.engine.preview_prompt_hash` accepts `(role, text)` pairs; please use it instead of a local `slot.prompt.preview_prompt_hash` (identity keys only through `identity.compute_keys`). `prompts.context.build_slot_context` accepts the keywords `slot/prompt.py` passes (`task_version`, `prose_variant`, `stack_profile`, `auth_mode`, `style`, `guidance`); pass `prompt_set=` too so a slot set's pinned fragments (if any) render.

## WP-03 → WP-09: statedness for compiled checks and the oracle queue

- Tag compiled checks with `library.lint.tag_checks(task_version.content, checks)` (fills `stated_in`); results read `TaskSpecVersion.derived.check_statedness` for the hand-written ones.
- `POST /library/tasks/{slug}/versions/{v}/oracle/` (staff) calls `evaluation.baseline.queue_oracle_runs(task_version, stacks: [StackProfile], *, user) -> list[run id]` (enqueue on commit) and answers 501 until it exists.

## WP-03 → WP-14: TS types

`LibraryItemOut` has the extra fields `tags: string[]`, `columns: Record<string, unknown>`, `unchanged?: boolean | null`; `ValidateOut` has `content`; `ImportOut = {mode, created, reused, conflicts}` with rows `{kind, ref, as, reason?, resolution?}`; `VariableOut` has `modes: ('app'|'slot')[]`; the visibility endpoint and error codes are listed under WP-03 decisions.

## WP-08 decisions (agentic run pipeline, runs API, frontend-validator), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/agentic/{dispatch,runner,sandbox,workspace,collector,manifest,images,outcome,validity,events,evidence,actions,static_checks}.py`, `backend/generation/runs/api.py`, `backend/generation/images/frontend-validator/{Dockerfile,validate.mjs}` (built once: `llmlab-v3-wt/frontend-validator:dev`), `backend/generation/testing/fake_agentic.py` (new fake for this area), the WP-08 settings block, `tests/wp08_agentic/**` (99 tests), `docs/GENERATION_PROCESS.md` (rewritten for v3).

**Published interfaces:**
- **Ninja operation ids** (default naming, module `backend.generation.runs.api`): `backend_generation_runs_api_{list_runs, get_run, get_run_manifest, list_run_files, get_run_file_content, get_run_trajectory, list_run_calls, list_run_evaluations, evaluate_run, retry_run, cancel_run, get_run_evidence}`. For the MCP curation (integrations owner): `list_runs` → `generation_runs_api_list_runs`, `get_run` → `generation_runs_api_get_run`. Error bodies `{"detail", "code"}`; codes: `not_found`, `not_infra`, `run_active`, `no_manifest`, `no_tree`, `no_eval_suite`, `bad_ref`, `eval_suite_not_found`, `binary`, `blob_missing`. List: `{items: [RunListOut], total, page, page_size}` (`page_size` ≤ 200); `agent_pass_rate` is set only when `metrics.eval.status == "measured"` (never 0 for not measured). `GET /runs/{id}/` adds `attempt` (1-based, below), `attempts[]` (`RunAttemptOut`: served, usage, time_split, observed_inputs, `has_trajectory`), `study {study_id, study_name, condition_id, cell_id, unit_key, repeat_index, stage}`, `resolved_lock` (parsed blob or null). `trajectory/` and `calls/` take `?attempt=` (default: the current one). `evaluate/` returns the id of a **pending** `RunEvaluation` it creates. Visibility: staff, the run's creator, or the creator of its study (same as the `run:<id>` channel); everything else is 404.
- **SSE `run:<id>`**: `step` events with steps `resolve`, `images`, `workspace`, `gateway`, `sandbox`, `collect`, `record` (status `started|ok|failed|skipped`, kept in `result.steps` with seconds); `usage` (every `state.json` poll); `trajectory_step` (≤ 2/s); `log` (stage summary). `status` events stay the executor's (`transitions.publish_run`).
- **`agentic.dispatch.run_stage(run, *, backend=None)`**: app_agentic → `runner.run_app_agentic`; slot_agent → `runner.run_agentic(run, slot=restricted_agent.prepare(run))`; slot_single_shot → `single_shot.run_stage` (batch submitter when `BATCH_BACKENDS_VERIFIED` and the effective `routing.serving_tier == "batch"`); reference → `finish_stage(OUTCOME_EVALUATE)`.
- **Attempt numbering:** `GenerationRun.attempt` stays the executor's 0-based requeue counter (the claim never bumps it; `infra_retry` does). The 1-based attempt number is `agentic.runner.attempt_number(run) == run.attempt + 1`, used for `RunAttempt.attempt`, `GenerationArtifact.attempt`, the evidence directory `GENERATION_RUNS_ROOT/<run>/a<n>/` and the `SandboxPlan`. `GatewayConfig.attempt` (PosInt) must be the same number (WP-11 ask below).
- **`agentic.manifest.build_manifest(run, resolved_cell, *, study=None, stimuli=None) -> RunManifest`**: validates `resolved_cell.manifest` (`ManifestBody`), adds the `study` block (derived from `run.cell`/`run.condition`/plan, or passed) and `created_at`, copies the identity keys (never computes one), mirrors onto the run `kind/protocol`, the identity columns, `seed` (`sampling.seed`, else `resolved_cell.seed`), `pinned_served_name`/`pinned_digest`, `prose_variant`, `auth_mode` (factor value), `repeat_index`, `stage`, and **refuses** (`ManifestMismatchError`, a `ValidationError`) any treatment FK the caller set that disagrees with the manifest's pinned refs (stack_profile, style, guidance, prompt_set, harness_preset, sampling_preset, resource_policy, starter, task_version, model slug). This closes WP-01 follow-up 3b in the builder (no model change). Call it inside the launch transaction, then `on_commit(enqueue_runs)`.
- **Images (`agentic/images.py`):** tags `llmlab/<dir>:<sha256[:12] of the dir + build args>`; a harness tag pins its `agent-base` tag (`BASE_IMAGE` build arg), the local gateway overlay pins `llm-gateway` (`GATEWAY_BASE`). `resolve_digest(tag, *, client=None)` returns the image id, or **`pending:<tag>`** when the image does not exist yet (never builds). A manifest digest `pending:<tag>` is accepted once the built image's tag equals `<tag>`; the first build's digest is recorded in `GenerationRun.execution["images"]` and later cells of the same plan must match it. `image_issues({role: digest})` emits `image_built_after_plan` (info). Mismatch → `infra_error:image_digest_mismatch` (not retried, nothing runs).
- **Outcome reasons written by the generation stage:** `output_invalid:no_model_code`, `output_invalid:workspace_too_large`, `output_invalid:<collector invalid_reason>` (slot `edit_violation`), `excluded:resource_limit` (OOM), `excluded:cancelled`, `excluded:harness_not_enabled`, `excluded:config_invalid` (no manifest, no brief stimulus, non-retryable credential errors such as a missing API key), `infra_error:image_digest_mismatch`, `infra_error:gateway_config_invalid` (gateway exit 78), and through `infra_retry` `infra_error:{infra, infra_interrupted, llm_error, harness_crash}` once retries run out. A harness crash is retried once (a repeat, or a policy with no retry left, proceeds to evaluation with note `harness_crash`).
- **Validity notes** written here: truncation kind, `harness_error`, `harness_crash`, `ended_on_llm_error`, `call_truncations:<n>`, `served_model_differs` (vs `manifest.model.endpoint.served_model_name` only), `response_model_differs` (> 1 response model in the attempt), `provider_mixed`, `translation_lossy`, `routing_partial` (a call record with a truthy `routing_partial`), `vendor_affinity` (harness affinity ≠ the model slug's vendor prefix), `unsupported_knob:<k>` (from `manifest.harness.unsupported_knobs`), `call_cap_without_compaction`, `depcache_unavailable` (WP-05 notes). `agentic.validity.add_notes(validity, notes)` merges notes for evaluation (existing codes win).
- **`RunResult.static`**: `python` (AST check: `ok|errors|not_applicable`) and `frontend` (esbuild in `frontend-validator`: `ok|errors|not_applicable|not_measured` + reason). Informational only.
- **Sandbox seam (`agentic/sandbox.py`):** `SandboxBackend` protocol (`ensure_images(manifest, spec, *, local_gateway, run_id) -> ImageSet`, `run(plan, hooks) -> SandboxResult`, optional `gateway_wiring(run, manifest)`, optional `frontend_static_check(rows)`); `DockerSandbox` (production; uses WP-07's `harnesses.base.sandbox_payload` and runs `payload.entrypoint`), `get_backend()` (`settings.GENERATION_SANDBOX_BACKEND`). `Supervisor` + `IdleDetector` are backend-independent (idle = no in-flight call **and** CPU < `AGENT_IDLE_CPU_FRACTION`; unknown facts never count as idle; reset on `last_call_end` and workspace changes). Idle termination sends SIGTERM to the `timeout` process (by `harness.pid`, else a `/proc` scan), so the entry script still writes `changes.patch` and the tar. `reap_orphans(older_than_seconds=0, *, client=None) -> int` (system maintenance's ask: done). The gateway container gets `GATEWAY_CONFIG` (the `GatewayConfig` dump, no extra keys), the credentials env, `GATEWAY_CONNECT_ALLOWLIST` (packages policy), `GATEWAY_PROXY_HOSTS` (WP-05 depcache pairs) and `GATEWAY_PING_INTERVAL_S`. The agent container gets WP-05's depcache env and files (no volume over `ephemeral_dirs`), `proxy_env` for `packages`, `MAVEN_OPTS=-Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/m2`, then the rendered env last.
- **Fake (`testing/fake_agentic.py`):** `LocalSandboxBackend(replies=..., cpu=..., oom_killed=..., mismatch_role=...)` runs the relocatable copy of the entry script (`sandbox.entry_script()`, same `/logs` layout as WP-07's: `harness.out`, `harness.err`, `exit.json`, `changes.patch`, `workspace.tar|workspace.manifest`) under `$LLMLAB_ROOT`, a `FakeGateway` for the attempt's `GatewayConfig`, and the production `Supervisor` on the calling thread.
- **Evidence:** `GENERATION_RUNS_ROOT/<run>/a<n>/` holds every `/logs` file plus `calls.jsonl`, `gateway_events.jsonl`, `state.json`, `workspace.tar` and `trajectory.json`; `RunAttempt.evidence_dir` points there. Settlement (`budget.ledger.settle_attempt(run, attempt=n)`) is called after the evidence and the `RunAttempt` row are written.
- **Settings (WP-08 block):** `GENERATION_SANDBOX_BACKEND`, `AGENT_SUPERVISOR_TICK_S`, `AGENT_IDLE_CPU_FRACTION`, `AGENT_IDLE_CPU_WINDOW_S`, `AGENT_SANDBOX_GRACE_S`, `AGENT_GATEWAY_MEM_LIMIT`, `GENERATION_FRONTEND_VALIDATOR`.

**Decisions:** the stage records an attempt only while it still owns the run (a heartbeat that finds the run requeued or reclaimed raises `LeaseLost`; a cancel records `cancelled`). OOM and LLM/INFRA attempts keep their evidence but write no `RunFile` rows. `RunFile` rows of an attempt replace the run's previous rows. The retry endpoint wipes the attempt's results and `RunFile` rows and requeues on commit; `RunAttempt` rows, artifacts, manifest and seed stay.

## WP-08 → WP-11: gateway config attempt, settlement, live spend, retries

- `budget.gateway_config.build(run)`: set `attempt = run.attempt + 1` (`agentic.runner.attempt_number(run)`) and `session_id = str(run.pk)` when `caching.sticky_session`.
- `budget.ledger.settle_attempt(run, attempt=n)` is called with the 1-based number after `GENERATION_RUNS_ROOT/<run>/a<n>/calls.jsonl` is written (read it there; `agentic.evidence.read_calls(run.pk, n)`). `NotImplementedError` is tolerated.
- Live spend: the supervisor calls **`budget.live.record_live(run, *, attempt: int, state: dict, seq: int)`** on every `state.json` poll (every `SandboxHooks.state_every_s`, 10 s) when that function exists; please implement it (ledger `usage` delta with `dedupe=f"live:{run}:{attempt}:{seq}"`, `study:<id>` `spend` event). WP-08 already publishes the `run:<id>` `usage` event.
- Note on `infra_retry`: with `retries.infra_retries = 1` (frugal) a run gets exactly one requeue (`attempt - lost < infra_retries` on the 0-based counter); the WP-08 tests rely on that.
- `execution/tests/test_executor_loop.py::TestLoop::test_an_unimplemented_stage_leaves_the_run_to_the_lost_run_check` assumes the generation stage is a stub; it now reaches the real `run_stage` (and WP-12's `single_shot.run_stage` for its slot run) and fails. Please patch `run_stage` to raise in that test.

## WP-08 → WP-09: re-evaluation row and eval notes

- `POST /runs/{id}/evaluate/` creates a **pending** `RunEvaluation` (suite, `eval_level`/`mode` from `manifest.planned_evaluation`), calls `execution.api.request_reeval`, and stores `execution.reeval_evaluation_id` and `execution.reeval_suite` (pinned ref). Please have `run_eval_stage` fill that row (it may update a pending row) instead of creating a second one, and use `reeval_suite` as the suite.
- The generation stage hands over `running/eval_queued` with `outcome=""`, `tree_hash`, `workspace_blob`, `RunFile` rows, `result` (incl. `seed_tree_hash`, `collection`, `static`) and `metrics` (no `eval` key). Add evaluation notes with `agentic.validity.add_notes(run.validity, [validity.note("eval_reused"), ...])` so the generation notes are kept.

## WP-08 → WP-12: slot-agent hooks in `slot/executors/restricted_agent.py`

- `prepare(run) -> SlotAgentContext` (exists in the skeleton) is passed as `HarnessContext.slot`.
- **`hole_overlay(run) -> dict[str, bytes | None]`**: the hole-applied files laid over the parent tree (`None` deletes a path). WP-08 seeds the parent's `app` scope minus artifact rows and minus tests unless `manifest.slot.include_tests`; without the hook the parent tree is seeded unchanged (warning logged).
- **`collect_variant(run, *, final: dict[str, SeedFile], seed: dict[str, SeedFile], workspace_tar: Path | None) -> agentic.collector.Collected`**: write the variant's `slot` rows and return `Collected` (`rows`, `tree_hash` of the assembled tree, `workspace_blob`, `summary`, plus `invalid_reason="edit_violation"` under `edit_policy=fail` and `model_code` = "the replacement is non-empty"). Without it a `slot_agent` attempt raises `NotImplementedError`. `agentic.collector.classify_tree`, `tree_hash_of`, `FileRow` and `blobs` are there to reuse.

## WP-08 → WP-10: launch, plan-time digests, attempt rule

- Launch: create the run with its treatment FKs, `study`, `condition`, `cell`, `cell_attempt`, then `agentic.manifest.build_manifest(run, resolved_cell)` in the same transaction (it fills seed/identity/pinned columns and raises `ManifestMismatchError` on disagreement), then `on_commit(enqueue_runs)`. `resolved_cell.manifest.identity` must hold the `compute_keys` output; `resolved_cell.stimuli` must carry the brief (`role "brief"`, or `"slot_agent_brief"`) with its blob, which the runner reads.
- Image digests at plan time: `agentic.images.resolve_digest(image_tag(...))` gives `sha256:...` or `pending:<tag>`; put the same strings into `IdentityInputs.images` and `manifest.{harness,gateway}.image_digest`; emit `agentic.images.image_issues(...)`.
- `runs.queries.refresh_current(cell)` is called by `finish_stage`/`infra_retry`; the retry endpoint also calls `transitions.refresh_cell` on commit.

## WP-08 → WP-02b (integrations): enable the run MCP tools

`list_runs` → `operation_id="generation_runs_api_list_runs"`, `get_run` → `"generation_runs_api_get_run"` (see the WP-08 decisions).

## WP-08 → WP-07: notes on the adapter contract as used

- The runner uses `adapter.parser().parse_line` for live steps (a `list[Step]` or a single `Step` is accepted) and falls back to step counts when `parser()` raises (e.g. `FakeHarness`).
- `observed_inputs(calls).native_system_prompt_blob` is taken as the sha; the runner stores `common.native_system_prompt_text(calls)` with `blobs.put_text` so the blob exists.
- The local test backend runs `agentic.sandbox.entry_script()`, a root-relocatable copy of your entry script with the same `/logs` layout plus `harness.pid`; keep `exit.json`'s keys (`exit_code`, `started`, `ended`, `workspace_bytes`, `workspace_too_large`) stable, the docker backend reads them.

## WP-08 → WP-10 (docs/README.md): index entry

`docs/GENERATION_PROCESS.md` is rewritten for v3 (agentic stage, collector, outcomes, attempts, runs API, WP-08 settings); please keep it linked from `docs/README.md`.

## WP-17 decisions (early announcement: re-pointed shared API modules), 2026-11-03

WP-17 owns these modules and has re-pointed them to the v3 backend (clean break, no v2 aliases). Importers outside WP-17 (mostly WP-15's moved `studies/**` and WP-16's `applications/**`) should switch to the new names:

| Module | v2 name → v3 name |
|---|---|
| `lib/api/job-insights.ts` | `getJobCodeProfile` → `getRunCodeProfile`, `getJobTimeline`/`JobTimeline` → `getRunTimeline`/`RunTimeline`, `getJobQualityMetrics`/`JobQualityMetrics` → `getRunQualityMetrics`/`RunQualityMetrics`; URLs `/generation/runs/{id}/{code-profile,timeline,quality-metrics}/`. `CodeProfile` now has `run_id`, `kind`, `loc` (model scope) + `loc_app` (app scope), origins `starter|authored|modified|slot|artifact|parent` with `deleted_starter_files`/`excluded_by_reason`, dependencies `starter`/`starter_ref`/`dropped_starter`/`starter_spec` (no `layout`, `scaffold*`, `healing`). `RunTimeline.study: StudyCellRef | null` (was `experiment`); segments `attempt_<n>` carry `termination`, `budget_exhausted`, `oom_killed`. |
| `lib/api/variability.ts` | `getExperimentVariability`/`downloadExperimentVariabilityCsv`/`ExperimentVariability` → `getStudyVariability`/`downloadStudyVariabilityCsv`/`StudyVariability` (`/generation/studies/{id}/variability/`); `VariabilityQuery = {fingerprint?: "all"\|"majority", sensitivity?: "exclude_truncated"\|null}` (no `exclude_invalid`/`exclude_truncated`/`common_cells`/`planned_only`; ITT is the default); payload `study_id`, `filters.{stage,fingerprint,sensitivity,itt,planned_only,common_cells}`, new `selection` and `billing` blocks, `design.n_units`, conditions `n_units`, cells `unit_key`/`task`/`task_title`/`run_ids`/`n_pass_known`, pairs `run_a`/`run_b`, no `approximate`. `getJobReplicates`/`JobReplicates` → `getRunReplicates`/`RunReplicates` (members `run_id`, `study_id`/`study_name`, `cell_attempt`, `beyond_plan`, `outcome`, `usable`, `truncated`; relation adds `retry`; `determinism[].run_id`/`relation`; `issue_coverage`). `ModelVariability.n_completed_runs`; groups `task`, `unit_key`, `kind`, `stack`, `harness`, `studies`, `run_ids` (no `app_slug`/`method`/`mode`/`experiments`/`job_ids`/`approximate`). |
| `lib/api/trajectory.ts` | v2 per-job trajectory records are gone: `getJobTrajectory`, `getTrajectoryStep`, `TrajectoryRecord`, `StepDetail`, `RuleTable`, `BehaviourSummary` removed. A run's trajectory is WP-16's `getSampleTrajectory` (`/runs/{id}/trajectory/`). The module keeps `getModelBehaviour` (v3 payload: per `harness`+`harness_version`, `n_runs`, `n_unusable_excluded`, `n_without_trajectory`, `parsers`, `metrics` over `steps/tool_calls/edits/shell_commands/compactions/subagent_calls`, `time_split`, `rates.{ended_on_llm_error,max_turns_hit}`) and adds the contract mirrors `Trajectory`, `TrajectoryStep`, `ObservedInputs`. |
| `lib/api/telemetry.ts` | `getModelCallTelemetry(id, {kind})` (was `{mode}`); summaries `n_runs`/`kinds` (were `n_jobs`/`modes`); `callsCsvUrl({study, run, model})` (was `experiment`/`job`). |
| `lib/api/execution.ts`, `types/execution.ts` | `Capacity.studies: StudyCapacityRow[]`, `draining_remaining.runs`, `BlockedReason` `study_cap`, `QueueItem.{run_id, kind, study_id, cell_id}`, `QueueQuery.study_id`. |
| `lib/api/types/events.ts` | v2 `JobEvent`/`ExperimentEvent`/`TickEvent`/`StateEvent`/`PostRunEvent`/`CellsPayload` removed. It re-exports WP-16's `RunEvent`/`RUN_EVENT_TYPES` and WP-15's `StudyEvent` members/`STUDY_EVENT_TYPES` from `lib/api/v3/types.ts` (one definition per event; please keep those names), adds `runChannel(id)`/`studyChannel(id)`, `TickEndpoint`, and `REWORK_EVENT_TYPES` now registers every run/study event name for `sse.ts`. |
| `lib/api/reports.ts` | `REPORT_TYPE_LABELS` (`template_comparison` = "Task comparison", `experiment_report` = "Study conditions"), config types `TaskComparisonConfig`/`StudyConditionsConfig`/`StudyReportConfig`; summary `study_id` links to `/studies/{id}`. |
| `lib/api/human-eval.ts`, `types/human-eval.ts` | `listCandidateJobs`/`CandidateJob(Query)` → `listCandidateRuns`/`CandidateRun(Query)` (`/human-eval/candidate-runs/?study_id=`); `TaskListItem.study`, `TaskApp.run_id`, `ResultApp.run_id`, create/patch `study_id`/`run_ids`, add-apps `{run_ids, study_id, current_only}`; `RequirementSource = "frontend"\|"api"\|"admin_api"`; `FunctionalSummary.source = "eval_suite"`; `AppSpecView` has `prose` (no `backend_requirements`/`admin_requirements`); `PortalApp.admin_credentials_status`. |
| `lib/constants/colors.ts` | v2 cell codes removed (`CellCode`, `cellCodeTones`, `cellCodeLabels`, `liveCellCodes`); new `CellState`/`RunOutcome`, `cellStateTones`/`cellStateLabels`, `outcomeTones`/`outcomeLabels`, `liveCellStates`, `cellTone(state, outcome?)` (a done cell takes its outcome's tone). `statusTone` knows the v3 outcomes, eval statuses (`not_measured` = warning), terminations, study and cell states. |

## WP-11 decisions (budget, ledger, stop rules, execution integration; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/budget/{constants,model_facts (new),policy,estimate,priors,ledger,claims (new),live,stop_rules,report (new),gateway_config,api}.py`, `backend/generation/data/resource_policies/{frugal,standard,generous}.yaml`, `backend/generation/management/commands/refresh_cost_priors.py`, `backend/execution/{scheduler,capacity,transitions,reconciler,executor}.py` (+ `tests/test_executor_loop.py`: the two "unimplemented stage / post-run" tests now patch the stage to raise, since WP-08/WP-10 landed), the WP-11 settings block (`BUDGET_LIVE_POLL_S`), `docs/{BUDGET (new),EXECUTION,BACKGROUND_SERVICES}.md`, tests `backend/generation/tests/wp11_budget/` (incl. the done-when FakeGateway study). No migrations, no new deps.

**Published interfaces:**
- **`budget.policy`**: `effective_policy(base, overrides=None, *, model=None, harness=None, study_ctx=None, study=None, executor=None, machine_memory=None, machine_nano_cpus=None) -> EffectivePolicy` (`base`: ResourcePolicy row | content dict | `ResourcePolicyContent`; `model`: LLMModel | `ModelFacts`; `harness`: adapter | `HarnessFacts` | name | `HarnessInfo`). `StudyPolicyContext {study_kind, mode, executor ("agentic" | "single_shot" | "restricted_agent"), n_model_levels, serving_tier (already resolved per study), stack_min_memory, stack_min_nano_cpus, any_non_billed_endpoint, p99_run_usd, random_effects, primary_features, sampling_policy, temperature, brief_tokens, slot_original_tokens, slot_prompt_tokens}`; `context_for_study(study | design)`. `treatment_view(effective)` (= `values_by_class(clamped, {"C","R"}, budget_basis)`), `generation_policy_hash(effective)`, `lint(content, *, model, harness, study_ctx, budget_basis)` (a rule whose input is missing does not fire), `lint_study([ConditionPolicy(condition_index, effective, stack, cells)], study_kind=)` (`resources_vary_by_stack`, `mixed_serving_tier`), `apply_overrides(base, {"dotted.key": v} | nested)` (unknown keys → `KeyError`), `resolve_serving_tier(policies, slot_executors=, models=) -> (tier, notes)`, `execution_options(effective) -> {"max_parallel", "execution_options"}` (launch copies both onto Study), `summary_line(policy, name=, version=, tier=)`, `diff_from_tier(content, tier=None)`, `knob_meta_payload()`, `explain(...)`. Clamp notes: `reasoning_unsupported`, `batch_unsupported`, `max_price_unresolved`, `sandbox_clamped_to_machine` (generous on the default 4g host: a clamp, not a lint issue, so all presets pass the lint), `stack_minimum_exceeds_machine`. `content.study.budget_basis/cap_basis` and `routing.serving_tier` are written back resolved (so the treatment view never contains `auto`).
- **`budget.model_facts`**: `ModelFacts` (limits, reasoning/tools support, prices per Mtok incl. cached/cache-write, endpoint kind and cost basis) and `model_facts(LLMModel | dict | ModelFacts)`, `is_model_alias(slug)`. Unknown values are `None`, never 0.
- **`budget.estimate`**: `estimate_cell(cell, *, priors=None, **overrides)`, `estimate_inputs(CellInputs, *, priors=None)`, `inputs_from_cell(cell, **overrides)`, `estimate_plan(plan, *, policy=None, tier=None, study_kind=, repeats=, effective_parallel=, priors=, cell_estimator=, cap_override=)` (policy defaults to the first cell's `effective_policy`, tier to the cell's `manifest.resources.ref` row), `auto_cap(p90_total, tier)` (round-up to the cent), `resolve_study_cap`, `reestimate_after_pilot(estimate, {condition_index: [costs]}) -> (Estimate, changes)`, `est_tokens(text) -> (n, method)` (tiktoken only with a seeded `TIKTOKEN_CACHE_DIR`, else `chars/3.6`). Estimates are truncated at the cell's per-attempt worst case `cap_usd`; an unpriced model's estimate is the run cap (`basis` ends `:unpriced`); subscription/local endpoints bill 0 and carry `expected_list_usd`.
- **`budget.priors`**: `refresh_priors(keys=, window_days=)`, `refresh_for_run(run)`, `lookup(...)`, `calibration(model, provider_sort)`, `overhead(kind, harness)`. CostPrior auxiliary rows: `calibration|<model>|<provider_sort>|*|*` (`cost_p50` = median billed/catalog) and `overhead|<kind>|<harness>|*|*` (`price_at = {infra_retry_rate, mean_partial_cost, failed_call_cost_share, wasted_share}`). Command `manage.py refresh_cost_priors [--key K]... [--window-days N] [--dry-run]`.
- **`budget.ledger`** (attempts are 1-based: `attempt_no(run) = run.attempt + 1`, same as WP-08): `reserve(run, attempt=, amount_usd=, tokens=)`, `reserve_block(runs, needs=, tokens=)`, `run_need(run) -> (usd, tokens)` (`min(run.max_cost_usd, cell.cap_usd)`), `release(entry)`, `record_usage(run, attempt=, source=, amount_usd=, list_cost_usd=, tokens_in=, tokens_out=, requests=, dedupe_key=)`, `settle_attempt(run, *, attempt=None, calls_path=None, final=None)` (final from `calls.jsonl` `usage.cost` → the record's accrued `cost` → manifest price table, else `RunAttempt.usage`, else the run's metrics; idempotent), `study_totals(study) -> StudyTotals {spent_billed, spent_list, open_reserved, held, spent_tokens, held_tokens, cap_basis; spent, exposure}`, `refresh_caches(study_id, cell_ids=)` (Study `spent_*`/`reserved_*`, cell `spent_*`), `reconcile()`. Dedupe keys: `res:{run}:{a}`, `rel:{run}:{a}`, `blk:{study}:{stage}:{block}:r|:mv:{run}:{a}|:x:{cell}|:close`, `live:{run}:{a}:{seq}`, `proxy:{llm_request_id}`, `adj:{run}:{a}`. Reservations mirror the amount into both columns; tokens of reservations go in `tokens_in`. Ledger rows carry the system OpenRouter endpoint id when the run's endpoint is NULL.
- **`budget.claims`** (called by `execution.scheduler` inside the claim transaction): `admit(run_ids) -> {id: Decision(ok, reason, detail, reserved, warnings)}`, `study_cap(study)` (the policy's numeric `study.max_cost_usd`, else `active_plan.estimate.cap_usd`, else no USD cap), `exclude_runs(runs, reason)` (pending → status `cancelled`, `outcome=excluded`, `outcome_reason=<reason>`, cell `excluded` + `exclusion_reason`; events/refresh/block close on commit), `pause_study(study, reason, condition_id=)`, `apply_budget_exhausted(study, detail, action=)`, `resume_paused_for_caps()`. Spend caps are checked **before** a block starts (a refused claim never holds block budget). SpendCap usage = window usage + what open reservations in scope still hold. Quota = `scope=model` with period `5h`/`week`.
- **`budget.live`**: `record_live(run, *, attempt, state, seq)` (WP-08's supervisor hook: done; publishes `spend`, not `usage`), `poll(run, *, state=None, read=None)` (also publishes `run:<id>` `usage`), `record_proxy_usage(llm_request)`, `docker_state_reader(container)`, `poll_interval_s(running)`, `publish_spend(run, delta=, before=)` (`budget_alert` once per crossing of an `alert_at` fraction).
- **`budget.stop_rules`**: `evaluate(study, *, apply=True) -> [{code, scope, scope_key, value, threshold, action, applied, ...}]`, `evaluate_by_id`, `evaluate_active`, `pilot_gates(study, *, runs=None) -> [{code, ok, value, threshold, n, measured, condition_id? | stack?}]` (+ a `cap_hits_by_kind` row; `min_build_rate` with nothing usable fails), `gates_pass`, `pilot_observations(study)`, `wilson_half_width(k, n)`. A rate rule needs `min(window, 5)` finished runs. `ci_width_reached`: exploratory slot only; reads the live `PatternRun` (`is_live=True`) `PatternHit`s with status present/absent (one per run and feature; features match `feature_key`, detector `ref`/`pinned_ref`/`slug`); sets `StudyCell.adaptive_n`; number of looks **`K = n_at_stop - n_min + 1`**. Pause reasons are `rule:<code>`; a study-scope `stop` sets `Study.stop_reason {code: "rule:<code>", rule}`.
- **`budget.report`**: `study_payload(study)` (the 4.7 payload plus `budget_basis`, `on_budget_exhausted`, `spent_billed`, `open_reserved`, `rules` (currently firing, not applied), `deviations`), `me_payload(user)`, `header_payload(user)`, `block_status(study)`, **`complete_blocks(study, stage=None) -> [(stage, block_index)]`**, **`budget_deviations(study) -> {stop_reason, exclusions: [{condition_id, cell_budget, budget_stop, stopped_by_rule, cancelled}], stopped_early: [{condition_id, unit_id, cells}], paused_conditions, adaptive_n}`**.
- **`budget.gateway_config.build(run, *, attempt=None)`**: no side effects; `attempt = run.attempt + 1`, `session_id = run uuid` when sticky, `budget.max_cost_usd = min(run cap, this attempt's reservation)`, `sampling.forward_seed=False`/`seed=None` for `anthropic_messages`/`openai_responses` (WP-06 ask: done), `cache_control_providers = [model family]` for explicit-cache families when `prompt_cache=on`, `rate_limit.bucket_key = "<study|adhoc>:<model slug>"`. `provider_object(content)` builds the OpenRouter `provider` object (unset knobs and empty lists left out). Raises `ValueError` without a manifest.
- **API (operation ids)**: `budget_estimate` (POST `/budget/estimate/` `{design}` | `{study}` | `{study, plan_hash}` + optional `cap`), `budget_study` (GET `/budget/studies/{id}/`), `budget_study_ledger` (GET `/budget/studies/{id}/ledger/?run=&kind=&page=&page_size=`), `budget_me`, `budget_header`, `budget_caps_list` (GET `/budget/caps/`), `budget_caps_create` (POST), `budget_caps_update` (PATCH `/budget/caps/{cap_id}/`), `budget_caps_delete` (DELETE `/budget/caps/{cap_id}/`), `budget_priors` (`?harness=&model=`), `budget_policy_explain` (GET `/budget/policies/{ref}/explain/?model=&harness=&study=&executor=` → `{effective, notes, issues, treatment_view, generation_policy_hash, knob_meta, summary, ref, tier, diff_from_tier}`), `budget_knobs` (GET `/budget/knobs/` → `{knobs: [KnobMeta.to_dict()], key_limits, tiers: {tier: content}}`). Errors `{"detail", "code"}`; codes `not_found`, `bad_ref`, `bad_request`, `design_invalid`, `resolver_unavailable`, `estimate_unavailable`, `staff_only`, `staff_cap`, `above_staff_cap`, `endpoint_required`, `model_required`.
- **SSE** (`study:<id>`): `spend`, `budget_alert {fraction}`, `paused {reason, condition_id?}` (+ `state`), `stopped {reason}` (+ `state`; reason `budget` or `rule:<code>`).
- **Execution**: blocked reasons `study_endpoint_cap`, `study_model_cap`, `study_provider_cap` (provider = `manifest.gateway.routing.only[0]`, else `gateway.routing.provider.only[0]`), `budget`, `cell_budget`, `budget_stop`, `spend_cap`, `quota`; runs of a paused `StudyCondition` are left out of the lanes (they never block other conditions); `eval_parallel` is enforced in the evaluation claim; `finish_stage` settles the spend when the generation stage ends and, on terminal transitions, refreshes the run's cost priors and evaluates the study's stop rules (`transitions.settle_spend`, `transitions.after_run_finished`); `infra_retry` settles before it wipes; `maybe_complete_study` ends a study with a pending stop (`stop_reason` set) as **`stopped`** from `running` or `piloting` (event `stopped {reason}`); reconciler steps `ledger` and `spend_caps` added; stop rules every 60 s in the executor loop.
- **Presets**: `data/resource_policies/<tier>.yaml` = `{name, description, tier, content}`; `content` equals `contracts.resources.preset_content(tier)` (tested).

## WP-11 → WP-03: seeding the budget presets

- Seed `data/resource_policies/*.yaml` with `ResourcePolicy.tier = <yaml "tier">` (the column is immutable per version and the UI badge reads it), `name`, `description`, `content`; `is_system=True`.

## WP-11 → WP-10: plan, launch and results hooks

- **Per-condition context.** `plan.py` calls `effective_policy(policy_row, study=..., model=..., harness=...)`; this works (context from the design), but for exact lint please pass `study_ctx=` (or at least `executor=` = the condition's `slot_executor` level mapped to `single_shot`/`restricted_agent`, `agentic` for app) plus `stack_min_memory`/`stack_min_nano_cpus` (max over the study's stacks), `any_non_billed_endpoint`, `serving_tier` from **one** `budget.policy.resolve_serving_tier(all condition policies, slot_executors=..., models=...)` call per study, and `brief_tokens` / `slot_original_tokens` / `slot_prompt_tokens` when known (then `brief_exceeds_call_cap`, `slot_output_cap_tight`, `slot_context_exceeds_model` fire at resolve). Also call `lint_study(...)` once with every condition's effective policy.
- **Study cap at launch.** `claims.study_cap(study)` uses the preset's numeric `study.max_cost_usd`, else `active_plan.estimate.cap_usd` (the resolved `auto`). A cap typed in the LaunchDialog (`study_cap`) must land in one of those (a custom preset version, or the frozen plan's estimate), otherwise the scheduler does not see it. Set `Study.cap_basis` (and `budget_basis`, `on_budget_exhausted`) from the effective policy at launch; claims read the column first.
- **Cells.** `StudyCell.cap_usd` = the cell estimate's `cap_usd`, `cell_cap_usd` = `cell.max_cost_usd`, `estimate_usd` = expected (launch already does). Block indices must be repeat-major and unique per (stage, block); claims reserve per `(stage, block_index)`.
- **Results.** Paired and by-condition comparisons should use `budget.report.complete_blocks(study, stage="main")` only, and the gate/results payload should embed `budget.report.budget_deviations(study)` (stop reason, exclusions per condition, `stopped_early` groups; in confirmatory studies the unfinished cells of a paused condition are `stopped_early`).
- **Expand.** `expand_pilot` can show the re-estimate with `estimate.reestimate_after_pilot(plan_estimate, stop_rules.pilot_observations(study))`.
- **`refresh_current`** must keep `StudyCell.state = excluded` (and its `exclusion_reason`) for cells budget/rules excluded before any claim (their only run is `cancelled` + `excluded:<reason>`).
- **docs/README.md**: please add `docs/BUDGET.md` (budget presets, estimate, reservations and blocks, live spend, spend caps, stop rules and pilot gates).

## WP-11 → WP-06: local-proxy usage in the ledger

- After each completed proxied `LLMRequest` of a run, call `backend.generation.budget.live.record_proxy_usage(llm_request)` (best effort; dedupe `proxy:{id}`, 0 billed USD for `free`/`list_price`, list cost from the manifest pricing, tokens for token caps), so local runs count against token caps and SpendCaps live.

## WP-11 → WP-12: slot calls in the ledger

- Single-shot executors: record each call with `budget.ledger.record_usage(run, attempt=attempt_no(run), source="gateway_final" | "batch", ..., dedupe_key=f"call:{run.pk}:{a}:{i}")` (billed cost, list cost, tokens) and end with `settle_attempt(run, final=...)` or let `finish_stage` settle. Use `budget.gateway_config.build(run)` for the in-process policy (its `call.max_output_tokens` already uses `slot.max_output_tokens`). The `cost_hint` of a target preview can use `estimate.estimate_inputs(estimate.CellInputs(kind="slot_single_shot", model=model_facts(model), policy=..., brief_tokens=..., original_tokens=..., context_tokens=..., n_replicates=30, serving_tier=...))`. `budget.policy` reads `settings.BATCH_BACKENDS_VERIFIED` with `getattr(..., False)`.

## WP-11 → WP-13: live hits for `ci_width_reached`

- The rule reads the study's live `PatternRun(is_live=True)` hits with status `present`/`absent` (errors never count). Write one hit per (variant, detector); the rule matches a listed feature against `feature_key`, the detector's `ref`, `pinned_ref` or `slug`. Stopped groups have `StudyCell.adaptive_n=True`; use `K = n_at_stop - n_min + 1` looks for the conservative interval (Wilson at alpha/K).

## WP-11 → WP-14 / WP-15: budget API shapes

- Operation ids and response shapes are listed in the WP-11 decisions above (`/budget/knobs/` is `{knobs, key_limits, tiers}`; each knob has `key, class, class_when_tokens, where, ui_group, key_limit, type, unit, description, defaults{frugal,standard,generous}`; caps PATCH/DELETE take `/budget/caps/{cap_id}/`). The 4.7 payload keys are those of `budget.report.study_payload`; `by_condition[].condition` is the StudyCondition id.

## WP-12 decisions (slot mode backend; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/slot/**` (new: `tree.py`, `part.py`, `hole.py`, `types.py`, `errors.py`, `variants.py`, `index/{languages,imports}.py`), `backend/generation/testing/fake_slot.py` (new, WP-12's own fake), the WP-12 settings block (`BATCH_BACKENDS_VERIFIED=False`, `BATCH_POLL_S=60`, `BATCH_EXPIRY_S=86400`), `docs/SLOT_MODE.md`, tests `backend/generation/tests/wp12_slot/` (incl. `test_slot_golden.py` of 13.3 and the 13.4 invariant `test_invariants.py`). No migrations, no new deps. `docs/SLOT_MODE.md` documents every rule; the interface summary:

- **Parents:** `parents.pool(PoolQuery)`, `parents.pool_for(task=, stack=, models=, harnesses=, studies=, min_pass_rate=, outcome=)`, `parents.draw(ParentSelection)` (needs a seed; ranks by `sha256(f"{seed}:{run id}")`, stratified round-robin over parent models: reproducible across Python versions), `parents.draw_with_record(selection) -> DrawResult(parents, seed, pool_size)` (fresh seed when omitted), `parents.selection_record(...)`. `freeze.freeze(parent, *, name, user, selection=None, allow_unbuilt_parent=False, pool_size=None, seed=None) -> SlotSet` (requires a manifest, tree hash and present blobs; `SlotInvalidError` 422 otherwise).
- **Index:** `index.build(run) -> SymbolIndex`, `index.build_from_files({path: bytes}, spec_endpoints=[{id, method, path}]) -> IndexBuild`, `index.ensure_index(run) -> RunSymbolIndex`, `index.load(row)`, `index.stats_of(index)`. Span convention: 1-based inclusive lines, exclusive `end_byte`; symbol spans start at the line start when only whitespace precedes; ids `path::qualname` (`#n` duplicates); inline route handlers are `route_handler` symbols `"<METHOD> <path>"`. `SymbolEntry.language` is `tsx` for `.tsx`. `routes.route_key(path)` is the parameter-agnostic match key.
- **Selection:** `selection.resolve(slot_set, SlotTargetIn | dict) -> Resolved`, `preview(...) -> dict`, `create_target(slot_set, target_in, *, user, validate=True) -> SlotTarget` (same part twice returns the existing row), `match(target, slot_sets | "same_task", user=)`, `auto_links`, `interface_of`, `match_key_of`, `overlapping_targets`, `anchors_for`, `target_issues(target, study_kind)`. `types.SlotTargetIn` = spec fields + optional `component`, `content_sha`, `anchors`. Errors (`slot/errors.py`): `AddressStaleError` 409 `address_stale`, `InUseError` 409 `in_use`, `SlotNotFoundError` 404, `SlotInvalidError` 422 `invalid` (`payload()` = `{"detail","code","data"?}`).
- **Hole / context / leak:** `hole.apply_hole(source, address, hole_mode, keep_docstring=, whole_file_component=) -> HoleResult(removed, holed, start, end, hole, prefix, suffix)`; `context.resolve_context(target, context_policy=None, *, hole_mode=None, keep_docstring=None) -> ResolvedContext` and `context.materialize(...) -> MaterializedContext(resolved, files, hole)` (`CONTEXT_VERSION=1`; tokens `ceil(chars/3.6)`); `leak.scan(target, *, context_policy=None, executor=None, hole_mode=None) -> LeakReport` (`executor=None` = both scopes, `single_shot` = context, `restricted_agent` = context + workspace), `leak.leak_issues(report, study_kind)`, `leak.blocks_use(report)`, `leak.score_texts(...)`.
- **Validation:** `validate.queue_validation(target, *, suite=None, user=None, enqueue=True)` (3 `slot_original` + 1 `slot_holed` reference runs with copied parent rows, priority -10, `execution.api.enqueue_evaluation` on commit; suite = `slot_set.parent_eval.eval_suite`, pinned in `run.execution["slot_validation_suite"]`), `validate.on_reference_evaluated(run) -> TargetValidation | None` (the eval-stage hook), `validate.validate_target(target, *, synchronous=False)`, `validate.revalidate(target, user=)`, `validate.target_usable(target) -> (bool, reason)` (status ok/uninformative and no verbatim leak), `validate.validation_issues(target, study_kind)`. The evaluated selection is recorded as the keys of `validation.original_pass` / `probe_stability` (no new field).
- **Prompt:** `prompt.build_slot_context(target | Part, *, hole_mode, keep_docstring, context: ResolvedContext | MaterializedContext, context_policy=None, executor="single_shot")` (a bare `ResolvedContext` is re-materialised and must hash to the same sha), `prompt.render_stimuli(target, prompt_set, *, executor, hole_mode, keep_docstring, context_policy, guidance, style) -> {stimuli, prompt_hash, est_tokens, resolved_context}` (uses WP-03's `build_slot_context` and `engine.preview_prompt_hash`), `prompt.output_contract`, `prompt.spec_excerpt`, `prompt.uses_files_output(part, hole_mode)`.
- **Executors:** `single_shot.run_stage(run)` (ends its stage itself: `finish_stage(evaluate | done)` or `infra_retry`), `single_shot.parse_output(response, SlotRequest) -> ParseResult`, `single_shot.build_request(run, manifest, stimuli, part)`, `single_shot.sampling_issues(sampling_content, study_kind)`, `single_shot.transport` (module attribute; tests swap it); `batch_submitter.run_batch(runs, *, backend=None, sleep=, clock=) -> {run id: outcome}`; `restricted_agent.{prepare, hole_overlay, collect_variant, seed_files, seed_workspace, finalize, diff_workspace}`.
- **Assembly / evaluation:** `assemble.assemble(run, replacement) -> [RunFile]` (slot rows only; sets `run.tree_hash` on the instance), `assemble.assemble_tree(target, replacement) -> AssembledTree`, `assemble.spliced_files(run) -> {path: bytes}` (WP-05's pure hook: done), `assemble.recorded_replacement(run)`; `evaluate.evaluate_variant(run, *, mode=None) -> RunEvaluation`, `evaluate.evaluate_original(target)`, `evaluate.selection_for(run)`, `evaluate.slot_pass_rate(evaluation, target)` (`None` for uninformative/unmeasured), `evaluate.outcome_of(evaluation)`. `SlotVariantResult.replacement_blob` holds the model's raw bytes (code, or sorted-key JSON of `{path: text}`); `replacement_sha` is the normalised identity.
- **API operation ids (WP-16):** `slots_parents_list`, `slots_parents_draw`, `slots_sets_create`, `slots_sets_list`, `slots_sets_get`, `slots_sets_tree`, `slots_sets_symbols`, `slots_sets_endpoints`, `slots_sets_components`, `slots_sets_file`, `slots_sets_preview_target`, `slots_sets_create_target` (201 new, 200 existing), `slots_targets_match`, `slots_targets_revalidate` (409 once variants exist), `slots_targets_delete` (204; 409 `in_use`), `slots_targets_get`, `slots_targets_render`, `slots_targets_variants`, `slots_variants_diff`. Response keys are in `slot/api.py` (`_parent_row`, `_slot_set_out`, `_target_out` incl. `usable`, `unusable_reason`, `issues`; preview keys in `selection.preview`).
- **Asks addressed to WP-12 that are done:** WP-05 `spliced_files`; WP-03 one prompt-hash implementation and `prompt_set=` passed; WP-08 `hole_overlay` / `collect_variant` (an invalid variant has no rows, `model_code=False` and `invalid_reason` = `edit_violation` or the parse status); WP-11 attempt numbering (`RunAttempt.attempt = run.attempt + 1`) and settlement through `finish_stage` from the `RunAttempt.usage` the executor writes (`cost_usd`, `list_cost_usd`, tokens, `requests_by_class={"main":1}`; no separate `record_usage` row).

## WP-12 → WP-09: slot runs in the eval stage

1. In `run_eval_stage(run)`, for a run with `slot_target` (kinds `slot_*` and `reference` with `reference_of` in `slot_original`/`slot_holed`) call **`slot.evaluate.evaluate_variant(run)`**: it computes the selection (validation candidates for reference runs; discriminating checks + linked probes for variants and the original), the mode (overlay fallback rules) and the reuse, and calls `suite.evaluate(run, suite, only_checks=, probes=, mode=, image_override=, overlay_files=)` itself. Then set the outcome as usual and, for the two slot reference kinds, call **`slot.validate.on_reference_evaluated(run)`** once the evaluation is final.
2. `overlay_files` is `{path: blob sha}` of the variant's `slot` rows and `image_override` the parent's built image id (`parent_eval.image_id`).
3. Please write `stages.tester.detail.only_checks` for these calls too (your `find_reusable` reads it) and keep the always-on health/smoke check running when `only_checks` is given.
4. When mirroring into `run.metrics["eval"]`, fill `slot_pass_rate` with `slot.evaluate.slot_pass_rate(evaluation, run.slot_target)` (discriminating checks only; `None` for uninformative targets and unmeasured runs).
5. Optional: `checks_for_endpoints(..., categories=["functional"])` would replace the component-target filter in `selection._functional_only` (it reads task `acceptance[].category` and assumes compiler checks are functional).

## WP-12 → WP-10: plan resolver hooks for slot studies

- Per (target, condition): `ctx = slot.context.resolve_context(target, condition_context_policy, hole_mode=<level>, keep_docstring=<level>)`, treatment `slot_policy = SlotPolicy(...).identity(ctx.sha)`; refuse targets with `slot.validate.target_usable(target)[0] is False`; add `slot.validate.validation_issues(target, kind)` and `slot.leak.leak_issues(slot.leak.scan(target, context_policy=..., executor=<level>, hole_mode=<level>), kind)`; `slot.executors.single_shot.sampling_issues(sampling_content, kind)` gives `slot_sampling_provider_default`.
- Executor levels are `single_shot` | `restricted_agent`.
- Record `sampling.forced_params["response_format"] = "json_schema"` in the manifest of single-shot cells whose model supports structured output (`LLMModel.supports_json_mode` at resolve); the executor reads only the manifest and strips the key from the sampling params.
- `ManifestSlot`: `address = target.address`, `allowlist = target.allowlist`, `leak_score = target.leak["score"]` (or the per-context scan's), `validation = {discriminating_checks, dropped_checks}` from `target.validation`, `parent_selection = {rule, seed}` from `slot_set.selection`, `include_tests = context_policy.include_tests`, `resolved_context = {files, sha}` of `ctx`.
- Stimuli: `prompts.context.build_slot_context(slot_target=target, slot_policy=..., resolved_context=ctx, executor=...)` (WP-03) calls `slot.prompt.build_slot_context`, which re-materialises the context and refuses a sha mismatch.
- Parent draws for `Design.parent_selection`: `slot.parents.draw(selection)` with the seed derived from `base_seed`; record it on each SlotSet through `freeze(..., selection=, pool_size=, seed=)`.

## WP-12 → WP-13: variant records and the golden normalizer values

- `SlotVariantResult.normalized_sha` / `normalizer_version` and `diff_vs_original.{normalized_equal, ast_edit_distance}` are left empty for the pattern pass; WP-12 writes `lines_added`, `lines_removed`, `similarity`, `new_imports`, `new_dependencies`, `out_of_slot_edits`, `edit_violation`. Read replacements with `slot.assemble.recorded_replacement(run)`; `leak_score` is `target.leak["score"]`.
- `slot.evaluate.evaluate_variant` calls `patterns.live.score_variant(run)` after a study variant's evaluation when `study.design.slot_primary_features` is non-empty (reused evaluations included).
- `tests/wp12_slot/test_slot_golden.py` freezes `normalized_sha` of three fixture variants (python, tsx, go) from today's `normalize.normalized_sha`. If NORMALIZER_VERSION 1 output changes before the wave-3 freeze, please update the three `VARIANTS` values there (WP-12 agrees to that edit) and say so here.

## WP-12 → WP-16: slot picker API

- Operation ids and keys above. `POST /slots/sets/{id}/preview-target/` returns `address, kind, language, match_key, default_hole_mode, default_keep_docstring, default_context_policy, edit_policy, allowlist, defaults (fields that took kind defaults), interface, original_text, hole_applied_text, hole_removes_file, resolved_context, linked_endpoints, linked_checks, linked_checks_source, linked_checks_diff, linked_probes, leak, target_hash, cost_hint {model, tokens_in, tokens_out, per_variant_usd, per_30_usd}, issues`. Send `content_sha` + `anchors` of the selection you show so a stale selection is relocated or refused with 409 `address_stale`. Selection snapping: region spans are whole lines; symbol spans start at the line start.

## WP-12 → WP-10 (docs/README.md): index entry

- Please add `docs/SLOT_MODE.md` (parents, symbol index, targets and holes, context and leak scan, validation, executors, assembly and evaluation, slots API).

## WP-13 decisions (pattern analysis; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/patterns/**` (new: `stats.py`, `variants.py`, `service.py`, `drafts.py`, `catalog.py`), `backend/generation/data/detectors/**` (22 seed detectors + `fixtures/`), `backend/analysis/services/rulepack.py`, `backend/human_eval/services/detector_audit.py`, `backend/generation/testing/{fake_semgrep,fake_patterns}.py` (WP-13 area fakes), the WP-13 settings block (`PATTERNS_SEMGREP_BACKEND=workspace|local`, `PATTERNS_SEMGREP_TIMEOUT_S=600`, `PATTERNS_EXACT_PERMUTATION_MAX_TARGETS=20`, `PATTERNS_PERMUTATION_DRAWS=10000`), `docs/PATTERNS.md`, tests `backend/generation/tests/wp13_patterns/`. No migrations, no model changes, no new deps.

- **Entry points:** `patterns.run(pattern_run, *, runner=None)`, `patterns.create_pattern_run(study, *, detectors="default"|[refs], cluster_params=None, scope=None, user=None, queue=True, trigger="manual")` (queue=True also creates the `StudyPostRun(kind=patterns, pattern_run=…)`; WP-10's `automation._run_patterns` with `queue=False` is compatible), `patterns.run_post_run(post_run)`. `PatternRunError` (ValueError) for non-slot studies / unknown refs.
- **`detector_set_hash`** (closes WP-01 follow-up #7 on the WP-13 side): `patterns.detectors.detector_set_hash(specs) = H({"version": 1, "detectors": sorted([[slug@v, full content_hash]])})`; `detector_set_hash_for_refs(refs)`. It is computed at creation, not derived by the model (WP-01 may move it into `identity.py` and derive it; same formula please). **Labelling:** `planned` iff the study is confirmatory, `locked_at` + `prereg_hash` set, and `analysis_plan.patterns` matches on detector set (its `detector_set_hash`, or the hash of its `detectors` refs), `normalizer_version`, normalized `cluster_params` and `scope`; live runs are always exploratory. `primary_features` may be feature keys or detector refs.
- **Live / stop rule (WP-11 ask honoured):** `patterns.live.score_variant(run, *, runner=None)` writes **one provisional hit per (variant, detector)** at scope `span` into the study's single live run (`is_live=True`, status `running`, label `exploratory`); no hits for variants without output. `patterns.live.ci_width_reached(study, rule)` and `live_estimates(study)` are also available (they record looks in the live run's `summary["looks"]`). Final prevalence of `adaptive_n` groups uses recorded looks if present, else **`K = n_at_stop - n_min + 1`** (n_min from the policy's `ci_width_reached` rule, default 10), Wilson at alpha/K.
- **WP-12 ask honoured:** the final run fills `SlotVariantResult.normalized_sha`/`normalizer_version` and `diff_vs_original.{normalized_equal, ast_edit_distance}` only where empty (never overwrites). The three golden `normalized_sha` values in `wp12_slot/test_slot_golden.py` are unchanged (verified).
- **Normalizer:** `NORMALIZER_VERSION=1`; grammars limited to `normalize.GRAMMARS` (python, javascript, typescript, tsx, vue, svelte, go, java, html, css). Asking tree-sitter-language-pack 0.13 for any other name (e.g. `cobol`) **hangs** (download attempt); anyone calling `get_parser` with user input should guard the same way.
- **Denominators:** ITT n = main-stage cells of the active plan, minus cells excluded `stopped_by_rule`/`budget_stop`/`cancelled`. A cell without a parsed replacement (incl. usable `output_invalid`) is *missing* (counted in ITT, bounds), never absent. Probe/manual detectors report scope `span` only.
- **Semgrep:** one rule-pack call per run over every variant's assembled file (paths `<run_id>/<path>`), rule ids rewritten `llmlab-d<i>-r<j>`; rules must carry their own `severity` (never defaulted). `rulepack.run_semgrep_rules(files, rules, *, user, timeout_s, runner) -> SemgrepRun` never raises; `SemgrepRun.file_error(path)` is the no-silent-clean check. The workspace backend uses `/tmp/llmlab-rulepack/<uuid>` in the analyzer container, not `/work`.
- **API operation ids (WP-16):** `patterns_run_create`, `patterns_run_list` (`?study=&live=`), `patterns_run_get` (summary without the big tables), `patterns_run_prevalence`, `patterns_run_comparisons`, `patterns_run_pooled` (`{method, rows, notes}`), `patterns_run_clusters` (`{clusters: [...], stats}`; `stats` is one object when both `target` and `condition` are given, else a list), `patterns_detector_from_selection`, `patterns_cluster_label`, `patterns_audit_create`, `patterns_audit_list`, `patterns_run_export`. Results endpoints answer 409 `pattern_run_not_completed` before completion (live runs: provisional prevalence). Prevalence rows carry `audit` (`{precision, npv}` of a complete audit) and `audit_status` (`not_audited|pending|in_progress|complete`). Errors `{"detail","code"}`.
- **Export column contract:** `patterns.export.{VARIANT_COLUMNS, FEATURE_COLUMNS, PREVALENCE_COLUMNS, COMPARISON_COLUMNS, AUDIT_COLUMNS}` (header snapshot in `test_pipeline.py`); feature columns in `variants.csv` are `f:<feature>@<scope>`.
- **Fakes for others:** `testing/fake_semgrep.FakeSemgrep` (+ `install(monkeypatch)`), `testing/fake_patterns.{build_slot_study, FakeSlotExecutor, TargetSpec}`.

Library lint for detectors can call `patterns.detectors.validate_content(content) -> [problems]` (regex/ts_query compile per language, semgrep YAML with languages + severity).

## WP-17 decisions (consumer re-point, wave 2), 2026-11-03

Files: everything WP-17 owns in 12.1, plus `lib/api/runs.ts` (the analysis client; unowned in 12.1 but assigned to WP-17 by the U1 analysis ask), `lib/api/reports.test.ts` (the module's own test), new `routes/(app)/analysis/[id]/{setup.ts,setup.test.ts}`, new tests `lib/api/{legacy-imports,consumer-repoint}.test.ts`. No backend change, no migration, no new dependency.

- **Clients used:** the consumers read WP-15's `lib/api/v3/studies.ts` (`listStudies`, `getStudy`, `getStudyResults`) and WP-16's `lib/api/v3/samples.ts` (`listSamples`, `getSample`) with their types from `lib/api/v3/types.ts`. WP-17 added nothing to `types.ts` (its block stays empty): every type it needs already lives in the WP-14/15/16 blocks. `types/events.ts` re-exports `RunEvent`/`RUN_EVENT_TYPES` (WP-16 block) and `StudyEvent`, `StudyRunEvent`, `StudyTickEvent`, `StudyStateEvent`, `StudyPostRunEvent`, `SpendEvent`, `BudgetAlertEvent`, `StoppedEvent`, `PausedEvent`, `STUDY_EVENT_TYPES` (WP-15 block) — **please keep these names** (or tell WP-17).
- **Human-eval:** `AppPoolPicker` props are `bind:selected` (run ids), `bind:studyId`, `excludeRunIds` (were `experimentId`/`excludeJobIds`). The study filter lists non-draft studies (`listStudies({page_size: 100})`); "Add the whole study" posts `{study_id, current_only: true}`; "Current run per cell only" maps to `current_only`. Baseline options of `vs_baseline` pairing come from `getStudyResults(study).leaderboard` (StudyCondition ids, `is_baseline` marked) because `StudyOut` carries no conditions. The wizard's session draft key is `llmlab:human-eval:new-task:v3` with `runIds`/`studyId`; a stored v2 draft is never read back. Functional cells show `functionalStatusLabel(status)` (null and `not_measured` read "Not measured", never a pass or 0) and the source "EvalSuite". Links go to `/samples/{run}`.
- **Analysis:** `AppPicker` lists `listSamples` rows (default `usable=true`, i.e. tested or a measured build/start/output failure; filters study and outcome; "App running" fetches the running containers' runs one by one, at most 50). `PickedApp`/`toPicked(run)` are run based. `/analysis/create` accepts `?run=` and `?generation_job_id=`, uses WP-15's moved `studies/builder/{ComposerSection,RunFormula,StartBar,ComposerSectionNav}`, and after a multi-app launch goes to `/analysis?launch=<launch_id>`. `RunOverview` counts a not-applicable tool (`skip_reason: not_applicable` or `tool_applicable: false`) as **not measured** (shown "had nothing to read (not measured)"), never as measured; re-running "unmeasured" still leaves not-applicable tools out (immutable tree). Truncation reads `summary.truncated_inputs = [validity.termination]`. Source LOC shows the model scope and `app_total`. Context scopes are `study | mine`. The analysis `[id]` header uses the local `setup.ts` (`runSetupItems`, `runTargetLines`: study › condition › pilot › repeat k/N · model · harness version · task (prose variant), linking `/studies/{id}`, `/samples/{id}`).
- **`lib/api/runs.ts`:** `RunTarget.kind = "run" | "code" | "deleted"` with `outcome, run_kind, task_title, task_slug, task_ref, stack_ref, study_id` (no `mode, app_name, app_slug, stack_slug, experiment_id`); list rows `launch_id`, `code_scope`, `code_scope_paths_sha` (no `batch_id`); `ListRunsParams.launch_id`; `createBatch` returns `{launch_id, run_ids}`; `RunSetup` is the v3 `RunSetupSchema` (no `mode, app_*, stack_slug, batch_id, experiment_*, bundle_key`); `SourceLoc.scope`/`app_total`; `ContextScope = "study" | "mine"`; `FileOrigin` and `RunFinding.file_origin`, `source_files[].origin`.
- **Models:** `ModelApplicationsSection`/`ModelUsageSection` read `listSamples({model: <model id>})`; rows link `/samples/{id}`, show run kind, harness preset, stack/prose/auth labels, outcome (`not usable` noted as attrition). "New" goes to `/studies/new?mode=app`. `ModelBehaviourPanel` renders the v3 behaviour payload per `harness@version` (no import from `applications/agent/format` any more).
- **Reports create:** tasks come from `listAllLibrary('task')` (`task_slug` + optional `task_version`), studies from `listStudies`; deep link `?type=…&study_id=…`; the study report sends `exclude_truncated` (default false = ITT) and the study (optional).
- **Grep gate** (`lib/api/legacy-imports.test.ts`): no WP-17 file imports `lib/api/{generation,experiments}.ts`; any other importer must be in its `PENDING` list (the v2 `components/{experiments,generator,templates}/**` and `routes/(app)/{experiments,sample-generator,templates}/**` that WP-14/WP-15 remove, plus `lib/api/client.ts` and `lib/api/types/experiments.ts`). Wave 3 empties the list and deletes the two modules.

## WP-10 decisions (study engine, results, runs.queries, docs index; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/study/{design,expand,order,plan,launch,results,stats,analysis_plan,automation,api,serialize,export,overrides,environment,deps,errors}.py` (`deps`, `environment`, `errors`, `stats`, `serialize`, `export`, `overrides` are new), `backend/generation/runs/queries.py`, `backend/generation/testing/fake_study.py` (new, WP-10's fake), **`backend/generation/models/study.py` (additive: `Study.cap_usd`, `Study.cap_tokens`) + `migrations/0025_wp10_study_cap.py`**, tests `backend/generation/tests/wp10_study/` (incl. `fixtures/expansion.json`), docs `docs/{STUDIES (new),README,ARCHITECTURE,USER_GUIDE,QUICKSTART,STUDY_TABLES,HUMAN_EVALUATION,api-reference}.md`, `backend/docs/services.py`. No new deps; no settings needed (the WP-10 block stays empty).

**Published interfaces:**
- **Ninja operation ids** (all under `/api/generation/`): `generation_studies_list` (GET `/studies/`), `generation_studies_create` (POST `/studies/`), `generation_studies_adhoc` (POST `/studies/adhoc/`), `generation_studies_cron_preview` (GET `/studies/cron-preview/?cron=&timezone=`), `generation_studies_get`, `generation_studies_update` (PATCH), `generation_studies_resolve`, `generation_studies_freeze`, `generation_studies_plan_cells` (GET `/studies/{id}/plans/{plan_hash}/cells/?condition=&unit=&page=&page_size=`), `generation_studies_plan_cell` (GET `.../cells/{cell_key}/`), `generation_studies_plan_diff` (POST `.../diff/` `{a, b}`), `generation_studies_launch`, `generation_studies_expand`, `generation_studies_pause`, `generation_studies_resume`, `generation_studies_cancel`, `generation_studies_top_up`, `generation_studies_add_replicates` (POST `/studies/{id}/add-replicates/` `{replicates: 1..5}`), `generation_studies_clone`, `generation_studies_condition_pause` / `_condition_resume`, `generation_studies_cells` (`?state=&condition=&unit=&stage=`), `generation_studies_results` (`?metric=&fingerprint=majority|all&sensitivity=exclude_truncated&baseline=<condition id>&common_cells=`), `generation_studies_attrition` (`?stage=`), `generation_studies_export` (zip), `generation_studies_prompt_overrides` / `_prompt_override_create`, `generation_studies_schedules` / `_schedule_create` / `_schedule_update` / `_schedule_delete` / `_schedule_run_now`, `generation_studies_post_runs` / `_post_run_create` (`{kind: analysis|patterns, trigger: manual|completed, config}`). **MCP (WP-02b integrations):** `list_studies` → `generation_studies_list`, `get_study` → `generation_studies_get`, `resolve_study` → `generation_studies_resolve`, `launch_study` → `generation_studies_launch`.
- **Error bodies**: `{"detail", "code", ...}`; codes `invalid_design` (400, with `path` and, when resolving failed, `issues`), `invalid_analysis_plan`, `plan_has_errors` (400, `data.issues`), `confirmation_required` (400, `data.{p90_total, expected_total, threshold}`), `plan_stale` / `plan_conflict` / `study_locked` / `not_launchable` / `not_piloting` / `pilot_active` / `pilot_gates_failed` (`data.gates`) / `not_running` / `not_paused` / `design_drifted` (409), `reason_required`, `invalid_cells`, `invalid_cap`, `invalid_replicates`, `invalid_results_query` (400).
- **Payloads** (`study/serialize.py`): `StudyOut` = every Study column incl. `cap_usd`, `cap_tokens`, `locked`, `active_plan` (`PlanSummary {id, plan_hash, design_hash, status, n_*, estimate, issues, resolver_version, created_at}`), `factors`; `StudyListOut` per 9.4; `PlanOut {plan_hash, design_hash, persisted, resolver_version, n_conditions, n_units, n_cells, n_cells_by_stage, n_blocks, unbalanced, estimate, issues, conditions, units, cells_sample}`; `ResolvedCellOut {cell_key, condition_index, unit_index, unit_key, repeat_index, stage, seed, sequence, block_index, levels, identity, estimate, excluded}`; `ResolvedCellDetailOut` per 9.4 plus `manifest`; `CellsPayload {cells: [{id, condition_id, condition_index, unit_id, unit_key, repeat_index, stage, block_index, sequence, seed, state, exclusion_reason, current_run, outcome, attempts, beyond_plan_runs, estimate_usd, cap_usd, cell_cap_usd, spent_usd, spent_list_usd, adaptive_n}], counts, states}`; results keys: `metric, sensitivity, itt, exclude_truncated, planned_repeats, stopped_early, common_cells, fingerprint_filter, baseline_condition_id, baseline_source, interim, balanced, leaderboard, matrix {units, cells}, paired {baseline_condition_id, pairing, status, correction, alpha, alternative, deviations, n_units, rows: [{condition_id, method: paired_crn|independent_repeats, primary_test: permutation|wilcoxon, n_pairs, mean_delta, wins, losses, ties, sign_test_p, wilcoxon_p, permutation_p, p_value, p_holm, p_adjusted, significant, ci95, untested_reason}], sensitivity}, gate {status, locked, deviations, stopped_early, planned_cells, multiplicity, significance_unreachable, exclusion_rate, differential_attrition, budget, complete_blocks}, attrition, by_factor {unbalanced, method, factors: {key: {level: {level, emm, n_conditions, n_measured_conditions}}}}, by_prose_variant, common_pass_rate, retest_agreement, issues, notes, generated_at (ISO string)`.
- **Python**: `study.plan.{resolve, resolve_bundle, freeze, persist_bundle, load_bundle, cached_bundle, clear_cache, PlanBundle, PlanConflictError, RESOLVER_VERSION}`; `study.launch.{create_study, update_study, launch, expand_pilot, top_up, add_replicates, clone, clone_with_diffs, launch_adhoc, pause, resume, cancel, pause_condition, resume_condition}` (`launch_adhoc(design, *, user, launch=True, name=None, confirm_usd=None) -> {study_id, plan_hash, run_ids, status, issues, estimate}`: the U1 automation ask is met); `study.results.{study_results, compute, ResultFilters}`; `study.analysis_plan.{normalize_analysis_plan, analysis_plan_hash, declared_metrics, plan_multiplicity, plan_excludes_truncated, significance_check, pattern_run_label}`; `study.automation.{fire_due_schedules, run_post_run, run_schedule_action, create_post_run, validate_schedule, cron_preview, next_runs, analysis_targets}`; `study.design.{parse_design, check_structure, validate_design, DesignError, ENUM_LEVELS, LIBRARY_FACTORS, SLOT_EXECUTORS}`; `study.expand.{expand, expand_grid}`; `study.order.{block_order, block_sequence, condition_order, unit_orders}`; `study.deps.{StudyDeps, get_deps, override_deps}`; `testing.fake_study.{FakeStudyEnv, use_fake_study_deps, fake_study_deps}`.
- **`runs.queries` (the swap):** `current_runs(study, stage="main")` is live and replaces `runs.view._current_runs_tmp`: per cell of the **active plan** (all cells when the study has none) and stage, the stored usable `StudyCell.current_run`, else the first usable run by `(created_at, pk)`, else the latest; `beyond_plan` runs never. `refresh_current(cell)` (row-locked) keeps a usable current run and maintains `StudyCell.state`/`exclusion_reason` (`queued`/`running`/`done`/`infra_pending`/`excluded:<cell reason>`; a cell excluded before any claim keeps `excluded`). Also `cells_for_stage(study, stage)`, `cell_current_ids(cells_qs)` (annotates `current_id`), `runs_for_study(study)`, `attrition(study, stage="main")` (rows `{condition_id, label, planned_cells, cells, cells_without_run, runs_by_outcome, usable, truncated, truncated_by_kind, excluded, excluded_by_reason, infra_error, infra_retries, cell_attempts, beyond_plan_runs, attrition_rate, install_share_by_stack}`).
- **Fixture**: `backend/generation/tests/wp10_study/fixtures/expansion.json` (factor-expansion cases with expected conditions/cells/blocks/unbalanced; pinned by `test_expand.py::test_shared_expansion_fixtures`). WP-15 copies it to `frontend/src/lib/components/studies/__fixtures__/expansion.json` (spec 12.3 WP-15 tests).

**Decisions:**
- **Bare refs are pinned at plan time** (open WP-01 follow-up #10, resolver part): a level `slug` takes the latest non-archived version visible to the study owner *when the plan resolves*; `slug@v` and `slug@v#hash12` are exact (a hash mismatch is `lint_unknown_library_ref`, error). Unknown / invisible refs are `lint_unknown_library_ref` (error) and drop the conditions that use them from the preview; an unknown model is a 400 `invalid_design`. `create_study` pins `Design.resource_policy` to the FK's pinned ref.
- **Defaults** for factors the design omits (recorded in every condition's levels and in `manifest.factors`): app `harness_preset=opencode-native`, `starter_level=minimal`, `style=model-choice`, `auth_mode=contract_bearer`, `guidance=none`, `prompt_set=app-default`, `prose_variant=standard`, `sampling_preset=provider-default`, `resource_policy=Design.resource_policy`, `network_policy=<policy>.network_policy`; slot `slot_executor=single_shot`, `guidance=none`, `sampling_preset=t07` (WP-04 note), `hole_mode=stub`, `keep_docstring=false`, `context_policy=related_files`, `prompt_set=slot-single-shot-default | slot-agent-default`, `harness_preset=opencode-native` for `restricted_agent`. `stack_profile` (app) and `model` are required.
- **Slot executor levels are `single_shot` and `restricted_agent`** (WP-12's names; kinds `slot_single_shot` / `slot_agent`). `context_policy` levels are a mode name or a `ContextPolicy` JSON; the pinned level is the canonical JSON of the full policy.
- **Plan contents**: pilot cells (`pilot.repeats` × the first `pilot.units` units in unit order) come first in sequence and block order; `block_index` is unique across stages (pilot blocks, then main blocks); every cell of one (condition, unit, repeat) shares the CRN seed across stages. Stimuli are rendered once per (condition, unit); the environment once per (condition, unit, brief). `plan.cells` excludes pairs that cannot be resolved (their issues name the cells). `MAX_CELLS = 20_000` (400 above).
- **Launch** materialises the plan's conditions and units and only the launched stage's cells (`expand_pilot` creates the main cells; with `reuse_as_main` it flips the pilot cells to `main` in place and skips their main twins). Runs are `bulk_create`d with `protocol`/`lane` set explicitly, then `build_manifest(run, resolved_cell)` per run, then `on_commit(enqueue_runs)`. Local endpoints get `pinned_served_name`/`pinned_digest` from `manifest.model.endpoint` (U1 execution ask). `Study.max_parallel`/`execution_options` come from the first cell's effective policy `concurrency`; `budget_basis`/`cap_basis` from the effective policy, `on_budget_exhausted` from the preset.
- **`Study.cap_usd` / `cap_tokens` (new columns, migration `0025_wp10_study_cap`)**: the cap resolved at launch: the request's `study_cap`, else the preset's `study.max_cost_usd`, with `auto` → `estimate.cap_usd` (else `worst_case_total`); `cap_tokens` = the preset's `study.max_total_tokens`. Not a locked field, so "raise cap" works on locked studies.
- **Confirmation**: `confirm_usd >= estimate.p90_total` is required when `p90_total > policy.estimate.require_confirm_above_usd`; scheduled `clone_and_run` confirms its own estimate.
- **`prereg_hash`** = `identity.prereg_hash(plan_hash, analysis_plan (normalized), stop_rules (policy.study.stop_rules dumps), pilot_gates (policy.pilot.gates dump), evaluation (Design.evaluation dump))`, computed for every launch; `locked_at` only for confirmatory studies.
- **Analysis plan v2** (`PLAN_VERSION = 2`): v2 keys kept for reports, plus `baseline {factor: level}` (the pre-registered baseline → `StudyCondition.is_baseline`), `itt` (default true), `exclusions.{truncated: false, exclusion_rate: 0.10, differential_attrition: 0.05}`, `sensitivity` (default `["exclude_truncated"]`), `estimand`, `random_effects`, `sequential {looks, alpha_spending, futility?, binding: false}`, `patterns {detector_set_hash | detectors, primary_features, normalizer_version, cluster_params, scope}` (one of the detector fields is required). `plan_multiplicity` defaults to `all`.
- **Results**: unit-level tests; `paired_crn` vs `independent_repeats` from `manifest.sampling.seed_forwarded` of both conditions; sign-flip permutation (exact ≤ 15 units, else 10 000 seeded draws) is the primary test below 20 units, with no paired bootstrap CI (`few_clusters`); `fingerprint=majority` (default) drops minority-fingerprint cells per (condition, unit) and is a gate deviation only when it actually dropped cells; complete blocks only (`budget.report.complete_blocks`, WP-11 ask), with `n_missing.incomplete_block`; the ITT zero for model failures applies only when the evaluation level measures functional checks.
- **Override result shape** (WP-03 ask honoured): `apply_override` passes `prompt_set_ref` (the single `prompt_set` level, when the design has one) and `role`, stores `owner_ref_base = result.owner_ref_base`, swaps `level_ref_base → level_ref_new` in the design (adding a fixed factor when the design used the default level).
- **Schedules**: `clone_and_run` clones exactly but with a fresh derived `base_seed` (`H({clone_of, at})`), so the replication is independent and has its own `plan_hash`; it skips the pilot when the source reached main. `add_replicates` is exploratory-only and refuses a drifted `config_fingerprint`.
- **Post-runs**: launch attaches `StudyPostRun(kind=patterns, trigger=completed, config={"detectors": "default"})` to slot studies **without** a PatternRun; `run_post_run` then calls `patterns.create_pattern_run(study, detectors=..., cluster_params=..., scope=..., user=..., queue=False)` and `patterns.run(...)` (detectors pinned after the variants exist). `NotImplementedError` from an area propagates (the worker puts the post-run back to pending); every other failure is recorded as `failed`.

2. Open follow-up #6: please freeze the plan child rows now that the resolver exists: `StudyCondition.{study, plan, index, label, condition_hash, levels, model, harness_preset, treatment_hash, is_baseline}`, `StudyUnit.{study, plan, index, unit_key, task_version, slot_target, unit_order}`, `StudyCell.{study, plan, condition, unit, repeat_index, block_index, seed, sequence}` (keep `StudyCell.stage` mutable for `reuse_as_main`, and `state`, `exclusion_reason`, `current_run`, money columns, `adaptive_n`, `StudyCondition.paused/pause_reason` mutable). ## WP-09 decisions (fix round 1), 2026-11-03

- **`reuse.find_reusable(run, suite, *, only_checks=None, probes=None, eval_level=None, mode="full", adopt=True)`** (published; additive keyword arguments, so existing callers still work). A candidate must now also: have an `eval_level` whose level-table stages **cover** the requested level (`reuse.covers(candidate, requested)`; `eval_level=None` means the run's manifest `planned_evaluation.eval_level`, else `security`); have the same `mode`; have the same probe selection (`stages.tester.detail.probes`); and must not have been evaluated with an `image_override` or `overlay_files` (`stages.tester.detail.image_override` / `.overlay`). An `overlay` request is never reused. Locks are compared **only when both runs have one**. A run with a blank lock reuses the source's evaluation and **adopts its lock** (`reuse.adopt_lock`: copies `resolved_lock_blob`, `lock_source`, `registry_snapshot_at`), so a reuse never re-resolves dependencies.
- **`reuse.selection_detail(only_checks=, probes=, image_override=, overlay_files=)`** is the dict that `suite.evaluate` writes to `stages.tester.detail` (normalised selections plus two booleans). Writers of `RunEvaluation` rows that may later be reused (FakeEvaluator, slot) should store the same keys.
- **`levels.stages_for` applies the 4.8 slot rules** when `spec.slot` is set. `slot.mode=static_only` switches the build off, and the dependency pass then turns off functional, probes, baseline, runtime adherence and DAST. When functional and build are on, probes are on and are limited to the target's `linked_probes` (new `levels.linked_probes_only(spec)`). This rule takes precedence over a `probes=False` override. Order: level table → overrides → slot rules → dependencies.

## WP-09 → WP-12: pass the full reuse key

In `slot.evaluate._reuse`, call `reuse.find_reusable(run, suite, only_checks=selection.only_checks, probes=selection.probes, eval_level=<the study's Design.evaluation.eval_level>, mode=<the mode used>)`. Without `probes`, a candidate stored with linked probes no longer matches (the fix is conservative: no reuse, never wrong data). The test double in `tests/wp12_slot/test_evaluate_batch.py` (`lambda run, suite, only_checks=None: ...`) has to accept `**kwargs` once you pass the new arguments.

## WP-06 fix round (budget accounting), 2026-11-03
- `budget_rules.prompt_text` now walks every string leaf (tool-call arguments, `tool_use.input`, Gemini `functionCall.args` as JSON); estimates for tool-heavy contexts rise accordingly (also `/messages/count_tokens` and `countTokens`).
- `Pricing.worst_case` prices reserved prompt tokens at `max(input, cache_write, cached_input)`; reservations (`reserved_usd`) are higher when cache writes cost more than input.
- `Ledger.settle(..., upstream_billed: bool = False)` (new keyword, backward compatible): a failed call without usage that the upstream accepted is settled at its reservation (`usage_estimated`, `cost_source: reservation`) and counted as failed. The gateway passes it for 2xx/3xx answers that then failed (stream drop, unreadable body, gateway error after forwarding). No calls.jsonl / state.json key changes.

## WP-14 decisions (Library, prompts, Budget preset editor: delivered), 2026-11-03

Files: `frontend/src/lib/api/v3/{types,library,harnesses}.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/api/v3/__fixtures__/*.json`, `frontend/src/lib/components/library/**` (components, `task.ts`, `policy.ts`, `spans.ts`, `diff.ts`, `variables.ts`, `preview.ts`, `skeletons.ts`, `slugify.ts`, `library.svelte.ts`, `validation.svelte.ts`, vitest `*.test.ts`), `frontend/src/routes/(app)/library/**`, `frontend/src/routes/(app)/templates/+page.ts` (redirect), `frontend/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (new "Library building blocks" section, `invalidateLibrary`, `/library/{kind}` Manage links, Budget preset vocabulary), `backend/generation/tests/wp14_frontend/test_fe_fixtures.py` (fixture drift test). A vitest test asserts every `KNOB_META` key lands in exactly one group.
