## WP-07 fix round (review findings)

- **Correction to the WP-07 decision "command timeout = `preset.command_timeout_s` else `time.command_timeout_s`":** it contradicted the 4 knob table. Now `time.command_timeout_s` overrides the preset when set; the preset's value applies only when the policy leaves it null (`harnesses.base.knobs`). Every tier sets it (120/300/600), so a controlled preset's 300 s no longer replaces a frugal budget's 120 s.
- **OpenCode command:** `--title llm-lab-run` (constant; was `'llm-lab run <run_id>'`). `command_sha` and `config_files_sha` are now equal for every run of a cell for all smoked adapters (`test_render_record_is_equal_for_every_run_of_a_cell`), honouring the WP-10 plan-time render ask. Fixture `fixtures/opencode-1.18.35/command.sh` updated.
- **Codex 0.166.0:** `time.command_timeout_s` is an unsupported knob (no default exec-timeout config key; checked in the binary), and `reasoning` is reported unsupported in `max_tokens` mode (only effort maps). Two more `open_questions` keep Codex blocked by the registry gate: no `apply_patch` tool for unknown slugs although the pinned instructions name it, and bundled system skills still listed (spec risk table: force function tools, else keep disabled with reason).

## WP-17 fix round: report configs' exclude_truncated (2026-11-03)

`lib/api/reports.ts`: `TaskComparisonConfig` and `StudyConditionsConfig` gain an optional `exclude_truncated?: boolean` (the backend already reads it, generators.py). New helper `excludeTruncatedConfig(on)` returns `{exclude_truncated: true}` or `{}`. Report configs send the key only when the researcher ticks the sensitivity option, so an untouched study report no longer triggers the "differs from the analysis plan's exclusions" validity note. `/reports/create` shows the option for task comparison, study conditions and study report; it notes that a locked plan's rule wins.

## WP-03 fix round (review findings), 2026-11-03

- **Override forks are per user** (`service._derive` / `fork_for_override`): a fork of someone else's entity reuses `<slug>--<hash8>` only when that row is the caller's own. A row owned by another user (even a shared one) or a system row is never reused. In those cases the fork goes to `<slug>--<hash8>-u<user_pk>`, then `-u<pk>-2`, `-3`, and so on. A study now pins only forks its owner owns, both the entity and the forked templates its prompt-set fork references. WP-10/WP-14: a fork's slug may end in `-u<pk>`, so do not parse fork slugs.
- **Imported resource policies are always `tier='custom'`** (`library/packages`): every imported row is user-owned, so the package's `tier` is never carried over. An import whose content equals a system preset still takes the reuse path to the preset row. Exports still write `tier` as information only.
- **`lint_library` / `lint_seed_data` no longer drop starters silently.**
  - A phase-A `compose.yaml` starter whose stack is neither in the data directory nor stored gets an `error` issue `lint_unknown_library_ref` (path `compose.stack`).
  - A phase-A starter whose `build.starters.compose` raises `NotImplementedError`/`LibraryError` or returns nothing gets `lint_starter_file_missing` (path `files`). That is an existing registered code, so no new code is needed from WP-01.
  - Phase-B starters that cannot be composed are not linted. They are listed as deferred, as `seed_library` lists them: `lint_seed_data(..., deferred=[])` is a new optional out-list, and the command prints `defer <key>: <reason>` plus `, N deferred (not linted)` in its summary line.
  - WP-04/WP-05: `lint_library` now fails while any phase-A starter in `starters/compose.yaml` cannot be composed.

## WP-08 → all: review-fix round (lost workspace, zero-call runs, re-eval refusal, slot seed)
- `POST /runs/{id}/evaluate/` (`runs.api.evaluate_run`) now also answers **409 `not_evaluable`** when the run's outcome was decided by the generation stage (`output_invalid`, `excluded`); frontends should show the detail instead of offering re-evaluation for those runs.
- `agentic.sandbox.workspace_loss(logs_dir, record)` (new, public): backends raise `SandboxError` (→ infra retry) when `exit.json` is missing, or `workspace.tar` is missing without the truncation manifest. `runner.infra_override` additionally forces an evaluated termination to `INFRA` when the result has no tar and is not truncated, and to `LLM_ERROR` (failed calls with upstream HTTP statuses) / `INFRA` (transport-only or zero calls) when no gateway call succeeded. `BUDGET` is exempt (the treatment's own cap).
- `agentic.workspace.slot_agent_seed` raises `runner.ConfigError` (run `excluded:config_invalid`) when `restricted_agent.hole_overlay` is missing, raises `NotImplementedError` or returns an empty overlay; it never seeds the unholed parent tree.
- `testing/fake_agentic.LocalSandboxBackend.drop_logs` (new): removes a `/logs` file after the entry script, to simulate a lost workspace.

## WP-05 fix round (review findings: base pins, lock completeness), 2026-11-03

- **`build.dockerfiles.allowed_bases_record()` → `{"sha", "refs", "tags"}` (published change):** `refs` are now the **digest-pinned** references (`python:3.13-slim@sha256:…`), `tags` the bare `image:tag` names (display only); `sha` is unchanged (it already committed to ref + digest, so `allowed_bases_sha` / `treatment_hash` do not move). Callers that pass `refs` on (WP-03 `prompts.context._free_choice_bases`, WP-10 `study.environment.allowed_bases_for` / `plan.py`) need no code change: the brief now shows the digests and `RunManifest.allowed_bases.refs` freezes them. `run-contract-free@1` asks the model to copy each base exactly as listed with its `@sha256:` digest.
- **New:** `dockerfiles.allowed_bases_from_refs(refs, *, sha=None)` (frozen manifest refs → `AllowedBase`s, verified against the sha), `dockerfiles.build_context_args(pins)`, `BaseCheck.pins` (`{familiar image:tag of a tag-only FROM: ref@digest}`) and `bases[].pinned`.
- **`build.context` (additive):** `prepare_build_dir` checks a free-choice Dockerfile against the allowlist **frozen in the run's manifest** (parent's for a variant; current file only when the run has none), never re-resolving a tag. `BuildContextReport.base_pins` + `BuildContextReport.build_context_args()` (`--build-context python:3.13-slim=docker-image://python:3.13-slim@sha256:…`); `to_dict()` adds `base_pins` (free-choice) and `bases.allowed_bases_source` (`manifest`|`allowlist`). `AllowedBasesChangedError(RuntimeError)` (`.reason == "allowed_bases_changed"`, constant `ALLOWED_BASES_CHANGED`): the manifest's refs are undigested and the allowlist sha changed since → **infra_error**, never `build_fail`. `build_identity(run)` adds `allowed_bases` (frozen sha) for free-choice stacks only (fixed-stack cache keys unchanged). `frozen_allowed_bases(run)` exposed. All re-exported by `runtime/services/build_context.py`.
- **Locks (`build.locks`, published):** `lock_covers(manifest_bytes, lock_bytes, *, ecosystem="pypi"|"npm") -> bool` (every direct requirement pinned `==` in the lock with a version its specifier accepts; npm: the lock's root package declares exactly the manifest's dependency maps) and `check_tree_locks(files, *, origins=None, platform_locks=(), index=None) -> [LockCheck]` (`status` `complete`|`incomplete`|`absent`, `reason` `stale_starter_lock`|`not_covered`, `source` `starter`|`platform`|`tree`). A starter-origin lock (bytes equal a checked-in starter lock per `lock/index.yaml`, or RunFile origin `starter`) next to a manifest it was not generated from is `stale_starter_lock` even if it happens to cover. `prepare_build_dir` records every manifest that has a lock in `BuildContextReport.locks` (→ `to_dict()["locks"]`, absent when there are none), `BuildContextReport.locks_incomplete`, and the note `lock_incomplete: <lock> (<reason>; <source> lock)` (constant `LOCK_INCOMPLETE`).

## WP-05 → runview owner / lead (`runtime/services/sample_builds.py`, `runtime/services/build_backend.py`): pin free-choice bases at build time

`build_backend.build(...)` has no way to pass BuildKit named contexts. Please add `build_contexts: Sequence[str] = ()` (extra `docker buildx build` args, inserted before the context path) and have `sample_builds.build_sample` pass `report.build_context_args()` after `prepare_build_dir`. Without it a tag-only `FROM python:3.13-slim` in a free-choice Dockerfile still resolves the tag at build time (Appendix A #1, 6.10 "re-evaluations never re-resolve"). `_context_key` already hashes `build_identity(run)`, which now includes the frozen `allowed_bases` sha for free-choice stacks, so cached images follow the pins. The benchmark path (`context_dir=`) has no report pins; it may call `build.context.frozen_allowed_bases(run)` + `dockerfiles.check_bases` itself or build unpinned (benchmark only).

## WP-05 → WP-09: incomplete locks are the lock step's job (supersedes the last bullet of the earlier WP-05 → WP-09 ask)

- Before measuring a build, run the lock step whenever `build.locks.check_tree_locks(files, origins=...)` (the collected tree's manifests/locks, RunView origins) reports an `incomplete` lock: write a platform lock (`ResolvedLock.files`, same path, e.g. `backend/requirements.lock`) generated from the **current** manifest. A minimal starter's hashed `requirements.lock` next to a manifest the model extended (pip `--require-hashes`: "all requirements must have their versions pinned with ==") is `stale_starter_lock`; a model-written lock missing a direct requirement is `not_covered`. `lock_covers(manifest, lock, ecosystem=)` checks the step's own output.
- After `prepare_build_dir`, `report.locks_incomplete` non-empty means the platform lock did not replace it: a resulting build failure must be recorded as `infra_error` / `not_measured` with reason `lock_incomplete` (store `report.to_dict()["locks"]` in `stages.build.detail`), **never** a model-attributed `build_fail`.
- `build.context.AllowedBasesChangedError` from `prepare_build_dir` is `infra_error` (reason `allowed_bases_changed`).

Add `!backend/generation/build/` directly after line 12 (`build/`) before the next commit, or `git add -f backend/generation/build` (all of `__init__, context, depcache, dockerfiles, locks, seeding, starter_rules, starters`); WP-05's wave cannot land otherwise.

## WP-16 decisions (sample view, slot picker, patterns; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `frontend/src/lib/api/v3/{samples,slots,patterns}.ts`, the `// --- WP-16 ---` block of `lib/api/v3/types.ts`, `lib/components/runs/**` (sample view), `lib/components/slots/**` (picker), `lib/components/patterns/**`, `routes/(app)/samples/**`, `routes/(app)/slots/**`, `routes/(app)/applications/**`, edits in `lib/components/applications/**`. Vitest: `runs/{format,refs}.test.ts`, `slots/{selection,parent-design,validation}.test.ts`, `patterns/format.test.ts`. No backend files, no migrations, no new deps.

- **Routes:** `/samples/[id]?tab=overview|manifest|files|trajectory|evaluation|calls|analyses|runtime`; `/samples` → `/applications`; `/applications` lists `GET /runs/?kind=app_agentic` (`?outcome=&usable=&model=&page=`); `/applications/[id]` and `/applications/[id]/failure` redirect (308) to `/samples/[id]`. `/slots` → `/slots/new`; `/slots/new` (no params: ParentPicker; `?parent=<run>[,<run>]`: freeze step; `?sets=<id>[,<id>]&set=<active>`: three-pane picker + targets tray); `/slots/[set]`; `/slots/targets/[id]?study=&page=`.
- **Exports for other WPs:**
  - `UseAsParentAction` (`$lib/components/runs`): props `{ runId: string; run?: {kind, status, usable, outcome} | null; label?; variant?: 'ghost'|'outline'|'default'; size?; hideWhenIneligible?: boolean }` → links to `/slots/new?parent=<id>`; eligible = completed, usable `app_agentic` with outcome `tested` (`canBeParent`). For WP-15's Results/Data run tables.
  - `PatternsTab` (`$lib/components/patterns/PatternsTab.svelte`, also the barrel `$lib/components/patterns`): props `{ studyId: string }` (spec 10.8). Self-loading; for WP-15's Patterns section (slot studies).
  - `PrevalenceTable { patternRunId; target?; feature?; conditionLabels?; targetLabels?; onaudit? }`, `ComparisonTable { patternRunId; feature? }`, `ClusterPanel { patternRunId; summary }`, `FeatureMatrix { studyId; targetId; features; conditionLabels? }`, `DetectorFromSelection { open (bindable); runId; path; span; language? }`, `SlotCodePane { setId; path; selection: Span | null; onselect({span, symbolId?}); file? }` (spec contract + optional preloaded file), `CodeViewer` (shiki, selectable gutter, symbol chips), `SampleLive` (SSE `run:<id>` + polling fallback), `slotStudyHref(setIds, targets)`.
  - **Slot-study link** (WP-15 builder input): `/studies/new?mode=slot&slot_sets=<uuid>,<uuid>&targets=<uuid>,<uuid>` — commas unescaped, only targets with `usable=true` (validation ok/uninformative) and the sets they belong to.
  - **Types** (WP-16 block): runs (`RunListOut` incl. optional `task`/`task_title`, `RunOut`, `RunAttemptOut`, `RunEvaluationOut`, `EvalResultOut`, `AdherenceRecordOut`, `RunManifestOut`, `RunFileOut`, `RunTrajectoryOut`, `RunCallOut`, `RunEvent`, `RUN_EVENT_TYPES`, `RUN_OUTCOMES`, `USABLE_OUTCOMES`), slots (`Span`, `SymbolEntry`, `EndpointEntry`, `ComponentEntry`, `SlotAddress`, `ContextPolicy`, `DEFAULT_CONTEXT_POLICY`, `SlotTargetIn`, `SlotTargetPreviewOut`, `SlotTargetOut`, `SlotTargetDetailOut`, `SlotSetOut`, `ParentSelection`, `SlotParentRow`, `SlotVariantRow`, `AdhocStudyIn/Out`, …), patterns (`PatternRunOut`, `PatternSummary`, `PrevalenceRow`, `ComparisonRow`, `ClustersOut`, `DetectorAuditOut`, …). WP-17's `types/events.ts` re-exports `RunEvent`/`RUN_EVENT_TYPES`; the names are kept.
- **Client modules:** `samples.ts` (`listSamples`, `getSample`, `getSampleManifest`, `listSampleFiles(id, scope, origin?)`, `getSampleFileContent`, `getSampleTrajectory`, `listSampleCalls`, `listSampleEvaluations`, `evaluateSample`, `retrySample`, `cancelSample`, `sampleEvidenceUrl`, `subscribeRun`, `buildQuery`), `slots.ts` (every `slots_*` operation + `generateParentApp` = `POST /studies/adhoc/` with `launch: true`), `patterns.ts` (every `patterns_*` operation + `patternExportUrl`). All take `{signal}`.
- **Validity in the UI:** `agent_pass_rate: null` renders "not measured" (never 0 %); evaluation `error`/`not_measured` never shows a pass rate; infra and flaky checks are listed apart and never counted; skipped probes show their reason; prevalence rows with `n_measured = 0` render "not measured"; detector errors, n/a and missing outputs are counted beside the measured n (never folded into absent); p only for planned rows, BH q for exploratory rows, both withheld while `interim`; no severity is computed or shown in patterns.
- **Slot picker semantics:** untouched form fields are omitted from `SlotTargetIn` (the server fills kind defaults and lists them in `preview.defaults`; the form labels hole mode / keep docstring / context policy "default (can be varied in the study)"). Regions are sent with whole-line byte offsets; drags snap to whole lines and, by default, widen to the boundaries of any symbol they cut. Preview and create send `content_sha` + `anchors` (client `textSha` = `identity.text_sha`, `anchorsFor` = `slot/selection.anchors_for`) as WP-12 asked. Saving is blocked while the preview has an `error` issue (e.g. `slot_leak_verbatim`). "Add matching part" sends the other frozen sets of the session (same task), else `"same_task"`. Pending targets are re-read every 10 s.
- **Generate a parent app:** a one-cell exploratory app design (every factor `fixed`, empty ones omitted so `APP_DEFAULTS` apply; budget preset `frugal` by default; evaluation `security` by default; repeats 1; random base seed unless given) posted to `/studies/adhoc/`; the dialog follows `run:<id>` with a 10 s poll and opens `/slots/new?parent=<run>` once the run is a tested, usable app.
- **`applications/**` cleanup:** deleted the v2-only components (JobsTable, JobCards, ApplicationHeader/Overview/Prompts/Files/Dependencies/ApiScan/Artifacts/Iterations/Metrics/Container/Tools, AgentRunSummary, JobProvenance, `agent/**`, `insights/**`; this also resolves the "healing" sections of `ApplicationContainer`/`EvaluationCard` noted by the U1 fixer) and the v2 helpers in `utils.ts` (`copyText`, `timeAgo`, `httpColors` and the formatter re-exports stay). Kept: `AppRuntimeControls`, `ApplicationAnalyses`, `ApplicationLogs` (props unchanged: `jobId` = a v3 run id) and `SameSetupPanel`, re-pointed to `getRunReplicates` (prop `jobId` kept for WP-17's `RunOverview`; links go to `/samples/<run>`; it imports `studies/variability/variability.ts`).
- **Detector from selection:** drafts via `patterns_detector_from_selection`, then either saves v1 directly (`createLibraryItem('detectors', …)`, private) or opens WP-14's `/library/detectors/new?prefill=<json>`.
- Wave 3 (seam only): the Playwright smoke "sample → regenerate a part → target saved and validated".

The U1 misc_apps note assigns to "Frontend (WP-16)" `lib/api/users.ts` / `routes/(app)/users/[pk]/+page.svelte` (`counts.experiments` → `counts.studies`, kind `experiment` → `study`), `ModelResultsTab.svelte` (`repair_rate`/`continuations_used`/`by_mode`) and `rankings.ts` filters. None of these files is in WP-16's 12.1 paths (routes `samples`/`slots`/`applications`, components `runs`/`slots`/`patterns`/`applications`, `api/v3/{samples,slots,patterns}.ts`); please assign them (WP-17 owns the neighbouring models/consumer modules).

### WP-12 fix round (review findings 1-4), 2026-11-03

Files: `slot/{assemble,validate,parents,api}.py`, `slot/executors/restricted_agent.py`, tests in `tests/wp12_slot/`. No migration, no model change.
- **Assembly follows the run's hole mode.** `assemble_tree` splices every text replacement at the span when the address has one; whole-file replacement happens only for `{path: text}` (file targets, removed whole-file components) or span-less addresses. A whole-file component under a `stub` condition now keeps its imports/exports (single shot, restricted agent and `spliced_files`).
- **Restricted agent, allowlisted extra files (§7.6).** Edits to allowlisted files other than the slot file (e.g. a `requirements.txt` the researcher allowed) are never out-of-slot edits, so `edit_policy=fail` no longer rejects them and `revert` no longer drops them. When such files changed, the variant's replacement is the files form `{slot path: full final text, other allowlisted path: text}` (the slot file's text outside the span is still checked byte-for-byte). `new_dependencies` is recorded as before. New helper `assemble.decode_replacement(part, text, *, files_form)`; `recorded_replacement` recognises this form (keys must be allowlisted and include the slot file). **WP-13 (patterns):** `SlotVariantResult.replacement_blob` can now be such JSON for span kinds too (`replacement_sha` is then `H(files)`); `patterns/variants.py` already re-locates the part in the assembled file, only `export.py`'s raw `variants/<target>/<run>.<ext>` would show the JSON.
- **Validation counts only measured failures on the holed tree.** `holed_fail[check]` is `True` only for `fail` or `skipped` with `skipped_due_to` (a failed `depends_on`), `False` for `pass`; a missing check, `error` or other `skipped` gets no entry and cannot be discriminating (dropped). If the holed evaluation measured none of the checks that pass 3/3 on the original, `validation.status = "error"`.
- **Parent pool restricted before ranking.** `slot.parents.pool_for/pool/draw/draw_with_record` take a keyword `base: QuerySet[GenerationRun] | None` (default: all runs; existing callers unchanged). `POST slots/parents/draw/` (`slots_parents_draw`) and `GET slots/parents/` (`slots_parents_list`) pass the user's visible runs, so `k`, `pool_size` and the seeded order hold for that user (response shape unchanged).

New test: every `mass_assignment` probe's `params.field` is absent from the endpoint's `request_schema.properties`.

## WP-04 → WP-09: setup captures also substitute in JSON body values

Extends the WP-04 → WP-09 conventions: a captured `{name}` is substituted in the path **and** in any request JSON string value that is exactly `{name}` (setup steps and the check's own request). `team_roles_multitenant` `members.admin_cannot_add_owner` needs it: `user_b` creates its own org, reads its own username from `GET /api/orgs/{b_oid}/members` (`capture: {b_name: "$.items[0].username"}`), then `user_a` adds it as admin with `json: {username: "{b_name}", role: admin}`.

## WP-11 decisions (fix round 2), 2026-11-03

- **WP-10 → WP-11 study-cap ask honoured:** `claims.study_cap(study)` returns `Study.cap_usd` when it is set; when it is NULL, the old rule applies (the policy's `study.max_cost_usd`, else `active_plan.estimate.cap_usd`). The token cap is `Study.cap_tokens` when set, else the policy's `study.max_total_tokens`. Admission, `report.study_payload` (cap/remaining) and `stop_rules` (spend_fraction) all read the cap through these functions, so a cap typed at launch or a later "raise cap" (an update of `Study.cap_usd`) takes effect on the next tick.
- **`claims.admit(run_ids, *, now=None, lane_of=None)`** (additive keyword): with `lane_of(run_id) -> lane key`, admission is head-of-line per lane. Once a run is refused, the later runs of its lane get no decision and no reservation. The scheduler passes its lane keys, so every admitted row is claimed in the same transaction.
- **Ledger:** `reconcile()` no longer settles a pending run whose open reservation belongs to its current attempt. Re-admitting an attempt that already holds an open reservation reuses that reservation; it is not counted twice. `settle_attempt` no longer treats "released, no live usage" as settled: a final record found later still writes the `adj:` row, so a single-shot attempt's spend is not recorded as a silent 0.

## WP-10 decisions (fix round), 2026-11-03

- **Replicate cells (`add_replicates`) are marked `StudyCell.beyond_plan = True`** (new additive column, migration **`0026_wp10_cell_beyond_plan`**, depends on `0025_wp10_study_cap`; integrator renumbers). Their runs stay `beyond_plan=True`. `runs.queries.refresh_current` / `cell_current_ids` pick a cell's current run among runs whose `beyond_plan` equals the cell's, so a finished replicate cell is `done` with its own current run (it no longer falls back to `planned`). **`cells_for_stage(study, stage, *, beyond_plan=False)`** (additive keyword; `None` = both, `True` = only replicates): by default the planned sample only, so `current_runs`, `results` (`n_missing.pending`) and `attrition` (`planned_cells`, `cells_without_run`) leave replicate cells out; `attrition.beyond_plan_runs` now also counts the replicate cells' runs. Top-ups of a replicate cell are `beyond_plan` too.
- **`CellsPayload.cells[]` gains `beyond_plan: bool`** and lists replicate cells (for a replicate cell, `attempts` counts its own runs and `beyond_plan_runs` is 0). List-page `cells_done`/totals count planned cells only.
- **Study cap:** `launch` returns `resolved_cap = budget.claims.study_cap(study)` (the cap the scheduler enforces; WP-11 reads `Study.cap_usd` first since its fix round 2). `auto` without an estimate `cap_usd` now resolves to no USD cap (`None`), the same as claims' fallback, instead of `worst_case_total`. Cross-WP test `wp10_study/test_launch.py::test_launch_cap_is_the_cap_claims_enforce`.
- Done-when test `wp10_study/test_done_when.py`: the Done-when design is launched and one block runs through `agentic.dispatch.run_stage` (FakeHarness, LocalSandboxBackend/FakeGateway) and a FakeEvaluator eval stage ending in `transitions.finish_stage`; cell state, `current_runs`, `by_factor` and attrition are asserted.

## WP-10 → WP-15: `CellsPayload` cell `beyond_plan`

Please add `beyond_plan: boolean` to the cells payload row type and badge replicate cells ("replicate, not in the planned sample") in the cells grid; they appear with `stage: "main"`.

## WP-10 → WP-11 (optional): replicate cells in block accounting

`budget.report._cells` / `complete_blocks` / `block_status` include replicate cells (`StudyCell.beyond_plan=True`); `complete_blocks` reads only `beyond_plan=False` runs, so replicate blocks never count as complete. Results are unaffected (they only use planned cells), but `block_status.total` counts the replicate blocks. Consider filtering `beyond_plan=False` cells there. → done by WP-11: `block_status` and `complete_blocks` now drop `beyond_plan=True` cells (also from a `cells=` argument), so replicates neither add blocks to `total` nor make a planned block with the same `block_index` incomplete; spend views (`study_payload` remaining/ETA) still include them. Test `wp11_budget/test_report_blocks.py`.

## WP-15 decisions (Study builder, record, operations, budget; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Files: `frontend/src/lib/api/v3/{studies,budget}.ts`, the `// --- WP-15 ---` block of `lib/api/v3/types.ts`, `lib/components/studies/**` (builder, study, record, results, monitor, budget, automation, variability, shared, `format.ts`, `spend.ts`, `index.ts`, `__fixtures__/`), `lib/components/layout/SpendChip.svelte`, the header mount in `routes/(app)/+layout.svelte`, `routes/(app)/{studies,budget}/**`, `lib/nav.ts`, redirects in `routes/(app)/{experiments,sample-generator}/**`; backend tests `backend/generation/tests/wp15_frontend/` (expansion fixtures + TS-contract drift test). Removed: `lib/components/{generator,experiments}/**`, the v2 `routes/(app)/{experiments,sample-generator}` pages, and WP-14's `lib/components/templates/slugify.ts` shim (as asked).

**Published interfaces:**
- **TS types** (WP-15 block of `lib/api/v3/types.ts`): `Design`, `FactorSpec`, `FactorKey`, `FactorRole`, `UnitSpec`, `GridSpec`, `EvaluationSpec`/`EvaluationOverrides`/`EvalLevel`/`EVAL_LEVELS`, `Estimate` (+ `CellEstimate`, `ConditionEstimate`, `EstimateFit`, `EstimateTime`), `StudyOut`, `StudyListOut`, `StudyListQuery`, `StudyCreateIn`/`StudyPatchIn`, `PlanSummary`, `PlanOut`, `ResolveIn`, `ResolvedConditionOut`, `ResolvedUnitOut`, `ResolvedCellOut`, `ResolvedCellDetailOut`, `CellDiffOut`, `LaunchIn`/`LaunchOut`, `PilotGate`, `ExpandOut`, `TopUpIn`/`TopUpOut`, `CloneIn`, `StudyCellOut`/`StudyCellsPayload`, `AttritionRow`, `LeaderboardRow`, `PairedRow`, `ByFactorOut`, `StudyResultsOut`, `PromptOverrideIn`/`StudyPromptOverrideOut`, `ScheduleIn`/`ScheduleOut`/`ScheduleAction`, `PostRunIn`/`PostRunOut`, `CronPreviewOut`, `AdhocIn`/`AdhocOut`; budget `StudyBudgetOut`, `BudgetMeOut`, `BudgetHeaderOut`, `SpendCapIn`/`SpendCapPatch`/`SpendCapOut`, `SpendLedgerEntryOut`, `CostPriorOut`, `EstimateIn`; SSE `StudyEvent` and its members `StudyRunEvent`, `StudyTickEvent`, `StudyStateEvent`, `StudyPostRunEvent`, `SpendEvent`, `BudgetAlertEvent`, `StoppedEvent`, `PausedEvent`, `STUDY_EVENT_TYPES`, plus `TrajectoryStepEvent`/`RunUsageEvent` (names kept for WP-17's `types/events.ts`). `ParentSelection`/`PoolQuery` are declared once, in the WP-16 block (identical shapes; the WP-15 copy was dropped). `backend/generation/tests/wp15_frontend/test_ts_contract.py` fails when `study_out`, `study_list_item`, `budget.report.study_payload` or `header_payload` send a key the TS type lacks (or the TS type requires one they never send).
- **Clients**: `studies.ts` (one helper per 9.4 route: `listStudies`, `createStudy`, `createAdhocStudy`, `cronPreview`, `getStudy`, `updateStudy`, `resolveStudy`, `freezePlan`, `listPlanCells`, `getPlanCell`, `diffPlanCells`, `launchStudy`, `expandStudy`, `pauseStudy`, `resumeStudy`, `cancelStudy`, `topUpStudy`, `addReplicates`, `cloneStudy`, `setConditionPaused`, `getStudyCells`, `getStudyResults`, `getStudyAttrition`, `studyExportUrl`, `listPromptOverrides`, `createPromptOverride`, schedules and post-runs); `budget.ts` (`estimateBudget`, `getStudyBudget`, `getStudyLedger`, `getMyBudget`, `getBudgetHeader`, caps CRUD, `listCostPriors`; re-exports WP-14's `getBudgetKnobs`/`explainPolicy`).
- **Fixtures**: `lib/components/studies/__fixtures__/expansion.json` is a verbatim copy of WP-10's `tests/wp10_study/fixtures/expansion.json` (a backend test fails when the copy drifts; re-copy after changing WP-10's file); `expansion-conditions.json` (WP-15) adds full expected condition lists and refused designs, run through `expand_grid` by the backend test and through the client mirror `builder/expansion.ts` by vitest.
- **Routes**: `/studies` (`?q&status&mode&kind&page`), `/studies/new` (`?draft=<id>` resumes a saved draft and is set by the first save; `?clone=<id>[&clone_mode=latest_versions]`; `?mode=slot&slot_sets=a,b&targets=x,y`; `?model=<id|slug>` pre-fills the model factor), `/studies/[id]` (`?tab=summary|method|results|patterns|data|operations`; aliases `design`/`plan` → method, `monitor`/`budget`/`automation` → operations, `overview`/`abstract` → summary, `runs` → data), `/studies/[id]/cells/[cell]` (`?plan=<hash>`, default the active plan), `/budget`. Redirects (308): `/experiments` → `/studies`, `/experiments/new` → `/studies/new`, `/experiments/<id>` → `/studies/<id>`, other `/experiments/**` → `/studies`, `/sample-generator` → `/studies/new` (keeps `?model=`), `/sample-generator/batches[/<id>]` → `/studies?kind=exploratory`.
- **Component exports** (`$lib/components/studies`): see the early announcement above for the moved generic pieces (`studies/shared/*`, `studies/builder/{ComposerSection,ComposerSectionNav,RunFormula,StartBar,SummaryIssues,issues}`, `studies/study/{StudySection,StudyFigure,tabs}`, `studies/variability/*`, `studies/automation/*`, `studies/monitor/CapacityStrip`). `refreshSpendChip()` (`$lib/components/studies/spend`) makes the header chip refetch (dispatches `llmlab:spend-changed`).

**Decisions:**
- **Plan lifecycle lives in `BuilderState`** (`builder/builder.svelte.ts`, spec 10.7), not in `PlanRail`: design edits are saved 500 ms after the last change (resolve reads the *saved* design), then resolved with `persist=false`; a newer edit aborts the request in flight and stale answers are dropped; text edits autosave after 2 s and never postpone a sooner design save. `PlanRail`'s props are therefore `{state: BuilderState, onfix, oncell, onfreeze, onstart}` instead of 10.8's `{studyId, design, onplan}`. Start needs a frozen plan whose `plan_hash`/`design_hash` equal the current plan's and the saved design's, with no error issue.
- **A draft is created only once the server can accept it** (`POST /studies/` validates the design: a model, a stack and ≥ 1 task in app mode, ≥ 1 target in slot mode); before that the rail shows the client checks. No sessionStorage.
- **Budget presets are never edited in place**: `ResourcePolicyPicker` edits a draft copy; "Save as preset" (and the quick fixes `set_study_cap` / `set_knob`) create a private preset (or version+1 of the user's own preset) and pin it in `Design.resource_policy`. `set_repeats` and `move_prose_variant` are applied to the draft directly.
- **Prompt edits** go through `POST /studies/{id}/prompt-overrides/` with the whole edited PromptTemplate (`template_ref` of the clicked span, or a role of a prompt-set level, `owner_kind: "prompt"`); after it the builder reloads the study (the server swapped the factor level) and re-resolves.
- **Model factor levels** are `LLMModel.model_id`s (picked with the catalog `ModelPicker`, tool calling required in app mode); `:free` models show a rate-limit warning. Slot executor levels are `single_shot` / `restricted_agent` (WP-10/12 names).
- **Patterns section** mounts WP-16's `lib/components/patterns/PatternsTab.svelte` (`{studyId}`) through `import.meta.glob`, so the record builds whether or not that file exists yet.
- **SpendChip** is mounted from `(app)/+layout.svelte` as a fixed overlay over the header bar (left of the theme toggle and account menu, hidden below `sm`), because `AppHeader` has no actions slot (ask below). It turns into a warning badge when a study is paused for `budget`, `quota` or `spend_cap`, and warns at ≥ 80 % of the period cap.
- Missing values are `—` everywhere (spend, rates, estimates, exclusion rate); a measured zero prints `$0.00`, never "Free".

- **WP-09 → WP-12 (pass the full reuse key) → done by WP-12:** `slot.evaluate._reuse(run, suite, selection, mode)` calls `reuse.find_reusable(run, suite, only_checks=selection.only_checks, probes=selection.probes, eval_level=selection.eval_level, mode=<the mode actually used after the overlay fallback>)`. `selection.eval_level` is the manifest's `planned_evaluation.eval_level`, else the study's `Design.evaluation.eval_level`, else `security`. The test double in `test_evaluate_batch.py` takes `**kwargs` and asserts the four keys.
- **WP-11 → WP-12 (slot calls in the ledger) → done by WP-12:** new `single_shot.record_spend(run, usage, *, serving_tier)`, called from `finalize_answer` (so single shot and batch both use it) right after `record_call`. It writes `ledger.record_usage(run, attempt=attempt_no(run), source="gateway_final" | "batch", amount_usd, list_cost_usd, tokens_in, tokens_out, requests=1, dedupe_key=f"call:{run.pk}:{attempt}:1")` and then `ledger.settle_attempt(run, attempt=..., final=FinalUsage(<the same usage>))` (adjust 0; the reservation is released). Invalid outputs are recorded too. It is best effort: an exception is logged and the reconciler repairs from `RunAttempt.usage`. The settlement in `finish_stage` is then a no-op. This supersedes "no separate `record_usage` row" in the WP-12 decisions above.

The helper `slot.api.parent_levels(run)` is public.

- **U1 human_eval → runtime owner (hosted-app credentials) → done by WP-02a:** `container_service._do_build` (run-backed containers) calls the new `_run_env(run)`: `evaluation.credentials(run)` → `env = {ADMIN_PASSWORD, SECRET_KEY}` and `metadata["runtime_env"] = {"source": "evaluation.credentials", "admin_password_sha256": password_fingerprint(pw)}` (constants from `human_eval.services.credentials`; the password/secret never go into metadata/config). Any exception (`CredentialsUnavailableError`, `NotImplementedError`, other) or an incomplete record → no env, no stamp. A stale `runtime_env` stamp from an earlier start is always dropped before the new start, so a rebuild without a record is `not_measured`. `DATABASE_URL` is not set: the hosted start uses the image default until WP-09's start stage defines one (then reuse the same helper). Tests: `test_hosted_run_gets_record_credentials_and_a_hash_stamp`, `test_hosted_run_without_a_record_starts_without_credentials_or_stamp` (3 error kinds).

## WP-01 follow-up #7 (`PatternRun.detector_set_hash`) → done by WP-13

→ done by WP-13: the formula now lives in `identity.py`: `identity.detector_set_hash(pairs)`, with `pairs` = `(ref, full content_hash)`, and `identity.DETECTOR_SET_HASH_VERSION = 1`. It returns `H({"version": 1, "detectors": sorted(unique([slug@version, content_hash]))})`. A pinned ref is reduced to `slug@version`; a malformed ref or a hash that is not a sha raises `ValueError`. This is the same formula that `patterns.detectors.detector_set_hash(specs)` already used, and that function now delegates to the identity one, so no stored or locked value changes. `PatternRun.derive_fields` (in `models/slot.py`; method only, no schema change, no migration) derives the hash on create from the pinned `detectors`. It resolves every ref to its `PatternDetector` row in one query to get the full content hash. Then:
- a supplied hash that does not match is a `ValidationError` on `detector_set_hash`;
- a ref that does not resolve (unknown `slug@version`, or a `#hash12` that does not match) is a `ValidationError` on `detectors`;
- a row with an **empty** `detectors` list keeps whatever hash was supplied. This keeps bare factory and test rows valid (`PatternRunFactory`, wp01/wp10 tests); real runs always carry detectors.

`analysis_plan.patterns.detector_set_hash` must equal this value. Tests: `backend/generation/tests/wp13_patterns/test_detector_set_hash.py`.

Once a `RunManifest` exists, `GenerationRun.save()` refuses (`ImmutableVersionError`) a change to any of them unless the new value is non-empty and agrees with the manifest (`agentic.manifest.run_column_mismatches`: library FKs by pinned ref, model by slug, the rest by value), so clearing is refused and empty → manifest value is allowed. This costs one extra query per `save()` that touches these columns (none for `update_fields` without them), plus the manifest read only when a column actually changed. `QuerySet.update()` is **not** guarded for these columns, because several consumer tests re-point runs with bulk updates (export, u1_runview, human_eval, wp12 helpers). Production code does not bulk-update them.

## WP-09 decisions (asks round: EvalSuite v2 delivered; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

Every module of `generation/evaluation/` is implemented; nothing raises `NotImplementedError` any more. Details in `docs/EVALUATION.md`.

- **`suite.evaluate(run, suite, *, only_checks=None, probes=None, mode="full", image_override=None, overlay_files=None, eval_level=None, evaluation=None, runtime=None, force_rebuild=False, cancelled=None, retest_of=None) -> RunEvaluation`** (the keywords after `overlay_files` are additive). Stages lock → build → start → tester (+ probes) → adherence → baseline per `levels.stages_for(spec_for(run, suite))`; the spec is the study's `Design.evaluation` (overrides, slot rules) with the level from the manifest's `planned_evaluation` (or the reference pin `execution.eval_level`). `stages.tester.detail` = `reuse.selection_detail(...)` plus the tester versions; the infra checks (`health`, `index_served`, `spa_fallback`) always run with `only_checks`. Mode `static_only` runs no app; `overlay` skips lock and build and starts `image_override` with `overlay_files` copied in.
- **`suite.run_eval_stage(run)`** (eval worker): suite = `execution.reeval_suite` | `execution.eval_suite` (reference runs) | `execution.slot_validation_suite` | manifest `planned_evaluation.eval_suite` | study design. Slot runs (`slot_*`, and `reference` with `reference_of ∈ {slot_original, slot_holed}`) go to `slot.evaluate.evaluate_variant(run)`; `slot.validate.on_reference_evaluated(run)` is called after the stage finished; `metrics.eval.slot_pass_rate = slot.evaluate.slot_pass_rate(evaluation, run.slot_target)`. Other runs reuse an identical tree's evaluation when the effective policy has `caches.reuse_eval_by_tree` (a copy row with `reused_from`, note `eval_reused`), else `evaluate`. Notes added with `agentic.validity.add_notes`: `lock_source:<model|platform>`, `adherence:<status>` (warn when violated), `eval_reused`. Starter-baseline, oracle and conformance references call `baseline.on_reference_evaluated`. The stage never calls `transitions.infra_retry`.
- **`suite.outcome_of(evaluation) -> (outcome, reason)`** (6.12): lock/build `fail` → `build_fail` (reasons `lock_unresolvable`, `base_not_allowed`, `""` for an ordinary build failure); start `fail` → `start_fail`; measured → `tested`; a level without the tester (`none`/`static`/`build`, or `static_only`) → **`tested` with `outcome_reason=not_tested:<level>`** and `result.measured=false` (no pass rate; deviation: 6.12 has no outcome for "built but not tested"); any stage `error` → `infra_error` with that stage's reason (`lock_infra`, `build_infra`, `allowed_bases_changed`, `variant_not_assembled`, `lock_incomplete`, `tester_crash`, `image_digest_mismatch`, `postgres_sidecar_unavailable`, ...). `RunEvaluation.status`: `measured` (tester ran), `not_measured` (build/start fail, level), `error` (infra).
- **`acceptance.compile(task_version, auth_mode, stack, *, probes="task", base_url=..., only=None) -> TesterPlan`** (`TesterPlan(AcceptancePlan)` adds `health_path`, `signed_routes`, `mock_host`, `mock_port`, `has_frontend`; secrets never in the plan), `compile_content(content, auth_mode, stack_content, ...)`, `plan_json(plan)`, **`checks_for_endpoints(task_version, endpoint_ids, *, categories=None)`**. Generated check ids: `health`, `index_served`, `spa_fallback`, `<ep>.ok`, `<ep>.anon_401`, `<ep>.other_user`, `<ep>.non_admin_403`, `<ep>.invalid.{missing,too_long,enum}`, `<ep>.duplicate`, `<ep>.oversize`, `<ep>.page_size`, `<ep>.filter.<param>`, `<ep>.search`, `<ep>.persists`. Endpoints without `request_schema` get bodies from the written entity's fields (and no validation checks). `COMPILER_VERSION=1`; snapshots for 3 tasks × 3 auth modes in `tests/wp09_eval/snapshots/`.
- **`lock.resolve_lock(run, *, runner=None) -> ResolvedLock`**; `LockUnresolvableError` (reason `lock_unresolvable`), `LockInfraError`; `lock.tree_files(run)`, `lock.load_lock(run)`.
- **`baseline`**: `ensure_baseline(starter, task_version, auth_mode, suite, *, runtime=None) -> EvalBaseline | None` (**`None`** when the starter's evaluation is not measured: no baseline row, `baseline_pass_rate` stays `None`), `queue_baseline(...) -> GenerationRun | None` (enqueue on commit; for launch), `queue_oracle_runs(task_version, stacks, *, user=None, suite=None, auth_mode="contract_bearer") -> list[run id]`, `reference_run(...)`, `reference_tree(task_slug, stack_slug)`, `on_reference_evaluated(run, evaluation)`, `oracle_key(stack, kit=False)`. `TaskSpecVersion.oracle` keys: `<stack pinned ref>` (oracle) and `kit:<stack pinned ref>` (conformance kit).
- **`conformance.coverage() -> {framework: bool}`**, `status_rows()`, `run_kit(stack, suite)`, `validate_oracle(task_version, stack, suite, runs=3)`, `validate_probe_kinds()`, `KIT_TASKS`.
- **Probes**: `evaluation/probes_catalog.yaml` (17 kinds, `requires` tags), `probes.kind_validated(kind)`, `probes.applicable(content, existing=)`, `probes.catalog_rows()`. Mutant pairs: `evaluation/conformance/probe_mutants/` (one stdlib reference app of the probe-kit task `kit_task.yaml`, `LLMLAB_VULN=<kind>` / `LLMLAB_MUTANT=bare|wrong_status|no_validation|crash|idor`). `manage.py oracle_validate --kit` validated all 17 kinds (`conformance/probe_validation.json`, tied to tester v1 / probe rules v1); an unvalidated kind compiles `disabled` → `skipped(disabled)`.
- **Adherence**: `adherence.check_adherence(run, runtime=None, *, observed=None) -> AdherenceRecord`; `adherence.not_measured()`.
- **Metrics**: `metrics.compute(...) -> EvalMetrics`, `metrics.eval_summary(evaluation, *, slot_pass_rate=None) -> EvalSummary | None`, `metrics.run_metrics(run) -> RunMetrics`. `common_pass_rate` stays `None` in a single evaluation (it needs the study's auth modes: results port).
- **Retest**: `retest.run_retests(study, *, fraction=None, runtime=None) -> list[evaluation id]` (rows carry `retest_of` from creation; final rows are frozen).
- **Runtime seam** `evaluation/runtime.py`: `EvalRuntime` protocol, `DockerEvalRuntime` (production), `LocalProcessRuntime` (bundled stdlib apps with a `llmlab-local.json` marker only; refuses any other tree). Settings (`# --- WP-09 ---`): `EVAL_RUNTIME` (`docker`), `EVAL_TESTER_IMAGE` (`llmlab-v3-wt/eval-tester:dev`), `EVAL_ADHERENCE_IMAGE` (`llmlab-v3-wt/adherence-checker:dev`), `EVAL_APP_MEM_LIMIT` (`1g`), `EVAL_APP_CPUS` (1.0).
- **Images** built locally (`images/eval-tester`, `images/adherence-checker` with syft 1.18.1) and pinned in `data/eval_suites/eval-v2.yaml` (image ids `sha256:235f2a80…` and `sha256:e46cecfc…`; a new suite content hash, so a new suite version on re-seed). The docker runtime refuses an image whose id/RepoDigest differs (`image_digest_mismatch`).
- **API (9.9)** operation ids: `eval_suite_checks` (`GET /eval/suites/{ref}/checks/?task=&auth_mode=&stack=` → `{suite, task, auth_mode, stack, plan, n_checks, n_probes, disabled_probes}`), `eval_baselines`, `eval_probe_catalog`, `eval_conformance`, `eval_retest`.
- **Commands**: `oracle_validate [--task] [--stack] [--suite] [--runs 3] [--kit] [--kinds] [--dry-run]`, `conformance_kit [--stack] [--suite] [--status]`.
- **Tester rules** (`TESTER_RULES_VERSION=1`): trailing-slash redirect followed (any method), 400/422 interchangeable, 413 from framework or app, 405 vs 404 never checked; webhook routes (endpoint tag `webhook`) are signed with `WEBHOOK_SECRET` except the main request of an `auth`-category check; an anonymous client starts every check with an empty cookie jar; `{user_a_username}`, `{user_b_username}`, `{admin_username}`, `{admin_password}`, `{rand}`, `{oversize}` placeholders; a body field `username` outside `/api/auth/` names `user_b`. Deviation: the tester writes a raw result (checks, probes, versions, observed auth) and `suite.py` builds the `EvalResult` (metrics, flaky list, baseline); `plaintext_password_db` is read by the suite from the data dir.
- **Not done (honest list):** the postgres sidecar (postgres stacks end `infra_error:postgres_sidecar_unavailable`, never `start_fail`); the docker runtime path is unit-untested here (no docker in the test container): its first real run is a wave-3 E2E item; probe mutants exist only for the stdlib reference app (real-stack mutants with the wave-3 references); the conformance kit has no reference trees yet, so `coverage()` reports every framework `False` (confirmatory app studies stay blocked by `conformance_missing`, now for a measured reason instead of "status unavailable"); registry existence lookups (`PackageRecord.exists`, `hallucinated`) are not done (left `None`/empty, never "exists").

## WP-05 decisions (wave 3: phase-B starters; published interfaces), 2026-11-03

- **Profiles (11 new, `data/stacks/`):** `go-{react,vue,svelte,none}` (framework `go-stdlib`, runtime `go1.25`, 2g / 1 CPU), `spring-boot-{react,vue,svelte,none}` (Spring Boot 4.1.2, `temurin21`, 3g / 2 CPUs), `{fastapi,django,express}-react-postgres` (`database: postgres`, 3g / 1 CPU). Each has its fragment `stack-<slug>@1` (`data/prompts/`). `spring-boot-react-postgres` ("if time allows") and the optional `full` level are not shipped; `find_starter(profile, "full")` stays `None` (`starter_missing`).
- **Starters (22 new, 55 in all):** bare + minimal for every new profile. `tests/wp05_build/starter_hashes.json` pins all 55 tree hashes (the 33 phase-A hashes are unchanged). `compose.yaml` keeps `phase: B` as provenance; **`build.starters.ACTIVE_PHASES = ("A", "B")`** is what `seed_starters`, `build_starters --check/--lock` use, and a phase-B composition failure is now an `error`, never a skip.
- **Fragment rule (published):** `fragments_for(profile, level)` picks `backend/<framework>/<level>-<database>/` from `minimal` up for a non-SQLite profile (no fallback to the SQLite fragment: `StarterCompositionError`); bare postgres starters share the SQLite bare fragment and their README names `DATABASE_URL`.
- **Run contract for `database: postgres` (published):** the app gets `DATABASE_URL=postgresql://<user>:<password>@<host>:5432/<db>`; `/api/health` does not touch the database; Django's minimal starter runs `migrate` at start (needs the sidecar). Sidecar image: **`build.dockerfiles.sidecar_images()["postgres"]`** (`base_images.yaml` `sidecars:`; `postgres:18-trixie@sha256:3a4264520e12…`).
- **Locks (`build.locks`, published):** Go = `go mod tidy` → `go.sum` (the container gets the whole module directory minus `*.jinja2`; fails when `go.mod` is not tidy; index gains `inputs_sha`); Maven = `dependency:resolve` + `dependency:list` → `dependency-list.txt` (a record; Maven never reads it). `generate_locks(..., phases=ALL_PHASES)` is the default. `Ecosystem.enforced` / `origin_bound`: **`check_tree_locks` now also reports Go** (`lock_covers(..., ecosystem="go")`: `/go.mod` sum of every require + module sum of every direct one; `replace` → not covered; a starter `go.sum` is judged by coverage only); Maven is never reported (cannot fail a build).
- **Smoke (host daemon, 2026-11-03):** all 11 phase-B minimal starters built and answered `/api/health`, `/` and an SPA client route (postgres ones with the pinned sidecar). `test_starter_docker.py` now uses an internal network + a probe container (Go/JRE runtime images have no HTTP client) and `LLMLAB_STARTER_ONLY`.

`library/seeding.py` (`_seed_one`, `lint_seed_data`) still treats `entry.phase != "A"` as a wave-3 seam: a phase-B starter whose composition fails is reported `deferred`, not an error. Phase B now has profiles, fragments and locks, so please treat every phase in `backend.generation.build.starters.ACTIVE_PHASES` like `A` (a stack that is not seeded → `skipped`, a composition failure → `lint_starter_file_missing` / error). With the real `data/` tree `seed_library` now creates 11 more stack profiles, 11 more `stack-*` fragments and 22 more starters (`wp03_library` and `wp04_content` stay green: 738 passed). Your test fixtures with a synthetic `go-react` phase-B row may keep using `phase: B` as a label.

## WP-05 → WP-09: postgres sidecar, Go platform locks, adherence for go/java

1. Postgres stacks: replace `postgres_sidecar_unavailable` with a sidecar from `build.dockerfiles.sidecar_images()["postgres"].pinned` on the app's internal network, env `POSTGRES_USER/PASSWORD/DB`, wait for `pg_isready -h 127.0.0.1` (TCP: the image's init server listens on the socket only), then start the app with `DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:pass@<alias>:5432/<db>` (contract above; the smoke test in `tests/wp05_build/test_starter_docker.py::_start_postgres` does exactly this).
2. `check_tree_locks` now returns `ecosystem="go"` rows. The platform Go lock is `go mod download all` run in `dockerfiles.base_images()["go"].pinned` with `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` on `go.mod` (+ the existing `go.sum`), written to `<dir>/go.sum`; it never edits `go.mod` (verified: it completes an empty `go.sum` and the build passes). Until that exists, an incomplete `go.sum` + build failure lands in `infra_error:lock_incomplete` (your rule), which is the conservative side.
3. Adherence: the go/spring profiles declare `backend_markers` `net/http` / `spring-boot-starter-webmvc` and `db_markers` `modernc.org/sqlite` / `sqlite-jdbc`; the checker (`images/adherence-checker/checker.py`) has no Go (`go.mod`) or Maven (`pom.xml`) dependency parsing yet, so these stacks would be "not measured" there.
→ done (items 2 and 3) by WP-09, pre-deploy follow-up: see "WP-09 pre-deploy follow-up" at the end of this file.
4. Conformance-kit references for `go-stdlib` / `spring-boot` follow the same Dockerfile conventions (templates `go-stdlib.Dockerfile.jinja2`, `spring-boot.Dockerfile.jinja2`).

## WP-05 → WP-07 / WP-08: toolchains for the phase-B stacks in the agent sandbox

Go and Spring Boot cells need `go` 1.25 (with `GOTOOLCHAIN=local`) and JDK 21 + Maven in the agent sandbox for a model to run `go mod tidy` / `mvn package` (the depcache env already carries `GOPROXY` and the Maven `settings.xml`). Spec 11.4: the sandbox base uses the same Debian family as `golang:1.25-trixie` / `maven:3.9-eclipse-temurin-21` (digests in `base_images.yaml`).

## WP-05 → lead / user: Go 1.25 is out of support (decision)

Spec 11.4 fixes Go 1.25, but Go 1.27 is current and 1.25 left support in Aug 2026. `go mod tidy` resolves the newest module versions, and some already need Go 1.26 (`modernc.org/sqlite` ≥ v1.60: "requires go >= 1.26.0 (running go 1.25.14; GOTOOLCHAIN=local)"), so a bare go cell whose model writes no `go.mod` and imports such a module fails its build for an environment reason. The minimal starter pins the compatible `modernc.org/sqlite` v1.59.0 and passes. Options: (a) keep 1.25 (spec as written; document the risk in the brief), (b) move the go profiles, base image and lock image to `golang:1.27-trixie` (new digests, new starter versions, `runtime: go1.27`, fragment text). WP-05 can apply (b) in one change once decided.

→ done (pf07): lead decision (2) = keep the newest Go verified in wave 3, i.e. option (a): `golang:1.25-trixie@sha256:2c4c60ef…` (go1.25.14) everywhere; no fragment change (the brief already says Go 1.25 and the sandbox runs the same toolchain, so a too-new module fails visibly inside the run). Details in the pf07 entry.

## WP-06 wave 3: gateway translators (translate mode for local endpoints and Gemini), 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/images/llm-gateway/{gateway.py,protocols/{common,anthropic_messages,openai_responses,gemini_generate,translation (new)}.py}`, `backend/generation/images/llm-gateway-local/local.py`, `config/settings/components/generation.py` (WP-06 block), `docs/GATEWAY.md`, tests `backend/generation/tests/wp06_gateway/{test_translators (new),test_translate_http (new),test_policy,test_gateway_http,test_asks,support}.py` + fixtures `fixtures/translate/{chat_answers,gemini_cli_session}.json`. Images rebuilt locally: `llmlab-v3-wt/llm-gateway:dev`, `llmlab-v3-wt/llm-gateway-local:dev`. No migrations, no new deps (stdlib only).

**Published interface (WP-07, WP-08, WP-10, WP-11 read this):**
- `protocol_mode="translate"` now runs for `anthropic_messages`, `openai_responses` (OpenRouter **and** `local_proxy` upstreams) and `gemini_generate` (translate only). `openai_chat` stays `native`; `gemini_generate` in `passthrough`/`native` and `openai_chat` in `translate` exit 78 (`config_invalid`). The image's list is `protocols.common.TRANSLATORS`.
- **`settings.GATEWAY_TRANSLATORS = ["anthropic_messages", "openai_responses", "gemini_generate"]`** (enabled: all three pass the golden fixtures). With it `adapter.protocol_mode(endpoint_kind="local_proxy", translators=...)` returns `translate` for Claude Code / Codex, and Gemini CLI resolves to `translate` everywhere.
- Translate path: native body → `front.to_chat(body, **front.request_options(path, query))` → the **chat** policy (`openai_chat.apply_policy_native`, the same as a native chat harness) → `{upstream.base_url}/chat/completions` → `front.from_chat` / `front.StreamTranslator`. The prompt estimate, reservation, ledger, request classes (from the native headers, e.g. `x-claude-code-request-class`), retries, keepalives and the whole call record are the canonical chat ones, so the same conversation fills `state.json` identically and exhausts at the same call on every front (pinned by `test_translate_http.py::test_the_same_conversation_fills_the_ledger_identically_on_every_front`). Record: `front_protocol` = the harness's front, `protocol_mode = "translate"`, `request` = the chat body sent upstream, `native_request_sha` = `identity.H` of the native body, `requested_model` = native `model` (Gemini: the model in the URL).
- **`translation_dropped`** is now filled (`{field: count}`, only non-zero entries, sorted): `request.<key>` (unknown native keys), `tools.<type>` (hosted/server tools), `tool_result.is_error`, `tools.custom.format` (custom-tool grammar), `functionResponse.unmatched`, `request.safetySettings`, `generationConfig.<key>`, `tool_call.invalid_arguments`, `cache_control` (markers on tools/tool calls; omitted when `prompt_cache` is `off`/`auto`). `agentic.validity` already turns any non-zero count into `translation_lossy`. The recorded sessions of the pinned Claude Code, Codex and Gemini CLI binaries translate with `{}`.
- **Reasoning continuity:** upstream `reasoning_details` travel in the native replayable field as `llmlab1.<base64url(JSON)>` tokens (Anthropic `thinking.signature` / `redacted_thinking.data`, Responses `reasoning.encrypted_content`, Gemini `thoughtSignature` on the first `functionCall` part) and come back unchanged in the next chat request; native signatures are forwarded as details of format `anthropic-claude-v1` / `openai-responses-v1` / `google-gemini-v1`. Plain `reasoning` strings (local models) replay as `reasoning`. The local overlay turns `reasoning_details` into `reasoning` text.
- Gemini front: `POST /v1beta/models/{m}:generateContent | :streamGenerateContent[?alt=sse]` (SSE, or a JSON array without `alt=sse`), local `:countTokens` (`{"totalTokens", "estimate": true}`), `GET /v1beta/models`; keepalive `\r\n`; in-stream errors are a bare `{"error": {code, message, status}}` (the Gemini SDK raises it). Budget refusal: 400 `INVALID_ARGUMENT` with the budget text; `call.max_input_tokens` refusal worded "The input token count (N) exceeds the maximum number of tokens allowed (M)."
- Translator module contract (pure, stdlib): `to_chat(native, **options) -> (chat, dropped)` (raises `ValueError`/`translation.TranslationError` → native 400 `unsupported_request`), `from_chat(chat_completion, native=None, *, dropped=None, **options)`, `StreamTranslator(native=None, *, dropped=None, **options)` (`content_type`, `feed`, `finish(failure=None)`, `fail(status, payload)`), `stream_from_chat(chat_sse_iter, native=None, **options)` (a `None` item yields the native keepalive), `upstream_error(status, message, code)`. `TranslatorUnavailableError` classes remain (never raised).

**Verified offline (no paid calls):** `tests/wp06_gateway` 252 passed (116 new pure-translator cases incl. reasoning round trip for every front × {Anthropic, OpenAI, open-weights, Google, local} × {JSON, stream}; 30+ HTTP cases with the scripted upstream). Real-binary smoke: Claude Code 2.1.316, Codex 0.166.0 and Gemini CLI 0.66.0 images each completed the WP-07 smoke task (`hello.txt`) through the rebuilt gateway image in translate mode against the WP-07 scripted chat upstream on an `--internal` network: 2 calls each, all `main`, `translation_dropped` `{}`. Not verified: a real model behind the translators (fixtures use OpenRouter-shaped synthetic answers), and LiteLLM / OpenRouter-native oracle comparisons (spec 5.4 rule 3; not run, no paid calls).

## WP-07 → WP-06: Gemini translator findings from the real CLI

1. **Synthetic signature forwarded:** Gemini CLI puts `thoughtSignature: "skip_thought_signature_validator"` (its `SYNTHETIC_THOUGHT_SIGNATURE`, added in `historyHardening` to the first `functionCall` of a turn that had none) on replayed calls. The translator turns it into a `reasoning.encrypted` detail `{data: "skip_thought_signature_validator", format: "google-gemini-v1"}` and sends it upstream to **any** model (seen in `fixtures/gemini-cli-0.66.0-gateway/calls.jsonl`, call 2, DeepSeek upstream). Please treat that literal as "no signature" (at least for non-Google upstream models) and count it in `translation_dropped` (e.g. `thoughtSignature.synthetic`).
2. **`requested_model` is `None` for vendor-prefixed slugs:** `_MODEL_IN_PATH = /models/([^/:]+):` stops at the slash of `/v1beta/models/deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash:streamGenerateContent`. The adapter presents `ctx.model_name` (OpenRouter ids always carry a vendor prefix), so please match `/models/(.+?):(generateContent|streamGenerateContent|countTokens)$`.
3. Fixtures you may use: `tests/wp07_harnesses/fixtures/gateway-translate/gemini-cli.json` (native requests from the pinned CLI with the current settings, the canonical chat SSE upstream answers, and `native_reference`: the Gemini SSE the CLI accepted). It is outside `fixtures/gateway/` on purpose, because `test_passthrough_sessions.py` globs that directory.

## WP-07 → lead: keep captured prompts and smoke fixtures byte-exact

`.pre-commit-config.yaml` (`trailing-whitespace`, `end-of-file-fixer`) rewrote `harnesses/captured/codex-0.166.0.md` at the wave-2 commit, so the pinned prompt no longer matched what Codex was recorded receiving. Please add `^backend/generation/harnesses/captured/` and `^backend/generation/tests/wp07_harnesses/fixtures/` to those two hooks' `exclude` (both directories hold verbatim captures). Until then: the wave-3 files are already newline-terminated with no trailing whitespace (the Gemini capture carries one final newline more than the CLI's own default, recorded in the fixture).

## WP-07 → lead (decision): opencode reaches for registry.npmjs.org

OpenCode 1.18.35, the default harness, opens one `CONNECT registry.npmjs.org:443` about one second before its first model call, with `autoupdate: false` and `OPENCODE_DISABLE_{AUTOUPDATE,DEFAULT_PLUGINS,MODELS_FETCH,LSP_DOWNLOAD,...}` set. The run completes without it (the wave-2 smoke runs had no egress at all). Under `network_policy=packages` the depcache proxy may let it through, so a runtime npm fetch could change the harness. WP-07 has not found which switch stops it. Options: (a) block `registry.npmjs.org` for the harness process only, which the network layer cannot express today; (b) keep opencode enabled and record the attempt as a known fact; (c) add it to opencode's `open_questions`, which disables the default harness until it is resolved. WP-07 recommends (b) plus a follow-up investigation, and has not changed opencode.

→ done (pf07): lead decision (3), pre-baked (details in the pf07 entry). Cause found in the bundle: OpenCode's config loader runs its bundled npm (arborist) `install` of `@opencode-ai/plugin@<version>` in every config directory (the global one is `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/opencode`) that has no `node_modules` with a `package-lock.json` covering its `package.json`.

## WP-07 → WP-08: sandbox notes for the new images

- `agent-base` now has Go/JDK/Maven on `PATH` (`GOTOOLCHAIN=local`, `GOPATH=/run/home/go`, `JAVA_HOME`). Your `MAVEN_OPTS=-Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/m2` and the depcache env still apply.
- Gemini CLI reads its system settings from the image's `/etc/gemini-cli/settings.json`. It skips that file unless `/` and `/etc/gemini-cli` stay owned by root. `put_archive("/", make_tar(...))` must therefore never carry an entry for `.` or `etc` (today it carries only the parent directories of rendered paths: `run`, `run/harness`, `run/home/...`). The smoke runner now copies its stage with `tar --no-overwrite-dir` for the same reason.

## WP-09 wave 3: conformance kit and oracle validation on real starters, 2026-11-03

Files: `backend/generation/evaluation/{acceptance,runtime,suite}.py`, `evaluation/conformance/probe_validation.json`, `images/eval-tester/{tester.py,Dockerfile}`, `images/adherence-checker/Dockerfile` (comment), `data/eval_suites/eval-v2.yaml` (digests only, as agreed in "WP-04 → WP-09: eval-v2 image digests"), `tests/wp09_eval/{test_tester,test_suite}.py`, `tests/wp09_eval/snapshots/*` (9), `docs/EVALUATION.md`.

**What ran (host docker, private network/postgres, no LLM calls).** The docker runtime's first real E2E: lock → build (BuildKit, `sample_builds`) → start → tester pre/restart/post → probes → adherence container.
- Starter baselines (`crud_todo_list`, contract_bearer): phase-A minimal starters of all four frameworks (`-none` plus one frontend stack each) measure 0.0; bare starters end `start_fail` (no app, correct). Phase B: `go-none-minimal`, `spring-boot-none-minimal`, `{fastapi,django,express}-react-postgres-minimal` build, start and measure 0.0.
- `conformance_kit` (the real command, references from `~/.cache/llmlab-v3-work/w3g9/refs/`): **flask, fastapi, django, express pass all three kit tasks** (`agent_pass_rate == 1.0`, every probe `not_exploited`), with the final pinned images. go and spring-boot have no references, so `coverage()` reports them not covered.
- `oracle_validate --runs 3`: 12/12 references pass 3/3, no flaky checks. The bare-starter leg is vacuous for these stacks (bare starters do not start → `starter_pass_rate` `None`, which `validate_oracle` accepts as "< 0.5"); the minimal starters' measured 0.0 above is the informative number.
- Probe kinds: 17/17 mutant pairs validated locally (`probe_validation.json` refreshed) **and** on the docker runtime (probe-mutant image + eval-tester on an internal network, incl. `ssrf_mock` via `mock.internal` and `plaintext_password_db` via `docker cp`).

**Defects found and fixed (evaluation/**, tester):**
1. **SPA catch-all false pass.** The Django minimal starter answers every request with `index.html` (200), so `auth.login.ok` passed (django-svelte-minimal scored 0.04). The compiler now requires the auth mode's credential on `<login>.ok`: a non-empty string at `token_json_path` (bearer) or a `Set-Cookie` header (cookie). The tester's schema subset gained `allOf`. Compiler snapshots regenerated; `COMPILER_VERSION` stays 1 (pre-release: no stored evaluation exists outside test DBs). Test `test_spa_catch_all_never_passes_the_login_check`.
2. **Early 413 became a broken pipe.** gunicorn/Flask `MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH` answers 413 from the headers and closes; the tester kept writing the 12 MiB body and recorded `error`. Bodies over 1 MiB are now streamed in 64 KiB chunks and sending stops as soon as the app answers (tolerance 4 of `TESTER_RULES_VERSION=1`, implementation fix, no rule change). Test `test_an_early_413_is_read_not_a_broken_pipe`.
3. **`{user_b_username}` named an account that did not exist yet.** `members.add.ok` (team_roles) ran as `user_a` before `user_b` was ever registered, so every correct app answered 404. A check that uses `{user_a_username}`/`{user_b_username}` now registers and logs in that actor first (skipped `auth:<actor>` if it cannot). Test `test_a_username_placeholder_registers_that_actor_first`.
4. **Postgres sidecar implemented** (was `infra_error:postgres_sidecar_unavailable` for every postgres stack). `DockerEvalRuntime.start_database("postgres")` runs `build.dockerfiles.sidecar_images()["postgres"]` (WP-05, pinned) as alias `db` with a fresh password and gives the app `DATABASE_URL`; failure or a runtime without a sidecar stays `infra_error:postgres_sidecar_unavailable`, never `start_fail`. Tests `test_postgres_profile_*`.
5. **Image pins were not reproducible.** On the host daemon (containerd snapshotter) the image id is the manifest-list digest, and BuildKit's default provenance attestation carries a timestamp, so every rebuild of unchanged sources produced a new id (the wave-2 pins `235f2a80…`/`e46cecfc…` could not be rebuilt). Images are now built with `--provenance=false --sbom=false` (deterministic) and re-pinned: tester `sha256:e516a965…`, adherence-checker `sha256:39a54949…` (a new eval-v2 version on re-seed).
6. Eval container/network names take `settings.EVAL_NAME_PREFIX` (default `llmlab-eval`), so local runs can use a private prefix.

## WP-01 → WP-10: freeze the local serving settings in `ManifestEndpoint`

`contracts.manifest.ManifestEndpoint` now has `num_ctx`, `keep_alive`, `api_flavor`, `capabilities` (all optional). In `study/plan.py:_manifest_model`, for a local endpoint, please fill them from `backend.inference.snapshot.endpoint_section(model)` (`num_ctx` = `effective_num_ctx`, `keep_alive` = `endpoint.keep_alive or None`, `api_flavor`, `capabilities`), and leave them `None` for OpenRouter. They are recorded, not identity inputs, so no golden value changes. The proxy then uses the frozen values instead of the live endpoint row.

## WP-06 pre-deploy follow-ups (Gemini paths and placeholder signatures, failed turns, seed on translated fronts), 2026-11-03

Files: `images/llm-gateway/gateway.py`, `images/llm-gateway/protocols/{translation,anthropic_messages,openai_responses,gemini_generate}.py`, `budget/gateway_config.py` (lead decision 1; WP-11's file, one function and the `forward_seed` line), `tests/wp06_gateway/{test_wave3_followups.py (new),test_translate_http.py}`, `tests/wp11_budget/test_gateway_config.py` (one test appended), `docs/GATEWAY.md`. No paid calls; the gateway image was not rebuilt (tests run the image code in-process; the next image build picks the files up).

- **WP-07 → WP-06 (2) `requested_model` for vendor-prefixed slugs → done:** `gemini_generate._MODEL_IN_PATH = /models/(.+?):(?:generateContent|streamGenerateContent|countTokens)$` (no more `":"` appended to the path). `deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash`, `%2F`-encoded slashes and `:free` suffixes are kept; the call record's `requested_model` names the full slug (HTTP test).
- **WP-07 → WP-06 (1) synthetic `thoughtSignature` → done:** `gemini_generate.SYNTHETIC_SIGNATURES = {"skip_thought_signature_validator", "context_engineering_is_the_way_to_go"}`. In a replayed model turn such a signature is "no signature" unless the requested model is a Google one (`gemini_generate.is_google_model`: `google/...` or a bare `gemini-...`; there it still goes upstream as a `google-gemini-v1` detail, since it means "skip validation" to Google): dropped and counted as `translation_dropped["thoughtSignature.synthetic"]` (so `agentic.validity` notes `translation_lossy`). Real signatures and our `llmlab1.` tokens are untouched. The WP-07 fixture `gateway-translate/gemini-cli.json` (DeepSeek) now translates with `{"thoughtSignature.synthetic": 1}`; the WP-06 Gemini CLI session (model `gemini-2.5-pro`) still with `{}`.
- **Non-streamed `finish_reason=error` → done, every front:** `translation.completion_failure(payload)` reads a failed turn (top-level `error`, OpenRouter's `choices[0].error`, or `finish_reason == "error"`, message `FINISHED_WITH_ERROR` plus `native_finish_reason`). `from_chat` turns it into the native error: Anthropic `{"type": "error", "error": {"type": "api_error", ...}}`, Gemini `{"error": {code, message, status}}`, Responses a `failed` Response object with the partial output and `error: {code, message}` (as the stream's `response.failed`; an error-only payload keeps the OpenAI error shape). The gateway sends it with **HTTP 502** (before: a 200 that looked like an answer, Gemini `finishReason: OTHER`, Responses `status: failed` with `error: null`). The call record is unchanged (upstream status, `finish_reason: "error"`). Streams: choice-level errors now become the error event (and `completion_sse` carries them when a JSON answer is re-encoded as a stream); a stream ending with `finish_reason=error` and no error object says `FINISHED_WITH_ERROR` instead of "stream ended before the turn finished" (`translation.turn_failure`, shared by the three `StreamTranslator.finish`).
- **Lead decision (1) `forward_seed` on translated Anthropic/Responses fronts → done (gateway):** `budget.gateway_config.seed_carried(front, mode)` is false only for `anthropic_messages`/`openai_responses` outside `translate`; `forward_seed = manifest.sampling.seed_forwarded and seed_carried(front, mode)`. A translated run is canonical chat, so the chat policy puts `seed` in the upstream body (HTTP test: `seed` reaches the upstream and the record's `request`); passthrough/native bodies still never carry one (`seed_not_applicable` in the policy diff). Pairing stays `paired_crn` for translated runs whose model accepts a seed. Whether the seed was honoured is recorded in the manifest's `sampling.seed_forwarded`, which needs the builder change below (WP-10).
- **Tests:** `.wt-test.sh pf06`: `tests/wp06_gateway` + `tests/wp11_budget` 562 passed (+ wp07: 750 passed / 16 skipped together; the one failure, `test_adapters.py::test_render_matches_the_smoke_run_snapshot[opencode]`, is WP-07's concurrent opencode change, not the gateway). ruff check/format clean on touched files.

### WP-06 → WP-10: make `manifest.sampling.seed_forwarded` truthful per front and mode

`study/plan.py:_sampling` sets `seed_forwarded = forward_seed and _supports_seed(model)` without looking at the harness's front. Today a passthrough Claude Code or Codex run (`anthropic_messages`/`openai_responses`, `protocol_mode` `passthrough`) on a seed-capable OpenRouter model gets `seed_forwarded: true` although the gateway sends no seed, so `study/results.py` and `patterns/prevalence.py` pair it as `paired_crn`. Please compute it as `bool(content.forward_seed and _supports_seed(model) and gateway_config.seed_carried(env.gateway.front_protocol, env.gateway.protocol_mode))` (the gateway block is known in `_manifest`, from `env.gateway`), so the manifest records whether the seed is honoured; translated runs keep `true`. This changes recorded manifests (not identity keys, unless `seed_forwarded` is hashed in your golden plan: then regenerate it with a protocol line).

→ done (verified by WP-06, pf06, 2026-11-03): `study/plan.py:_sampling(condition, gateway)` (called from `_manifest` with `env.gateway`) sets `seed_forwarded = bool(content.forward_seed and _supports_seed(model) and (gateway is None or gateway_config.seed_carried(gateway.front_protocol, gateway.protocol_mode)))`, which is lead decision (1) for the manifest: translated Anthropic/Responses runs keep `true` (`paired_crn`), native/passthrough ones record `false` (`independent_repeats` in `study/results.py` / `patterns/prevalence.py`). `gateway is None` only for single-shot slot cells (seed applied in-process by `slot/executors/single_shot.py`) and for blocked agentic cells that never run. `seed_forwarded` is recorded, not hashed: `test_identity_golden.py` passes unchanged, so no golden regeneration or identity-changelog line is due. Tests: `.wt-test.sh pf06 -- pytest backend/generation/tests/wp10_study` 116 passed (incl. `test_wave3_followups.py::test_seed_forwarded_needs_a_front_that_carries_the_seed`, 5 front/mode cases).
